import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import events from '../events.json';
import activities from '../activities.json';
import parties from '../parties.json';
import officeItems from '../office_items.json';
import type { GameEvent, Activity, Party } from '../../engine/types';

// --- 1. WALIDACJA EVENTÓW ---
describe('events.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  const eventList = events as GameEvent[];

  it('każdy event ma wymagane pola i unikalne id', () => {
    const ids = eventList.map(e => e.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);

    for (const ev of eventList) {
      expect(ev.id, 'Event bez id').toBeTruthy();
      expect(ev.title, `${ev.id}: brak title`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(ev.description, `${ev.id}: brak description`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(typeof ev.weight, `${ev.id}: weight musi być liczbą`).toBe('number');
      expect(ev.conditions, `${ev.id}: brak conditions`).toBeDefined();
      expect(ev.choices, `${ev.id}: brak choices`).toBeDefined();
    }
  });

  it('każdy event ma co najmniej jedną opcję', () => {
    for (const ev of eventList) {
      expect(ev.choices.length, `${ev.id}: 0 opcji`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it('każda opcja ma label i (effects lub gamble lub outcomes)', () => {
    for (const ev of eventList) {
      for (const c of ev.choices) {
        expect(c.label, `${ev.id}/${c.id}: brak label`).toBeTruthy();
        const hasEffect = c.effects || c.gamble || c.outcomes;
        expect(hasEffect, `${ev.id}/${c.id}: brak efektu`).toBeTruthy();
      }
    }
  });
});

// --- 2. WALIDACJA AKTYWNOŚCI (Daily Actions) ---
describe('activities.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  const activityList = activities as Activity[];

  it('każda aktywność ma wymagane pola', () => {
    for (const a of activityList) {
      expect(a.id, 'Aktywność bez id').toBeTruthy();
      expect(a.label, `${a.id}: brak label`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(a.description, `${a.id}: brak description`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(typeof a.weight, `${a.id}: weight musi być liczbą`).toBe('number');
      expect(a.conditions, `${a.id}: brak conditions`).toBeDefined();
      expect(a.effects, `${a.id}: brak effects`).toBeDefined();
    }
  });

  it('id aktywności są unikalne', () => {
    const ids = activityList.map(a => a.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
  });
});

// --- 3. WALIDACJA PARTII ---
describe('parties.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  const partyList = parties as Party[];

  it('każda partia ma wymagane pola i unikalne id', () => {
    const ids = partyList.map(p => p.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);

    for (const p of partyList) {
      expect(p.id).toBeTruthy();
      expect(p.name, `${p.id}: brak name`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(p.ideology, `${p.id}: brak ideology`).toBeTruthy();
      expect(p.sizeSchedule, `${p.id}: brak sizeSchedule`).toBeDefined();
      expect(Array.isArray(p.sizeSchedule), `${p.id}: sizeSchedule nie jest tablicą`).toBe(true);
      expect(typeof p.activeFrom).toBe('number');
      expect(typeof p.activeTo).toBe('number');
    }
  });

  it('sizeSchedule ma poprawne przedziały czasowe', () => {
    for (const p of partyList) {
      for (const sched of p.sizeSchedule) {
        expect(sched.from, `${p.id}: sizeSchedule from > to`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(sched.to);
        expect(sched.size, `${p.id}: brak size w sizeSchedule`).toBeTruthy();
      }
    }
  });
});

// --- 4. WALIDACJA PRZEDMIOTÓW BIUROWYCH ---
describe('office_items.json - walidacja struktury', () => {
  it('każdy przedmiot ma unikalne id, nazwę, opis i dodatnią cenę', () => {
    const ids = officeItems.map(i => i.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);

    for (const item of officeItems) {
      expect(item.id).toBeTruthy();
      expect(item.name).toBeTruthy();
      expect(item.description).toBeTruthy();
      expect(item.price).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from '../initialState';
import {
  applyHire,
  applyLoan,
  applyChangeActivity,
  applyJoinParty,
  applyLeaveParty,
  applyEmergencyLoan,
  undoLastAction,
} from '../actions';
import { nextTurn } from '../turn';
import { WORKER_CONFIG, MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE } from '../constants';
import type { GameState } from '../types';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../economy';

function makeState(): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed: 'test',
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity: 'handel',
    companyName: 'Test',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
  });
}

describe('applyHire', () => {
  it('dodaje nowych pracowników do kolejki i wciela ich w kolejnej turze', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const afterHire = applyHire(state, 'zlecenie', 3);

    expect(afterHire.company.employees.zlecenie).toBe(0);
    expect(afterHire.pendingHires?.reduce((s, h) => s + h.count, 0)).toBe(3);

    const afterTurn = nextTurn(afterHire);
    expect(afterTurn.company.employees.zlecenie).toBe(3);
    expect(afterTurn.pendingHires ?? []).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('nie pozwala zejść poniżej zera', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = applyHire(state, 'zlecenie', -5);
    expect(result.company.employees.zlecenie).toBe(0);
  });

  it('przy zwolnieniu UOP zabiera odprawę z kasy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 2;
    const cashBefore = state.company.finances.stanKonta;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'uop', -2);

    const expectedSeverance = scaleByInflation(2 * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.severance, state);
    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(cashBefore - expectedSeverance);
  });

  it('nie zwalnia UOP gdy nie stać na odprawę', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 5;
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = 1000;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'uop', -5);

    expect(result.company.employees.uop).toBe(5);
    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(1000);
  });

  it('zwolnienie bez umowy obniża opinię pracowników', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.bezUmowy = 3;
    const repBefore = state.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'bezUmowy', -2);

    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy).toBeLessThan(repBefore);
  });

  it('zwolnienie UOP nie obniża opinii pracowników (bo jest odprawa)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 2;
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = 100_000;
    const repBefore = state.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy;

    const result = applyHire(state, 'uop', -1);

    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy).toBe(repBefore);
  });
});

describe('undoLastAction', () => {
  it('cofa ostatni ruch wykonany w bieżącej turze', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const before = state.company.finances.stanKonta;

    const afterLoan = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);
    expect(afterLoan.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(before + 20_000);

    const undone = undoLastAction(afterLoan);
    expect(undone.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(before);
    expect(undone.company.loans).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe('applyLoan', () => {
  it('zwiększa stan konta o kwotę kredytu', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const before = state.company.finances.stanKonta;

    const result = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);

    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(before + 20_000);
  });

  it('dodaje kredyt do listy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);
    expect(result.company.loans).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('kredyt z szarej strefy ma wyższe odsetki niż bankowy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const bank = applyLoan(state, 'bank', 20_000);
    const strefa = applyLoan(state, 'szara_strefa', 20_000);

    expect(strefa.company.loans[0].interestRate).toBeGreaterThan(
      bank.company.loans[0].interestRate
    );
  });
});

describe('applyChangeActivity', () => {
  it('zmienia działalność firmy', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.turn = 10;
    const result = applyChangeActivity(state, 'it');
    expect(result.company.activity).toBe('it');
  });

  it('resetuje opinię mafii do baseline nowej branży', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.turn = 10;
    const result = applyChangeActivity(state, 'windykacja');

    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaMafia).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaMafia).toBeLessThan(100);
  });

  it('nie pozwala zmienić działalności w cooldownie', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.turn = 1;
    state.company.lastActivityChangeTurn = 0;
    const result = applyChangeActivity(state, 'it');
    expect(result.company.activity).toBe('handel');
  });
});

describe('applyJoinParty / applyLeaveParty', () => {
  it('dołączenie do partii ustawia currentPartyId', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1990;
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBe('sld');
  });

  it('dołączenie do partii zwiększa licznik przefarbowań', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1990;
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase.chaos).toBe(1);
  });

  it('nie pozwala przekroczyć limitu przefarbowań', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1990;
    state.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase.chaos = MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE;
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBeNull();
  });

  it('nie pozwala dołączyć do partii w 1989 roku', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.year = 1989;
    const result = applyJoinParty(state, 'sld');
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBeNull();
  });

  it('opuszczenie partii ustawia currentPartyId na null', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.ceo.currentPartyId = 'sld';
    const result = applyLeaveParty(state);
    expect(result.ceo.currentPartyId).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('applyEmergencyLoan', () => {
  it('dodaje 50 000 PLN do konta', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = -10000;
    const before = state.company.finances.stanKonta;
    const result = applyEmergencyLoan(state);
    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBe(before + scaleByInflation(50_000, state));
  });

  it('resetuje bankruptWarningTurns', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = -10000;
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = 2;
    const result = applyEmergencyLoan(state);
    expect(result.bankruptWarningTurns).toBe(0);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from '../initialState';
import { recomputeReputation } from '../reputation';
import {
  generateCompetitors,
  ensureBranchCompetitors,
  tickCompetitors,
  computeRanking,
  interactWithCompetitor,
  proposeAlliance,
  breakAlliance,
  declareWar,
  performWarAction,
  getWarActions,
  getAllianceTradeDiscount,
  isInPlayerBranch,
  SIZE_ORDER,
  SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR,
  COMPETITOR_SIZE_MIX,
  INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN,
  LEAGUE_TARGET_SIZE,
  MIN_SAME_BRANCH_COMPETITORS,
  MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES,
} from '../competition';
import type { GameState } from '../types';
import competitionData from '../../data/competition.json';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';

const SIZES = new Set<string>(SIZE_ORDER);
const VALID_CHARACTERS = new Set(['aggressive', 'neutral', 'peaceful']);

function newState(seed = 'test-seed', activity = 'handel_odziez'): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed,
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity,
    companyName: 'Testex',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
  });
}

/** Symuluje upływ czasu: turn++, kwartały, lata — bez reszty silnika. */
function advance(s: GameState, ticks: number) {
  for (let i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
    s.turn += 1;
    const total = s.year * 4 + (s.quarter - 1) + 1;
    s.year = Math.floor(total / 4);
    s.quarter = ((total % 4) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
    tickCompetitors(s, 0);
  }
}

describe('data/competition.json', () => {
  const categoryIds = new Set((branchesData as { id: string }[]).map((c) => c.id));
  const templates = competitionData as any[];

  it('ma unikalne id, wymagane pola, 3 motta i poprawny charakter', () => {
    const ids = templates.map((t) => t.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const t of templates) {
      expect(typeof t.name).toBe('string');
      expect(typeof t.description).toBe('string');
      expect(Array.isArray(t.mottos)).toBe(true);
      expect(t.mottos.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
      expect(VALID_CHARACTERS.has(t.character)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('kategorie, jeśli podane, istnieją w branches.json', () => {
    for (const t of templates) {
      if (t.categories) {
        for (const c of t.categories) {
          expect(c === '*' || categoryIds.has(c)).toBe(true);
        }
      }
    }
  });
});

describe('generateCompetitors', () => {
  it('jest deterministyczne dla tego samego seeda', () => {
    expect(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'abc')).toEqual(
      generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'abc')
    );
  });

  it('różne seedy dają różne statystyki', () => {
    const a = JSON.stringify(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'seed-a'));
    const b = JSON.stringify(generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'seed-b'));
    expect(a).not.toBe(b);
  });

  it('generuje ligę 20 unikalnych firm z min. 3 z branży gracza', () => {
    const list = generateCompetitors('produkcja', 1989, 'xyz');
    expect(list).toHaveLength(LEAGUE_TARGET_SIZE);
    expect(new Set(list.map((c) => c.id)).size).toBe(list.length);

    const sameBranch = list.filter(
      (c) => c.categories.includes('*') || c.categories.includes('produkcja')
    );
    expect(sameBranch.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_SAME_BRANCH_COMPETITORS);
  });

  it('rozmiary zgadzają się z COMPETITOR_SIZE_MIX dla ligi 20 firm', () => {
    const list = generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'mix');
    const expectedCount = (size: string) =>
      COMPETITOR_SIZE_MIX.filter((s) => s === size).length;
    const actualCount = (size: string) =>
      list.filter((c) => c.size === size).length;

    for (const size of SIZE_ORDER) {
      expect(actualCount(size)).toBe(expectedCount(size));
    }
  });

  it('wartość mieści się w przedziale rozmiaru, a statystyki w 0-100', () => {
    for (const c of generateCompetitors('handel', 1989, 'ranges')) {
      const idx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(c.size);
      expect(c.value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[c.size] || 1000);
      if (SIZE_ORDER[idx + 1]) {
        expect(c.value).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SIZE_VALUE_FLOOR[SIZE_ORDER[idx + 1]]);
      }
      for (const v of [c.reputation, c.condition, c.opinionOfPlayer]) {
        expect(v).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(v).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
      }
      expect(typeof c.motto).toBe('string');
      expect(c.motto.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(VALID_CHARACTERS.has(c.character)).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});

describe('ensureBranchCompetitors', () => {
  it('nie zmienia ligi, gdy są co najmniej 3 firmy z branży gracza', () => {
    const s = newState('already-migrated');
    const ids = s.competitors.map((c) => c.id);

    ensureBranchCompetitors(s);

    expect(s.competitors.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(ids);
  });

  it('uzupełnia brakujące firmy z aktywnej branży gracza', () => {
    const s = newState('branch-refill');
    s.competitors = s.competitors.slice(0, 4);

    ensureBranchCompetitors(s);

    const activeInBranch = s.competitors.filter(
      (c) => c.status === 'active' && isInPlayerBranch(s, c)
    );
    expect(activeInBranch.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_SAME_BRANCH_COMPETITORS);
  });
});

describe('tickCompetitors', () => {
  it('jest deterministyczne dla tego samego seeda', () => {
    const a = newState('same');
    const b = newState('same');
    advance(a, 40);
    advance(b, 40);
    expect(a.competitors).toEqual(b.competitors);
  });

  it('utrzymuje statystyki w granicach przez 160 tur', () => {
    const s = newState('bounds');
    advance(s, 160);
    for (const c of s.competitors) {
      for (const v of [c.reputation, c.condition, c.opinionOfPlayer]) {
        expect(v).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(v).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
      }
      expect(c.value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1000);
      expect(SIZES.has(c.size)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('nie resetuje ligi po zmianie branży i gwarantuje min. 3 aktywne firmy z nowej branży', () => {
    const s = newState('switch');
    const initialIds = new Set(s.competitors.map((c) => c.id));

    s.company.activity = 'gast_kebab';
    s.company.activities = ['gast_kebab'];
    advance(s, 1);

    expect(s.competitionCategory).toBe('uslugi');
    // Dotychczasowe firmy zostają w świecie gry
    for (const id of initialIds) {
      expect(s.competitors.some((c) => c.id === id)).toBe(true);
    }
    const activeInNewBranch = s.competitors.filter(
      (c) => c.status === 'active' && isInPlayerBranch(s, c)
    );
    expect(activeInNewBranch.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_SAME_BRANCH_COMPETITORS);
  });
});

describe('computeRanking', () => {
  it('sortuje malejąco, numeruje od 1 i zawiera gracza oraz aktywne firmy', () => {
    const s = newState('rank');
    advance(s, 20);
    const rows = computeRanking(s);
    const activeCount = s.competitors.filter((c) => c.status === 'active').length;

    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.isPlayer)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(activeCount + 1);
    rows.forEach((r, i) => expect(r.rank).toBe(i + 1));
    for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
      expect(rows[i - 1].score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(rows[i].score);
    }
  });
});

describe('interactWithCompetitor', () => {
  it('zużywa punkt interakcji i nie mutuje starego stanu', () => {
    const s = newState('interact');
    const id = s.competitors[0].id;
    const next = interactWithCompetitor(s, id, 'positive');
    expect(next.interactionPoints).toBe(s.interactionPoints - 1);
    expect(next).not.toBe(s);
    expect(s.interactionPoints).toBe(INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN);
  });

  it('bez punktów albo dla nieznanego id zwraca ten sam obiekt', () => {
    const s = newState('nopoints');
    s.interactionPoints = 0;
    expect(interactWithCompetitor(s, s.competitors[0].id, 'positive')).toBe(s);
    const s2 = newState('unknown');
    expect(interactWithCompetitor(s2, 'nie_ma_takiego', 'negative')).toBe(s2);
  });
});

describe('sojusze, cynk rynkowy i wojny', () => {
  it('respektuje twardy limit sojuszy, daje rabat i skaluje dokładność reputacją sojusznika', () => {
    let s = newState('alliances');
    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES + 1; i++) {
      s.competitors[i].opinionOfPlayer = 80;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES; i++) {
      s = proposeAlliance(s, s.competitors[i].id);
    }
    expect(s.competitors.filter((c) => c.isAlliedWithPlayer)).toHaveLength(MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES);
    expect(getAllianceTradeDiscount(s)).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    // Próba przekroczenia limitu nie dodaje kolejnego sojusznika
    const overLimit = proposeAlliance(s, s.competitors[MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES].id);
    expect(overLimit).toBe(s);

    // Zerwanie sojuszu zwalnia slot
    const afterBreak = breakAlliance(s, s.competitors[0].id);
    expect(afterBreak.competitors.filter((c) => c.isAlliedWithPlayer)).toHaveLength(
      MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES - 1
    );

    // Cynk rynkowy: wyższa reputacja sojusznika => wyższa dokładność przewidywań
    const lowRepState = newState('intel-test');
    lowRepState.competitors[0].isAlliedWithPlayer = true;
    lowRepState.competitors[0].reputation = 10;
    const highRepState = structuredClone(lowRepState);
    highRepState.competitors[0].reputation = 95;

    const lowAcc = recomputeReputation(lowRepState).dokladnoscPrzewidywan;
    const highAcc = recomputeReputation(highRepState).dokladnoscPrzewidywan;
    expect(highAcc).toBeGreaterThan(lowAcc);
  });

  it('pozwala wypowiedzieć wojnę i przesunąć pasek stanu wojny płatną akcją', () => {
    let s = newState('war-test');
    s.company.finances.stanKonta = 1_000_000;
    const targetId = s.competitors[0].id;

    s = declareWar(s, targetId);
    expect(s.competitors[0].atWarWithPlayer).toBe(true);
    expect(s.activeWars?.length).toBe(1);
    expect(s.activeWars?.[0].warScore).toBe(0);

    const action = getWarActions().find((a) => !a.requiresMafia)!;
    const beforeCash = s.company.finances.stanKonta;
    const afterAction = performWarAction(s, targetId, action.id);

    expect(afterAction.company.finances.stanKonta).toBeLessThan(beforeCash);
    expect(afterAction.activeWars?.[0].warScore).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from '../initialState';
import { tickEconomy, scaleByInflation } from '../economy';
import { WORKER_CONFIG, COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG, BELOW_REQUIRED_WORKERS_PENALTY } from '../constants';
import type { GameState } from '../types';

function makeState(overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof createInitialState>[0]> = {}): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed: 'test',
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity: 'handel',
    companyName: 'Test',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
    ...overrides,
  });
}

describe('tickEconomy', () => {
  it('generuje przychód z działalności nawet bez pracowników (CEO sam pracuje)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = tickEconomy(state);
    expect(result.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('pobiera czynsz co turę', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = tickEconomy(state);
    expect(result.company.finances.wydatki.czynsz).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

it('koszt pracowników = suma stawek wg WORKER_CONFIG', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.bezUmowy = 2;
    state.company.employees.zlecenie = 3;
    state.company.employees.uop = 1;

    const expected = scaleByInflation(
      2 * WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.wage +
      3 * WORKER_CONFIG.zlecenie.wage +
      1 * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.wage,
      state
    );

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    expect(result.company.finances.wydatki.pracownicy).toBe(expected);
  });

  it('pracownik UOP zarabia więcej niż kosztuje (netto na plus)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.uop = 1;

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    const revenue = result.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci;
    const wages = result.company.finances.wydatki.pracownicy;

    expect(revenue).toBeGreaterThan(wages);
    expect(WORKER_CONFIG.uop.revenue).toBeGreaterThan(WORKER_CONFIG.uop.wage);
  });

  it('pracownik bez umowy zarabia więcej niż kosztuje (netto na plus)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.employees.bezUmowy = 1;

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    expect(WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.revenue).toBeGreaterThan(WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.wage);
    expect(result.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('po turze z dodatnim saldem stanKonta rośnie', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const before = state.company.finances.stanKonta;

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    expect(result.company.finances.stanKonta).toBeGreaterThan(before);
  });

  it('resetuje bankruptWarningTurns gdy saldo dodatnie', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = 2;

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    expect(result.bankruptWarningTurns).toBe(0);
  });

  it('zwiększa bankruptWarningTurns gdy saldo ujemne', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.finances.stanKonta = -100_000;
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = 0;

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    expect(result.bankruptWarningTurns).toBe(1);
  });

  it('kara reputacyjna gdy firma ma mniej pracowników niż wymaga rozmiar', () => {
    const state = makeState({ size: 'medium' });
    // medium wymaga 10 pracowników, a startowy stan ma 10 - obniżamy
    state.company.employees.zlecenie = 0;
    state.company.employees.uop = 0;
    state.company.employees.bezUmowy = 0;
    const repBefore = state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza;

    const result = tickEconomy(state);

    expect(COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG.medium.requiredWorkers).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(result.company.reputation.opiniaBranza).toBeLessThan(repBefore);
    expect(repBefore - result.company.reputation.opiniaBranza).toBe(BELOW_REQUIRED_WORKERS_PENALTY);
  });

  it('podatki są dodatnie gdy przychód dodatni', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const result = tickEconomy(state);
    expect(result.company.finances.wydatki.podatki).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { createInitialState } from '../initialState';
import { recomputeReputation, politicianWeight } from '../reputation';
import { MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD } from '../constants';
import type { GameState } from '../types';

function makeState(): GameState {
  return createInitialState({
    seed: 'test',
    difficulty: 'medium',
    inPZPR: false,
    activity: 'handel',
    companyName: 'Test',
    suffix: '',
    size: 'micro',
  });
}

describe('recomputeReputation', () => {
  it('zwraca obiekt z polem ogolna', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);
    expect(rep).toHaveProperty('ogolna');
    expect(typeof rep.ogolna).toBe('number');
  });

  it('ogolna mieści się w zakresie 0-100', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);
    expect(rep.ogolna).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(rep.ogolna).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
  });

  it('bardzo wysoka opinia mafii szkodzi opinii publicznej', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia = MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD + 20;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaMedia = 50;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy = 50;

    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);

    // Opinia publiczna nie może być wyższa niż przed kaskadą mafii
    // (dokładna wartość zależy od wag, ale spadek musi być)
    expect(rep.opiniaPubliczna).toBeLessThan(80);
  });

  it('klamruje wartości do 0-100', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = 150;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = -50;
    state.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy = 200;

    const rep = recomputeReputation(state);

    for (const key of ['branza', 'opiniaPubliczna', 'opiniaPracownicy', 'opiniaBranza', 'opiniaMafia', 'opiniaMedia', 'opiniaPolitycy', 'ogolna'] as const) {
      expect(rep[key]).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(rep[key]).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
    }
  });

  it('nie modyfikuje oryginalnego stanu (immutability)', () => {
    const state = makeState();
    const originalRep = { ...state.company.reputation };
    recomputeReputation(state);
    expect(state.company.reputation).toEqual(originalRep);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { makeRng } from '../rng';

describe('makeRng', () => {
  it('daje ten sam wynik dla tego samego seeda', () => {
    const rng1 = makeRng('test-seed', 1);
    const rng2 = makeRng('test-seed', 1);
    expect(rng1.next()).toBe(rng2.next());
    expect(rng1.next()).toBe(rng2.next());
  });

  it('daje różne wyniki dla różnych seedów', () => {
    const rng1 = makeRng('seed-a', 1);
    const rng2 = makeRng('seed-b', 1);
    expect(rng1.next()).not.toBe(rng2.next());
  });

  it('int zwraca liczby w podanym zakresie', () => {
    const rng = makeRng('test', 1);
    for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
      const value = rng.int(1, 10);
      expect(value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(value).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);
    }
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import type { ContractType, GameState } from '../types';
import { createInitialState, getEligibleActivities } from '../initialState';
import { nextTurn, consumeActivity, resolveEventChoice } from '../turn';
import { matchesConditions } from '../events';
import { makeRng } from '../rng';
import {
  applyHire,
  applyLoan,
  applyEmergencyLoan,
  applyJoinParty,
  applyLeaveParty,
  applySetPace,
  toggleAccountant,
  changeCompanySize,
  buildBranch,
  buyAd,
  rentWarehouse,
} from '../actions';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

type Rng = ReturnType<typeof makeRng>;
type Policy = 'passive' | 'random';

function allActivityIds(): string[] {
  const raw: any = branchesData;
  const cats: any[] = Array.isArray(raw)
    ? raw
    : Array.isArray(raw?.categories)
      ? raw.categories
      : Object.values(raw ?? {});
  const ids: string[] = [];
  for (const cat of cats) for (const a of cat?.activities ?? []) if (a?.id) ids.push(a.id);
  return ids;
}

const ACTIVITY_IDS = allActivityIds();

function newGame(seed: string, i: number): GameState {
  const s = createInitialState({
    seed,
    difficulty: (['easy', 'medium', 'hard'] as const)[i % 3],
    inPZPR: i % 2 === 0,
    activity: ACTIVITY_IDS[i % ACTIVITY_IDS.length],
    companyName: 'Test',
    suffix: 'sp. z o.o.',
    size: 'micro',
  });
  s.currentActivities = getEligibleActivities(s);
  return s;
}

const median = (a: number[]) => {
  const b = [...a].sort((x, y) => x - y);
  return b.length ? b[Math.floor(b.length / 2)] : 0;
};

function findBadNumbers(obj: unknown, path = 'state', out: string[] = []): string[] {
  if (out.length > 5) return out;
  if (typeof obj === 'number') {
    if (!Number.isFinite(obj)) out.push(path);
  } else if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    obj.forEach((v, i) => findBadNumbers(v, `${path}[${i}]`, out));
  } else if (obj && typeof obj === 'object') {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) findBadNumbers(v, `${path}.${k}`, out);
  }
  return out;
}

function checkInvariants(s: GameState, before?: GameState): string[] {
  const v: string[] = [];

  const bad = findBadNumbers(s);
  if (bad.length) v.push(`NaN/Infinity w stanie: ${bad.slice(0, 3).join(', ')}`);

  for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(s.company.reputation)) {
    if (typeof val === 'number' && (val < -1e-9 || val > 100 + 1e-9)) v.push(`Reputacja poza 0-100: ${k}`);
  }
  for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(s.company.employees)) {
    if (val < 0 || !Number.isInteger(val)) v.push(`Zła liczba pracowników: ${k}`);
  }
  for (const [k, val] of Object.entries(s.company.resources)) {
    if (val < 0) v.push(`Ujemny zasób w magazynie: ${k}`);
  }
  for (const l of s.company.loans) {
    if (l.remaining < -1e-6) v.push('Ujemne saldo kredytu');
  }
  if (![1, 2, 3, 4].includes(s.quarter)) v.push(`Zły kwartał: ${s.quarter}`);

  const expectedPhase = s.year < 2005 ? 'chaos' : s.year < 2030 ? 'duopol' : 'invasion';
  if (s.phase !== expectedPhase) v.push('Faza nie pasuje do roku');
  if (s.pendingEvents.length > 1) v.push('Więcej niż 1 event naraz');

  if (before && !s.gameOver) {
    if (s.turn !== before.turn + 1) v.push('Licznik tury nie wzrósł o 1');
    const step = before.pace === 'year' ? 4 : 1;
    const dq = s.year * 4 + s.quarter - (before.year * 4 + before.quarter);
    if (dq !== step) v.push(`Data przesunęła się o ${dq} kwartałów zamiast ${step}`);
  }
  return v;
}

const CONTRACTS: ContractType[] = ['bezUmowy', 'zlecenie', 'uop'];

function randomActions(s: GameState, rng: Rng): GameState {
  const cash = s.company.finances.stanKonta;
  if (cash < 0 && s.bankruptWarningTurns > 0 && rng.next() < 0.7) return applyEmergencyLoan(s);

  const r = rng.next();
  if (r < 0.1) return applyHire(s, rng.pick(CONTRACTS), rng.pick([-1, 1, 1, 2]));
  if (r < 0.16 && cash < 50_000) {
    return applyLoan(
      s,
      rng.pick<'bank' | 'szara_strefa'>(['bank', 'szara_strefa']),
      rng.pick([10_000, 50_000, 100_000])
    );
  }
  if (r < 0.22) return buyAd(s, rng.pick<'radio' | 'tv' | 'internet'>(['radio', 'tv', 'internet']));
  if (r < 0.26) return changeCompanySize(s, rng.pick<'up' | 'down'>(['up', 'down']));
  if (r < 0.3) return buildBranch(s);
  if (r < 0.34) return rentWarehouse(s);
  if (r < 0.37) return toggleAccountant(s);
  if (r < 0.42 && s.politics.parties.length > 0) return applyJoinParty(s, rng.pick(s.politics.parties).id);
  if (r < 0.44) return applyLeaveParty(s);
  if (r < 0.46) return applySetPace(s, rng.pick<'quarter' | 'year'>(['quarter', 'year']));
  return s;
}

function playTurn(s: GameState, policy: Policy, rng: Rng): GameState {
  if (s.pendingEvents.length > 0) {
    const ev = s.pendingEvents[0];
    const choices = ev.choices.filter((c) => !c.requires || matchesConditions(s, c.requires));
    if (choices.length > 0) {
      const choice = policy === 'passive' ? choices[0] : rng.pick(choices);
      s = resolveEventChoice(s, ev.id, choice.id);
    }
  }
  if (s.gameOver) return s;

  if (s.currentActivities.length > 0 && (policy === 'passive' || rng.next() < 0.8)) {
    const id = policy === 'passive' ? s.currentActivities[0] : rng.pick(s.currentActivities);
    s = consumeActivity(s, id);
  }
  if (s.gameOver || policy === 'passive') return s;

  return randomActions(s, rng);
}

interface GameReport {
  final: GameState;
  violations: string[];
  eventTurns: number;
  emptyTurns: number;
  chases: number;
  combats: number;
  maxCash: number;
}

function playGame(seed: string, i: number, policy: Policy): GameReport {
  const mathRng = makeRng(seed, 777);
  vi.spyOn(Math, 'random').mockImplementation(() => mathRng.next());

  const rng = makeRng(seed, 1_000_001);
  let s = newGame(seed, i);
  const rep: GameReport = {
    final: s,
    violations: [],
    eventTurns: 0,
    emptyTurns: 0,
    chases: 0,
    combats: 0,
    maxCash: 0,
  };

  const check = (label: string, before?: GameState) => {
    for (const msg of checkInvariants(s, before)) {
      rep.violations.push(`${msg}  [${seed}, tura ${s.turn}, ${label}]`);
    }
  };

  for (let step = 0; step < 400 && !s.gameOver && s.year < 2031; step++) {
    s = playTurn(s, policy, rng);
    check('akcje gracza');
    if (s.gameOver) break;

    const before = s;
    s = nextTurn(s);
    check('nextTurn', before);

    if (s.pendingEvents.length > 0) rep.eventTurns++;
    else if (s.year < 2030) rep.emptyTurns++;
    if (s.activeChase) {
      rep.chases++;
      s.activeChase = null;
    }
    if (s.activeCombat) {
      rep.combats++;
      s.activeCombat = null;
    }
    rep.maxCash = Math.max(rep.maxCash, s.company.finances.stanKonta);
  }

  if (s.gameOver && nextTurn(s) !== s) rep.violations.push(`nextTurn działa po gameOver  [${seed}]`);
  rep.final = s;
  return rep;
}

const N_GAMES = 100;

describe('symulacja headless', () => {
  for (const policy of ['passive', 'random'] as const) {
    it(
      `${N_GAMES} gier, gracz "${policy}": niezmienniki + statystyki`,
      () => {
        const reports: GameReport[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < N_GAMES; i++) reports.push(playGame(`sim-${policy}-${i}`, i, policy));

        const reasons: Record<string, number> = {};
        for (const r of reports) {
          const key = r.final.gameOver?.reason ?? `dożył ${r.final.year}`;
          reasons[key] = (reasons[key] ?? 0) + 1;
        }
        const avg = (f: (r: GameReport) => number) =>
          (reports.reduce((a, r) => a + f(r), 0) / reports.length).toFixed(1);

        console.log(
          [
            `=== Gracz "${policy}", ${N_GAMES} gier ===`,
            `Zakończenia: ${JSON.stringify(reasons)}`,
            `Mediana roku końca: ${median(reports.map((r) => r.final.year))}`,
            `Tury z eventem / bez (przed 2030): ${avg((r) => r.eventTurns)} / ${avg((r) => r.emptyTurns)}`,
            `Ucieczki / walki: ${avg((r) => r.chases)} / ${avg((r) => r.combats)}`,
            `Mediana kasy na końcu: ${Math.round(median(reports.map((r) => r.final.company.finances.stanKonta))).toLocaleString('pl-PL')}`,
          ].join('\n')
        );

        const groups = new Map<string, { n: number; example: string }>();
        for (const v of reports.flatMap((r) => r.violations)) {
          const key = v.replace(/\s+\[.*\]$/, '');
          const g = groups.get(key);
          if (g) g.n++;
          else groups.set(key, { n: 1, example: v });
        }
        const summary = [...groups.entries()].map(([k, g]) => `${g.n}x ${k}`);
        expect(summary).toEqual([]);
      },
      120_000
    );
  }

  it('ten sam seed daje ten sam przebieg', () => {
    const a = playGame('sim-det', 3, 'random');
    const b = playGame('sim-det', 3, 'random');
    expect(JSON.stringify(a.final)).toEqual(JSON.stringify(b.final));
  });
});

const clamp = (x: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, x));
const MAX_UNDO_STEPS = 15;

const CONTRACT_LABELS: Record<ContractType, string> = {
  bezUmowy: 'bez umowy',
  zlecenie: 'umowa zlecenie',
  uop: 'umowa o pracę',
};

const AD_LABELS: Record<'radio' | 'tv' | 'internet', string> = {
  radio: 'Reklama radiowa',
  tv: 'Reklama telewizyjna',
  internet: 'Reklama internetowa',
};

/** Zapisuje migawkę stanu przed mutacją, aby gracz mógł cofnąć ruch w bieżącej turze. */
export function captureUndoSnapshot(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const prevStack = Array.isArray(s.undoStack) ? s.undoStack.slice(-(MAX_UNDO_STEPS - 1)) : [];
  const snapshot = structuredClone(state);
  delete snapshot.undoStack;
  s.undoStack = [...prevStack, snapshot];
  return s;
}

/** Cofa ostatni odwracalny ruch wykonany w bieżącej turze. */
export function undoLastAction(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (state.gameOver) return state;
  const stack = Array.isArray(state.undoStack) ? state.undoStack : [];
  if (stack.length === 0) return state;

  const previous = structuredClone(stack[stack.length - 1]);
  previous.undoStack = stack.slice(0, -1);
  return previous;
}

/** Rejestruje akcję wykonaną w bieżącej turze na rozwijanym pasku aktywności. */
export function recordTurnAction(
  state: GameState,
  entry: Omit<TurnActionEntry, 'id'>
): void {
  if (!Array.isArray(state.turnActions)) {
    state.turnActions = [];
  }
  state.turnActions.push({
    id: `ta_${state.turn}_${state.turnActions.length}`,
    canUndo: true,
    ...entry,
  });
  state.lastTradeNotice = entry.text;
}

export function calculateInstallmentPrice(price: number, installments: InstallmentOption): number {
  if (installments <= 1) return price;
  const tiers: Record<InstallmentOption, number> = {
    1: 0,
    2: 0.05,
    6: 0.10,
    12: 0.15,
    24: 0.20,
  };
  const surcharge = tiers[installments] ?? 0.05;
  return Math.round(price * (1 + surcharge));
}

export function applyHire(state: GameState, contract: ContractType, delta: number): GameState {
  const current = state.company.employees[contract];
  const pendingForContract = (state.pendingHires ?? [])
    .filter(h => h.contract === contract)
    .reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.count, 0);

  if (delta > 0) {
    const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
    const repImpact = WORKER_CONFIG[contract].reputationImpact;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy = clamp(
      s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy + repImpact * delta
    );
    const nextPendingIdx = (s.pendingHires ?? []).length;
    s.pendingHires = [
      ...(s.pendingHires ?? []),
      {
        id: `hire_${s.turn}_${nextPendingIdx}_${contract}`,
        contract,
        count: delta,
        hiredOnTurn: s.turn,
      },
    ];
    s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

    recordTurnAction(s, {
      icon: '👥',
      category: 'Pracownicy',
      text: `Zakontraktowano +${delta} os. (${CONTRACT_LABELS[contract]}) — dołączą w kolejnej turze.`,
      target: 'employees',
    });

    return s;
  }

  if (delta < 0) {
    const fireWant = Math.abs(delta);

    // Najpierw możemy anulować zakontraktowanych jeszcze nieobecnych w załodze
    if (current === 0 && pendingForContract > 0) {
      const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
      let leftToCancel = Math.min(fireWant, pendingForContract);
      const nextPending = [];
      for (const h of s.pendingHires ?? []) {
        if (h.contract === contract && leftToCancel > 0) {
          const take = Math.min(h.count, leftToCancel);
          leftToCancel -= take;
          if (h.count - take > 0) {
            nextPending.push({ ...h, count: h.count - take });
          }
        } else {
          nextPending.push(h);
        }
      }
      s.pendingHires = nextPending;
      recordTurnAction(s, {
        icon: '👥',
        category: 'Pracownicy',
        text: `Anulowano zakontraktowanie ${Math.abs(delta)} os. (${CONTRACT_LABELS[contract]}).`,
        target: 'employees',
      });
      return s;
    }

    const newCount = Math.max(0, current - fireWant);
    const fired = current - newCount;
    if (fired <= 0) return state;

    const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
    if (contract === 'uop') {
      const severance = scaleByInflation(fired * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.severance, s);
      if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < severance) return state;
      s.company.finances.stanKonta -= severance;
    } else {
      s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy = Math.max(
        0,
        s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy - fired * 2
      );
    }

    s.company.employees[contract] = newCount;
    s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

    recordTurnAction(s, {
      icon: '👥',
      category: 'Pracownicy',
      text: `Zwolniono ${fired} os. (${CONTRACT_LABELS[contract]}).`,
      target: 'employees',
    });

    return s;
  }

  return state;
}

export function applyLoan(state: GameState, source: 'bank' | 'szara_strefa', amount: number): GameState {
  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  const rate = getLoanQuarterlyRate(source, getInflationRate(s));
  s.company.loans.push({
    id: `loan_${s.turn}_${s.company.loans.length}`,
    source,
    principal: amount,
    remaining: amount,
    interestRate: rate,
    quartersLeft: 12,
  });
  s.company.finances.stanKonta += amount;
  if (source === 'bank') s.company.finances.wplywy.srodkiZKredytow += amount;
  else s.company.finances.wplywy.dofinansowaniaSzaraStrefa += amount;

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '💰',
    category: 'Finanse',
    text: `Zaciągnięto ${source === 'bank' ? 'kredyt bankowy' : 'pożyczkę z szarej strefy'}: ${formatMoney(amount, s)}.`,
    target: 'statistics',
  });

  return s;
}

export function applyChangeActivity(state: GameState, activityOrList: string | string[]): GameState {
  if (state.turn - state.company.lastActivityChangeTurn < ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN) return state;

  const selectedActivities = Array.isArray(activityOrList)
    ? activityOrList
    : activityOrList.split(',').filter(Boolean);

  if (selectedActivities.length === 0) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  const oldBranza = s.company.reputation.branza;
  const { branza, mafia } = computeStartingActivityStats(selectedActivities);

  s.company.activity = selectedActivities[0];
  s.company.activities = selectedActivities;
  s.company.reputation.branza = branza;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaMafia = mafia;
  s.company.lastActivityChangeTurn = s.turn;

  const hasServices = selectedActivities.some(id => id.startsWith('usl_') || id.startsWith('gast_'));
  if (!hasServices) {
    s.activeServiceIds = [];
  }

  const delta = s.company.reputation.branza - oldBranza;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + delta * 0.3);

  // Samowolna zmiana branży pod kontrolą spółki-matki irytuje centralę
  const parentInfo = getPlayerParentInfo(s);
  if (parentInfo?.isControlling) {
    s.company.parentDispleasure = clamp((s.company.parentDispleasure ?? 0) + 20);
  }

  // Stały świat: nie resetujemy ligi — dociągamy tylko z rezerwy, jeśli w nowej branży jest < 3 firm
  ensureBranchCompetitors(s, true);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  const info = getActivityFullInfo(selectedActivities);
  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🏢',
    category: 'Działalność',
    text: `Zmieniono profil działalności na: ${info.category} (${info.subcategory}).`,
    target: 'changeActivity',
  });

  return s;
}

export function applyJoinParty(state: GameState, partyId: string): GameState {
  if (state.year <= 1989) return state;
  if (state.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[state.phase] >= MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.ceo.currentPartyId = partyId;
  s.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[s.phase] += 1;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy - 5);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🏛️',
    category: 'Polityka',
    text: `Wstąpiono do partii (${partyId.toUpperCase()}).`,
    target: 'party',
  });

  return s;
}

export function applyLeaveParty(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (!state.ceo.currentPartyId) return state;
  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.ceo.currentPartyId = null;

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🏛️',
    category: 'Polityka',
    text: 'Opuszczono dotychczasową partię polityczną.',
    target: 'party',
  });

  return s;
}

export function applyEmergencyLoan(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta > 0) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  const amount = scaleByInflation(BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.amount, s);
  const rate = Math.max(
    BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.interestRate,
    getLoanQuarterlyRate('szara_strefa', getInflationRate(s)) + 0.03
  );

  s.company.loans.push({
    id: `emergency_${s.turn}`,
    source: 'bank',
    principal: amount,
    remaining: amount,
    interestRate: Math.round(rate * 1000) / 1000,
    quartersLeft: BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.quartersLeft,
  });
  s.company.finances.stanKonta += amount;
  s.bankruptWarningTurns = 0;

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🚨',
    category: 'Finanse',
    text: `Zaciągnięto kredyt ratunkowy: ${formatMoney(amount, s)}.`,
    target: 'statistics',
  });

  return s;
}

/** Tempo gry jest na sztywno ustawione na kwartalne ('quarter'). */
export function applySetPace(state: GameState, _pace: Pace): GameState {
  if (state.pace === 'quarter') return state;
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  s.pace = 'quarter';
  return s;
}

export function buyOfficeItem(state: GameState, itemId: string, price: number): GameState {
  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < price) return state;
  if (state.company.inventory.includes(itemId)) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= price;
  s.company.inventory.push(itemId);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🪑',
    category: 'Biuro',
    text: `Kupiono wyposażenie biura (${itemId}) za ${formatMoney(price, s)}.`,
    target: 'office',
  });

  return s;
}

export function applyEffects(state: GameState, e: EventEffects) {
  if (e.reputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.reputation)) {
      if (k === 'dokladnoscPrzewidywan' || k === 'bazowaDokladnosc') {
        const currentBase = state.company.reputation.bazowaDokladnosc ?? 50;
        state.company.reputation.bazowaDokladnosc = clamp(currentBase + (v as number));
      } else {
        (state.company.reputation as any)[k] = clamp((state.company.reputation as any)[k] + (v as number));
      }
    }
  }
  if (e.stanKonta) {
    state.company.finances.stanKonta += scaleByInflation(e.stanKonta, state);
  }
  if (e.wplywy) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wplywy)) {
      (state.company.finances.wplywy as any)[k] += scaleByInflation(v as number, state);
    }
  }
  if (e.wydatki) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wydatki)) {
      (state.company.finances.wydatki as any)[k] += scaleByInflation(v as number, state);
    }
  }
  if (e.flagsAdd) {
    for (const flag of e.flagsAdd) {
      if (!state.ceo.flags.includes(flag)) state.ceo.flags.push(flag);
    }
  }
  if (e.flagsRemove) {
    state.ceo.flags = state.ceo.flags.filter(f => !e.flagsRemove!.includes(f));
  }
  if (e.joinMafia) state.ceo.inMafia = true;
  if (e.leaveMafia) state.ceo.inMafia = false;
  if (e.setParty) state.ceo.party = e.setParty;
  if (e.setCurrentPartyId) state.ceo.currentPartyId = e.setCurrentPartyId;
  if (e.divideFinances) {
    state.denominated = true;
  }
  if (e.employeesDelta) {
    if (e.employeesDelta.bezUmowy) {
      state.company.employees.bezUmowy = Math.max(0, state.company.employees.bezUmowy + e.employeesDelta.bezUmowy);
    }
    if (e.employeesDelta.zlecenie) {
      state.company.employees.zlecenie = Math.max(0, state.company.employees.zlecenie + e.employeesDelta.zlecenie);
    }
    if (e.employeesDelta.uop) {
      state.company.employees.uop = Math.max(0, state.company.employees.uop + e.employeesDelta.uop);
    }
  }
  if (e.addItem && !state.company.inventory.includes(e.addItem)) {
    state.company.inventory.push(e.addItem);
  }

  // --- Efekty dyplomacji, wojen i spółki-matki ---
  if (typeof e.parentDispleasureDelta === 'number') {
    state.company.parentDispleasure = clamp(
      (state.company.parentDispleasure ?? 0) + e.parentDispleasureDelta
    );
    const parentInfo = getPlayerParentInfo(state);
    if (parentInfo?.isControlling && state.company.parentDispleasure >= 100) {
      state.gameOver = {
        reason: `Odwołanie ze stanowiska CEO — spółka-matka (${parentInfo.parent.name}) straciła do Ciebie cierpliwość.`,
      };
    }
  }
  if (e.joinWarAsAlly) {
    joinAllyWarInState(state, e.joinWarAsAlly.warId, e.joinWarAsAlly.allyId);
  }
  if (e.refuseWarCall) {
    refuseAllyWarInState(state, e.refuseWarCall.allyId);
  }
  if (e.sellEquityTo) {
    grantEquityToCompetitor(
      state,
      e.sellEquityTo.holderId,
      e.sellEquityTo.percent,
      e.sellEquityTo.cashReceived
    );
  }
  if (e.addTribute) {
    if (!Array.isArray(state.tributes)) state.tributes = [];
    state.tributes.push({
      id: `trib_fx_${state.turn}_${state.tributes.length}`,
      ...e.addTribute,
    });
  }
  if (e.payReparationsTo) {
    state.company.finances.stanKonta -= e.payReparationsTo.amount;
    state.company.finances.wydatki.kary += e.payReparationsTo.amount;
    const comp = (state.competitors ?? []).find(c => c.id === e.payReparationsTo!.compId);
    if (comp) comp.value += e.payReparationsTo.amount;
  }
  if (e.startWarWith) {
    openWarInState(state, 'player', e.startWarWith, false);
  }

  state.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(state);
}

export function toggleAccountant(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.company.hasAccountant = !s.company.hasAccountant;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🧮',
    category: 'Pracownicy',
    text: s.company.hasAccountant ? 'Zatrudniono księgową.' : 'Zwolniono księgową.',
    target: 'employees',
  });

  return s;
}

export function changeCompanySize(state: GameState, direction: 'up' | 'down'): GameState {
  const sizes: CompanySize[] = ['micro', 'small', 'medium', 'large', 'very_large', 'corporation'];
  const currentIndex = sizes.indexOf(state.company.size);

  let newIndex = currentIndex;
  if (direction === 'up' && currentIndex < sizes.length - 1) newIndex++;
  if (direction === 'down' && currentIndex > 0) newIndex--;
  if (newIndex === currentIndex) return state;

  const newSize = sizes[newIndex];
  const config = COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG[newSize];

  if (direction === 'up') {
    const totalWorkers =
      state.company.employees.bezUmowy +
      state.company.employees.zlecenie +
      state.company.employees.uop;
    const activeCats = getCategoryIdsForActivities(getCompanyActivities(state.company));
    const reqWorkersForNextSize = getRequiredWorkers(newSize, activeCats.length);
    const expansionCost = scaleByInflation(config.cost, state);

    if (totalWorkers < reqWorkersForNextSize) return state;
    if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < expansionCost) return state;
  }

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  if (direction === 'up') {
    const expansionCost = scaleByInflation(config.cost, s);
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= expansionCost;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna += config.repBonus;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy += config.repBonus;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza += config.repBonus;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia += config.repBonus;
  } else {
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna += config.repPenalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPracownicy += config.repPenalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza += config.repPenalty;
    s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia += config.repPenalty;
  }

  s.company.size = newSize;
  s.company.warehouseCapacity = COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[newSize].warehouseCapacity;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  const label = COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[newSize]?.label ?? newSize;
  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: direction === 'up' ? '📈' : '📉',
    category: 'Działalność',
    text: `${direction === 'up' ? 'Rozbudowano' : 'Zmniejszono'} firmę do rozmiaru: ${label}.`,
    target: 'changeActivity',
  });

  return s;
}

export function buildBranch(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (!['large', 'very_large', 'corporation'].includes(state.company.size)) return state;
  const cost = scaleByInflation(BRANCH_COST, state);
  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < cost) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= cost;
  s.company.branches += 1;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza += 5;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🏗️',
    category: 'Działalność',
    text: `Otwarto nową filię (łącznie: ${s.company.branches}) za ${formatMoney(cost, s)}.`,
    target: 'changeActivity',
  });

  return s;
}

export function buyAd(state: GameState, type: 'radio' | 'tv' | 'internet'): GameState {
  let baseCost = 0;
  let repGain = 0;
  let mediaGain = 0;

  if (type === 'radio') { baseCost = 5000; repGain = 2; mediaGain = 2; }
  if (type === 'tv') { baseCost = 50000; repGain = 8; mediaGain = 10; }
  if (type === 'internet') { baseCost = 15000; repGain = 4; mediaGain = 5; }

  const cost = scaleByInflation(baseCost, state);
  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < cost) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= cost;
  s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna + repGain);
  s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaMedia + mediaGain);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '📣',
    category: 'Marketing',
    text: `Wykupiono: ${AD_LABELS[type]} za ${formatMoney(cost, s)}.`,
    target: 'changeActivity',
  });

  return s;
}

export function rentWarehouse(state: GameState): GameState {
  const cost = scaleByInflation(10000, state);
  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < cost) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= cost;
  s.company.warehouseCapacity += 50;

  const activeCats = getCategoryIdsForActivities(getCompanyActivities(s.company));
  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🏬',
    category: 'Magazyn',
    text: `Powiększono magazyn o +50 szt. (pojemność: ${s.company.warehouseCapacity}) za ${formatMoney(cost, s)}.`,
    target: activeCats.includes('handel') ? 'buy' : 'production',
  });

  return s;
}

export function buyGoods(
  state: GameState,
  supplier: TradeSupplier,
  amount: number,
  installments: InstallmentOption = 1
): GameState {
  if (amount <= 0) return state;

  const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const capacity = state.company.warehouseCapacity || COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size].warehouseCapacity;
  if (currentStock + amount > capacity) return state;

  let discount = 0;
  if (amount === 10) discount = 0.10;
  if (amount === 50 || amount === 100) discount = 0.25;

  const allianceDiscount = getAllianceTradeDiscount(state);
  const totalDiscount = Math.min(0.45, discount + allianceDiscount);

  const baseTotal = Math.round(supplier.price * amount * (1 - totalDiscount));
  const finalPrice = calculateInstallmentPrice(baseTotal, installments);

  if (installments <= 1 && state.company.finances.stanKonta < finalPrice) {
    return state;
  }

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  if (installments <= 1) {
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= finalPrice;
    s.company.finances.wydatki.zakupy += finalPrice;
  } else {
    s.company.loans.push({
      id: `trade_install_${s.turn}_${s.company.loans.length}`,
      source: 'zakup_raty',
      principal: finalPrice,
      remaining: finalPrice,
      interestRate: 0,
      quartersLeft: installments,
    });
  }

  const resId = `${supplier.id}_${supplier.quality}`;
  s.company.resources[resId] = (s.company.resources[resId] || 0) + amount;

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🛒',
    category: 'Handel',
    text: `Kupiono ${amount} szt. (${supplier.name}) za ${formatMoney(finalPrice, s)}${allianceDiscount > 0 ? ` (rabat sojuszniczy -${Math.round(allianceDiscount * 100)}%)` : ''}${installments > 1 ? ` w ${installments} ratach` : ''}.`,
    target: 'buy',
  });

  return s;
}

export function sellGoods(
  state: GameState,
  buyer: TradeBuyer,
  resId: string,
  amount: number,
  unitPrice: number,
  installments: InstallmentOption = 1
): GameState {
  if (amount <= 0) return state;

  const available = state.company.resources[resId] || 0;
  if (available < amount) return state;

  const parts = resId.split('_');
  const quality = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10) || 1;

  if (buyer.req?.minQuality && quality < buyer.req.minQuality) {
    return state;
  }

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  s.company.resources[resId] -= amount;
  if (s.company.resources[resId] <= 0) {
    delete s.company.resources[resId];
  }

  const baseEstimated = Math.round(unitPrice * buyer.multiplier * amount);
  const estimatedRevenue = calculateInstallmentPrice(baseEstimated, installments);
  const accuracy = s.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const { min, max } = getRevenueRange(estimatedRevenue, accuracy);

  s.pendingSales.push({
    id: `sale_${s.turn}_${s.pendingSales.length}`,
    buyerName: buyer.name,
    resId,
    amount,
    estimatedRevenue,
    minRevenue: min,
    maxRevenue: max,
    quality,
    hasComplaints: buyer.complaints,
    installments,
  });

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '📦',
    category: 'Sprzedaż',
    text: `Wysłano ${amount} szt. do ${buyer.name} (orientacyjnie: ${formatMoneyRange(min, max, s)}${installments > 1 ? ` w ${installments} ratach` : ''}).`,
    target: 'sell',
  });

  return s;
}

export function resolvePendingSales(state: GameState): void {
  if (state.pendingSales.length === 0) return;

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 13 + 9);
  let totalRevenueThisTurn = 0;
  const lines: string[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < state.pendingSales.length; i++) {
    const sale = state.pendingSales[i];
    const accuracy = state.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
    const fallbackRange = getRevenueRange(sale.estimatedRevenue, accuracy);
    const minRev = sale.minRevenue ?? fallbackRange.min;
    const maxRev = sale.maxRevenue ?? fallbackRange.max;

    const finalRevenue = Math.round(minRev + rng.next() * (maxRev - minRev));
    const installments = sale.installments || 1;

    if (installments <= 1) {
      totalRevenueThisTurn += finalRevenue;
      state.company.finances.stanKonta += finalRevenue;
      state.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci += finalRevenue;
      lines.push(
        `• ${sale.buyerName}: ${sale.amount} szt. (jakość ${sale.quality}) → ostateczny utarg: ${formatMoney(finalRevenue, state)} (szacowano ${formatMoneyRange(minRev, maxRev, state)}).`
      );
    } else {
      const quarterlyPayment = Math.round(finalRevenue / installments);
      totalRevenueThisTurn += quarterlyPayment;
      state.company.finances.stanKonta += quarterlyPayment;
      state.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci += quarterlyPayment;
      lines.push(
        `• ${sale.buyerName}: ${sale.amount} szt. (jakość ${sale.quality}) → rata 1/${installments}: +${formatMoney(quarterlyPayment, state)} (ostateczna wartość kontraktu: ${formatMoney(finalRevenue, state)}).`
      );
    }

    if (sale.hasComplaints && sale.quality < 5) {
      const chance = 0.6 - sale.quality * 0.1;
      if (rng.next() < chance) {
        state.pendingComplaints.push({
          id: `comp_${state.turn}_${i}_${state.pendingComplaints.length}`,
          itemName: sale.resId.split('_')[0],
          buyerName: sale.buyerName,
          penalty: 10 - sale.quality,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  if (totalRevenueThisTurn > 0 || state.pendingComplaints.length > 0) {
    const newsItem: NewsItem = {
      id: `sales_report_${state.turn}_${state.news.length}`,
      turn: state.turn,
      year: state.year,
      quarter: state.quarter,
      headline: 'Rozliczenie transakcji handlowych i zbytu',
      body: `Kontrahenci rozliczyli zamówienia z poprzedniego kwartału.\nŁączny wpływ do kasy: ${formatMoney(totalRevenueThisTurn, state)}.\n\nSzczegóły:\n${lines.join('\n')}`,
      category: 'economy',
      read: false,
    };
    state.news.unshift(newsItem);
    if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
  }

  state.pendingSales = [];
}

export function resolveComplaint(state: GameState, id: string, accept: boolean): GameState {
  const comp = state.pendingComplaints.find(c => c.id === id);
  if (!comp) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  if (accept) {
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= scaleByInflation(5000, s);
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna + 2);
  } else {
    s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna - comp.penalty);
    s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = clamp(s.company.reputation.opiniaBranza - comp.penalty);
  }

  s.pendingComplaints = s.pendingComplaints.filter(c => c.id !== id);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '📋',
    category: 'Reklamacje',
    text: `${accept ? 'Uznano' : 'Odrzucono'} reklamację od ${comp.buyerName}.`,
    target: 'sell',
  });

  return s;
}

// --- Moduł Produkcji ---

export function buyProductionTool(state: GameState, toolId: string): GameState {
  const tool = getProductionTools(state).find(t => t.id === toolId);
  if (!tool || tool.owned || !tool.meetsRequirements) return state;
  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < tool.effectiveCost) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= tool.effectiveCost;
  s.company.finances.wydatki.zakupy += tool.effectiveCost;
  s.company.ownedTools = [...getOwnedToolIds(s), tool.id];

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🔧',
    category: 'Produkcja',
    text: `Kupiono narzędzie na stałe: ${tool.name} (${formatMoney(tool.effectiveCost, s)}).`,
    target: 'production',
  });

  return s;
}

export function runProductionBatch(
  state: GameState,
  ingredientId: string,
  toolId: string,
  workersCount: number,
  sellNowAmount = 0
): GameState {
  const safeWorkers = Math.floor(workersCount);
  if (safeWorkers < 1) return state;

  const availableWorkers = getAvailableProductionWorkers(state);
  if (safeWorkers > availableWorkers) return state;

  const ingredient = getProductionIngredients(state).find(i => i.id === ingredientId);
  if (!ingredient || !ingredient.meetsRequirements) return state;

  const ownedTools = getOwnedToolIds(state);
  if (!ownedTools.includes(toolId)) return state;

  const estimate = estimateProductionBatch(state, ingredientId, toolId, safeWorkers);
  if (!estimate) return state;

  if (state.company.finances.stanKonta < estimate.totalCost) return state;

  const clampedSellNow = Math.max(0, Math.min(estimate.units, Math.floor(sellNowAmount)));
  const toStore = estimate.units - clampedSellNow;

  if (toStore > 0) {
    const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const capacity = state.company.warehouseCapacity || COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size].warehouseCapacity;
    if (currentStock + toStore > capacity) return state;
  }

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  s.company.finances.stanKonta -= estimate.totalCost;
  s.company.finances.wydatki.zakupy += estimate.totalCost;
  s.productionWorkersUsedThisTurn = (s.productionWorkersUsedThisTurn ?? 0) + safeWorkers;

  const resId = `prod_${ingredient.id}_${estimate.quality}`;

  if (toStore > 0) {
    s.company.resources[resId] = (s.company.resources[resId] || 0) + toStore;
  }

  let saleSummary = '';
  if (clampedSellNow > 0) {
    const estimatedRevenue = Math.round(estimate.estimatedUnitPrice * clampedSellNow);
    const accuracy = s.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
    const { min, max } = getRevenueRange(estimatedRevenue, accuracy);

    s.pendingSales.push({
      id: `prod_sale_${s.turn}_${s.pendingSales.length}`,
      buyerName: 'Zbyt produkcji',
      resId,
      amount: clampedSellNow,
      estimatedRevenue,
      minRevenue: min,
      maxRevenue: max,
      quality: estimate.quality,
      hasComplaints: false,
      installments: 1,
    });

    saleSummary =
      toStore > 0
        ? ` Zbyto ${clampedSellNow} szt. (${formatMoneyRange(min, max, s)}), do magazynu: ${toStore} szt.`
        : ` Zbyto całość (${formatMoneyRange(min, max, s)}).`;
  } else {
    saleSummary = ` Całość (${toStore} szt.) złożono w magazynie.`;
  }

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '⚙️',
    category: 'Produkcja',
    text: `Wyprodukowano ${estimate.units} szt. (jakość ${estimate.quality}/10, ${safeWorkers} os., koszt ${formatMoney(estimate.totalCost, s)}).${saleSummary}`,
    target: 'production',
  });

  return s;
}

export function sellProducedStock(state: GameState, resId: string, amount: number): GameState {
  const safeAmount = Math.floor(amount);
  if (safeAmount <= 0 || !resId.startsWith('prod_')) return state;

  const available = state.company.resources[resId] || 0;
  if (available < safeAmount) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  const info = estimateStoredProducedItem(s, resId);
  const estimatedRevenue = Math.round(info.estimatedUnitPrice * safeAmount);
  const accuracy = s.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const { min, max } = getRevenueRange(estimatedRevenue, accuracy);

  s.company.resources[resId] -= safeAmount;
  if (s.company.resources[resId] <= 0) {
    delete s.company.resources[resId];
  }

  s.pendingSales.push({
    id: `prod_stock_sale_${s.turn}_${s.pendingSales.length}`,
    buyerName: 'Zbyt produkcji (magazyn)',
    resId,
    amount: safeAmount,
    estimatedRevenue,
    minRevenue: min,
    maxRevenue: max,
    quality: info.quality,
    hasComplaints: false,
    installments: 1,
  });

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🚛',
    category: 'Produkcja',
    text: `Wysłano do zbytu ${safeAmount} szt. (${info.label}, jakość ${info.quality}/10) — ok. ${formatMoneyRange(min, max, s)}.`,
    target: 'production',
  });

  return s;
}

// --- Eksporty zachowane dla kompatybilności ---

export function buyRawMaterials(
  state: GameState,
  supplier: TradeSupplier,
  amount: number,
  installments: InstallmentOption = 1
): GameState {
  if (amount <= 0) return state;

  const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const capacity = state.company.warehouseCapacity || COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size].warehouseCapacity;
  if (currentStock + amount > capacity) return state;

  let discount = 0;
  if (amount === 10) discount = 0.10;
  if (amount === 50 || amount === 100) discount = 0.25;

  const allianceDiscount = getAllianceTradeDiscount(state);
  const totalDiscount = Math.min(0.45, discount + allianceDiscount);

  const baseTotal = Math.round(supplier.price * amount * (1 - totalDiscount));
  const finalPrice = calculateInstallmentPrice(baseTotal, installments);

  if (installments <= 1 && state.company.finances.stanKonta < finalPrice) {
    return state;
  }

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  if (installments <= 1) {
    s.company.finances.stanKonta -= finalPrice;
    s.company.finances.wydatki.zakupy += finalPrice;
  } else {
    s.company.loans.push({
      id: `raw_install_${s.turn}_${s.company.loans.length}`,
      source: 'zakup_raty',
      principal: finalPrice,
      remaining: finalPrice,
      interestRate: 0,
      quartersLeft: installments,
    });
  }

  const resId = `raw_${supplier.id}_${supplier.quality}`;
  s.company.resources[resId] = (s.company.resources[resId] || 0) + amount;

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🧱',
    category: 'Produkcja',
    text: `Kupiono ${amount} szt. surowców (${supplier.name}) za ${formatMoney(finalPrice, s)}${allianceDiscount > 0 ? ` (rabat sojuszniczy -${Math.round(allianceDiscount * 100)}%)` : ''}.`,
    target: 'production',
  });

  return s;
}

export function produceGoods(state: GameState, rawResId: string, amount: number): GameState {
  if (amount <= 0 || !rawResId.startsWith('raw_')) return state;

  const availableRaw = state.company.resources[rawResId] || 0;
  if (availableRaw < amount) return state;

  const maxCap = getProductionCapacity(state);
  const used = state.productionUsedThisTurn ?? 0;
  if (used + amount > maxCap) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);

  const parts = rawResId.split('_');
  const rawQuality = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10) || 1;

  const emp = s.company.employees;
  const totalWorkers = emp.bezUmowy + emp.zlecenie + emp.uop;
  const qualityBonus = totalWorkers > 0 && emp.uop / totalWorkers >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
  const finalQuality = Math.min(10, rawQuality + qualityBonus);

  s.company.resources[rawResId] -= amount;
  if (s.company.resources[rawResId] <= 0) {
    delete s.company.resources[rawResId];
  }

  const prodResId = `prod_wyrob_${finalQuality}`;
  s.company.resources[prodResId] = (s.company.resources[prodResId] || 0) + amount;
  s.productionUsedThisTurn = used + amount;

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '⚙️',
    category: 'Produkcja',
    text: `Wytworzono ${amount} szt. wyrobów gotowych (jakość ${finalQuality}/10).`,
    target: 'production',
  });

  return s;
}

export function toggleService(state: GameState, serviceId: string): GameState {
  const activeIds = state.activeServiceIds ?? [];
  const available = getAvailableServices(state);
  const target = available.find(srv => srv.id === serviceId);

  if (activeIds.includes(serviceId)) {
    const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
    s.activeServiceIds = activeIds.filter(id => id !== serviceId);
    recordTurnAction(s, {
      icon: '🛠️',
      category: 'Usługi',
      text: `Wstrzymano stałą usługę: ${target?.title ?? serviceId}.`,
      target: 'services',
    });
    return s;
  }

  if (!target || !target.meetsRequirements) return state;

  const totalSlots = getTotalServiceWorkerSlots(state);
  const busySlots = getBusyServiceWorkers(state);
  if (busySlots + target.workersRequired > totalSlots) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  s.activeServiceIds = [...activeIds, serviceId];
  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '🛠️',
    category: 'Usługi',
    text: `Uruchomiono stałą usługę: "${target.title}" (${formatMoneyRange(target.minPayout, target.maxPayout, s)} / kw.).`,
    target: 'services',
  });

  return s;
}

export function acceptServiceOrder(state: GameState, order: ServiceDefinition | string): GameState {
  const serviceId = typeof order === 'string' ? order : order.id;
  return toggleService(state, serviceId);
}

export function resolvePendingServices(state: GameState): void {
  const activeIds = state.activeServiceIds ?? [];
  if (activeIds.length === 0) return;

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 71 + 13);
  const evaluated = getAvailableServices(state);
  const totalSlots = getTotalServiceWorkerSlots(state);
  let usedSlots = 0;

  let totalPayout = 0;
  const lines: string[] = [];
  const stillActiveIds: string[] = [];

  for (const id of activeIds) {
    const srv = evaluated.find(item => item.id === id);
    if (!srv) continue;

    if (!srv.meetsRequirements) {
      lines.push(
        `• ❌ ${srv.title}: usługa wyłączona — firma przestała spełniać wymagania (${srv.missingReasons.join(', ')}).`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (usedSlots + srv.workersRequired > totalSlots) {
      lines.push(
        `• ❌ ${srv.title}: usługa wyłączona — brak wolnych mocy kadrowych (wymagane ${srv.workersRequired} os.).`
      );
      continue;
    }

    usedSlots += srv.workersRequired;
    stillActiveIds.push(id);

    const rolledRevenue = Math.round(srv.minPayout + rng.next() * (srv.maxPayout - srv.minPayout));
    const repProtection = (state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna + state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza) / 400;
    const effectiveRisk = Math.max(0.02, srv.risk - repProtection);

    if (rng.next() < effectiveRisk) {
      const reducedPayout = Math.round(rolledRevenue * 0.5);
      totalPayout += reducedPayout;
      state.company.finances.stanKonta += reducedPayout;
      state.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci += reducedPayout;
      state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna - 3);
      lines.push(
        `• ⚠ ${srv.title}: reklamacje klientów! Przychód wyniósł tylko ${formatMoney(reducedPayout, state)} (-3 rep.).`
      );
    } else {
      totalPayout += rolledRevenue;
      state.company.finances.stanKonta += rolledRevenue;
      state.company.finances.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci += rolledRevenue;
      state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza = clamp(state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza + srv.repGain);
      lines.push(
        `• ✓ ${srv.title}: +${formatMoney(rolledRevenue, state)} (szacowano ${formatMoneyRange(srv.minPayout, srv.maxPayout, state)}, +${srv.repGain} rep. branży).`
      );
    }
  }

  state.activeServiceIds = stillActiveIds;
  state.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(state);

  if (lines.length > 0) {
    const newsItem: NewsItem = {
      id: `services_report_${state.turn}_${state.news.length}`,
      turn: state.turn,
      year: state.year,
      quarter: state.quarter,
      headline: 'Rozliczenie świadczonych usług',
      body: `Podsumowanie przychodów z aktywnych usług za miniony kwartał.\nŁączny przychód: ${formatMoney(totalPayout, state)}.\n\nSzczegóły:\n${lines.join('\n')}`,
      category: 'economy',
      read: false,
    };
    state.news.unshift(newsItem);
    if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
  }
}
