import type { GameState } from './types';

type CurrencyContext = Pick<GameState, 'denominated' | 'year'> | boolean | null | undefined;

export function isDenominated(ctx: CurrencyContext): boolean {
  if (typeof ctx === 'boolean') return ctx;
  if (!ctx) return true;
  if (typeof ctx.denominated === 'boolean') return ctx.denominated;
  return typeof ctx.year === 'number' ? ctx.year >= 1995 : true;
}

function formatScaledUnit(absValue: number, divisor: number, unit: string): string {
  const scaled = absValue / divisor;
  const maxDigits = scaled >= 100 ? 0 : scaled >= 10 ? 1 : 2;
  const formatted = scaled.toLocaleString('pl-PL', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: maxDigits,
  });
  return `${formatted} ${unit}`;
}

/**
 * Formatuje kwotę z silnika na walutę widoczną dla gracza:
 * - przed denominacją (1989–1994): mnoży ×10 000 i używa skrótów (tys. / mln / mld zł),
 * - po denominacji (1995+): wyświetla w PLN (opcjonalnie kompaktowo).
 */
export function formatMoney(
  amount: number,
  ctx: CurrencyContext,
  opts?: { compact?: boolean; signed?: boolean }
): string {
  const safeAmount = Number.isFinite(amount) ? amount : 0;
  const sign = safeAmount < 0 ? '-' : opts?.signed && safeAmount > 0 ? '+' : '';
  const denominated = isDenominated(ctx);

  if (!denominated) {
    const absOld = Math.abs(Math.round(safeAmount * 10_000));
    if (absOld >= 1_000_000_000_000) return `${sign}${formatScaledUnit(absOld, 1_000_000_000_000, 'bln zł')}`;
    if (absOld >= 1_000_000_000) return `${sign}${formatScaledUnit(absOld, 1_000_000_000, 'mld zł')}`;
    if (absOld >= 1_000_000) return `${sign}${formatScaledUnit(absOld, 1_000_000, 'mln zł')}`;
    if (absOld >= 10_000) return `${sign}${formatScaledUnit(absOld, 1_000, 'tys. zł')}`;
    return `${sign}${absOld.toLocaleString('pl-PL')} zł`;
  }

  const absNew = Math.abs(Math.round(safeAmount));
  if (opts?.compact) {
    if (absNew >= 1_000_000_000) return `${sign}${formatScaledUnit(absNew, 1_000_000_000, 'mld PLN')}`;
    if (absNew >= 1_000_000) return `${sign}${formatScaledUnit(absNew, 1_000_000, 'mln PLN')}`;
  }
  return `${sign}${absNew.toLocaleString('pl-PL')} PLN`;
}

export function formatMoneyRange(min: number, max: number, ctx: CurrencyContext): string {
  return `${formatMoney(min, ctx)} – ${formatMoney(max, ctx)}`;
}

import type { GameState, Wplywy, Wydatki } from './types';
import {
  BELOW_REQUIRED_WORKERS_PENALTY,
  WORKER_CONFIG,
  SKARBOWKA_DECAY_BEZ_UMOWY,
  SKARBOWKA_DECAY_ZLECENIE,
  SKARBOWKA_GAIN_UOP,
  ACCOUNTANT_COST,
  ACCOUNTANT_BONUS,
} from './constants';
import {
  getCompanyActivities,
  getCategoryIdsForActivities,
  getRequiredWorkers,
} from './activityHelper';
import { makeRng } from './rng';

const BASE_REVENUE = 5000;
const CZYNSZ = 2500;
const TAX_RATE = 0.15;

export const INITIAL_INFLATION_RATE = 36;
export const INITIAL_PRICE_INDEX = 0.25;

export function getPriceIndex(state: GameState): number {
  const idx = state.macroStats?.priceIndex;
  return typeof idx === 'number' && Number.isFinite(idx) && idx > 0 ? idx : 1;
}

export function getInflationRate(state: GameState): number {
  const rate = state.macroStats?.inflationRate;
  return typeof rate === 'number' && Number.isFinite(rate) ? rate : 5;
}

export function scaleByInflation(amount: number, state: GameState): number {
  return Math.round(amount * getPriceIndex(state));
}

/**
 * Kwartalne oprocentowanie nowego kredytu zależne od bieżącej rocznej stopy inflacji.
 * Bank: marża 1.5% + 85% inflacji kwartalnej (min. 2%, max. 15%).
 * Szara strefa: +4.5 p.p. ponad bank.
 */
export function getLoanQuarterlyRate(
  source: 'bank' | 'szara_strefa',
  annualInflationRate: number
): number {
  const safeAnnual = Math.max(0, annualInflationRate);
  const quarterlyInflation = Math.pow(1 + safeAnnual / 100, 0.25) - 1;
  const bankRate = Math.min(0.15, Math.max(0.02, 0.015 + quarterlyInflation * 0.85));
  const raw = source === 'bank' ? bankRate : bankRate + 0.045;
  return Math.round(raw * 1000) / 1000;
}

function getTargetInflationForYear(year: number): number {
  if (year <= 1989) return 36;
  if (year === 1990) return 42;
  if (year === 1991) return 30;
  if (year === 1992) return 22;
  if (year === 1993) return 18;
  if (year === 1994) return 14;
  if (year === 1995) return 11;
  if (year === 1996) return 9;
  if (year === 1997) return 7.5;
  if (year === 1998) return 6;
  if (year === 1999) return 5;
  if (year === 2000) return 4.5;
  if (year === 2001) return 3.5;
  return 3.0;
}

function calculateWorkerRevenue(count: number, baseRevenue: number): number {
  let total = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const efficiency = Math.max(0.2, 1 - i * 0.05);
    total += baseRevenue * efficiency;
  }
  return total;
}

export function tickEconomy(state: GameState): GameState {
  const oldProfitability = state.profitabilityScore;
  const c = structuredClone(state.company);
  const f = c.finances;
  const priceIndex = getPriceIndex(state);

  const baseRevenue = Math.round(estimateRevenue(c) * priceIndex);
  f.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci = baseRevenue;

  const rawWages =
    c.employees.bezUmowy * WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.wage +
    c.employees.zlecenie * WORKER_CONFIG.zlecenie.wage +
    c.employees.uop      * WORKER_CONFIG.uop.wage;

  f.wydatki.pracownicy = Math.round(rawWages * priceIndex);
  f.wydatki.czynsz = Math.round(CZYNSZ * priceIndex);
  f.wydatki.podatki = Math.max(0, Math.round(baseRevenue * TAX_RATE));

  // Raty zaciągniętych kredytów pozostają nominalne (inflacja realnie zmniejsza stare długi)
  f.wydatki.splacaneKredyty = 0;
  for (const loan of c.loans) {
    if (loan.remaining <= 0) continue;
    const installment = loan.remaining / Math.max(1, loan.quartersLeft);
    const interest = loan.remaining * loan.interestRate;
    f.wydatki.splacaneKredyty += Math.round(installment + interest);
    loan.remaining = Math.max(0, Math.round(loan.remaining - installment));
    loan.quartersLeft -= 1;
  }

  let skarbowkaDelta =
    c.employees.bezUmowy * SKARBOWKA_DECAY_BEZ_UMOWY +
    c.employees.zlecenie * SKARBOWKA_DECAY_ZLECENIE +
    c.employees.uop      * SKARBOWKA_GAIN_UOP;

  if (c.hasAccountant) {
    skarbowkaDelta += ACCOUNTANT_BONUS;
    f.wydatki.inne = Math.round(ACCOUNTANT_COST * priceIndex);
  } else {
    f.wydatki.inne = 0;
  }

  c.reputation.opiniaSkarbowa = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, c.reputation.opiniaSkarbowa + skarbowkaDelta));

  const inflow = sum(f.wplywy) - f.wplywy.srodkiZKredytow - f.wplywy.dofinansowaniaSzaraStrefa;
  const outflow = sum(f.wydatki);
  f.stanKonta = Math.round(f.stanKonta + inflow - outflow);

  const totalWorkers = c.employees.bezUmowy + c.employees.zlecenie + c.employees.uop;
  const activeCategories = getCategoryIdsForActivities(getCompanyActivities(c));
  const requiredWorkers = getRequiredWorkers(c.size, activeCategories.length);

  if (totalWorkers < requiredWorkers) {
    c.reputation.opiniaBranza = Math.max(0, c.reputation.opiniaBranza - BELOW_REQUIRED_WORKERS_PENALTY);
  }

  if (state.ceo.flags.includes('hipnoza_dziala')) {
    c.finances.stanKonta += Math.round(5000 * priceIndex);
  }
  if (state.ceo.flags.includes('hipnoza_nie_dziala')) {
    c.finances.stanKonta -= Math.round(5000 * priceIndex);
  }

  state.company = c;

  if (f.stanKonta < 0) {
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = (state.bankruptWarningTurns ?? 0) + 1;
  } else {
    state.bankruptWarningTurns = 0;
  }

  let score = 50;

  if (c.size === 'micro') score += 10;
  else if (c.size === 'small') score += 5;
  else if (c.size === 'medium') score -= 5;
  else if (c.size === 'large') score -= 10;
  else if (c.size === 'very_large') score -= 15;
  else if (c.size === 'corporation') score -= 20;

  if (f.wydatki.pracownicy > 0) {
    const ratio = f.wplywy.przychodyZDzialalnosci / f.wydatki.pracownicy;
    score += Math.min(20, (ratio - 1) * 10);
  }

  const avgRep = (c.reputation.opiniaBranza + c.reputation.opiniaPubliczna) / 2;
  score += (avgRep - 50) / 2;

  state.profitabilityScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(score)));

  const diff = state.profitabilityScore - oldProfitability;
  if (diff > 5) state.profitabilityTrend = 'up2';
  else if (diff > 0) state.profitabilityTrend = 'up1';
  else if (diff < -5) state.profitabilityTrend = 'down2';
  else if (diff < 0) state.profitabilityTrend = 'down1';
  else state.profitabilityTrend = 'neutral';

  // --- Makroekonomia (bezrobocie, rozwój, inflacja) ---
  const macroRng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn + 555);
  state.macroStats.unemployment = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(100, state.macroStats.unemployment + (macroRng.next() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1))
  );
  state.macroStats.development = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(100, state.macroStats.development + (macroRng.next() > 0.6 ? 1 : 0))
  );

  const currentInflation = getInflationRate(state);
  const targetInflation = getTargetInflationForYear(state.year);
  const pull = (targetInflation - currentInflation) * 0.35;
  const noise = (macroRng.next() - 0.5) * 4;
  const devNudge =
    state.macroStats.development > 65 ? -0.3 : state.macroStats.development < 30 ? 0.4 : 0;

  const nextInflation = Math.round(
    Math.max(0.5, Math.min(55, currentInflation + pull + noise + devNudge)) * 10
  ) / 10;

  const quarterlyFactor = Math.pow(1 + nextInflation / 100, 0.25);
  const nextPriceIndex = Math.round(priceIndex * quarterlyFactor * 10000) / 10000;

  state.macroStats.inflationRate = nextInflation;
  state.macroStats.priceIndex = nextPriceIndex;

  // Bezpiecznik: gdyby event denominacji nie wylosował się w 1995 r., przełączamy automatycznie od 1996
  if (state.year >= 1996 && !state.denominated) {
    state.denominated = true;
  }

  return state;
}

function estimateRevenue(c: GameState['company']): number {
  const workerRevenue =
    calculateWorkerRevenue(c.employees.bezUmowy, WORKER_CONFIG.bezUmowy.revenue) +
    calculateWorkerRevenue(c.employees.zlecenie, WORKER_CONFIG.zlecenie.revenue) +
    calculateWorkerRevenue(c.employees.uop, WORKER_CONFIG.uop.revenue);

  const activityMultiplier = 1 + (c.reputation.branza - 50) / 200;
  return (BASE_REVENUE + workerRevenue) * activityMultiplier;
}

function sum(obj: Wplywy | Wydatki): number {
  return (Object.values(obj) as number[]).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
}

import type { GameState, GameEvent, EventConditions } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import eventsData from '../data/events.json';

const ALL_EVENTS: GameEvent[] = eventsData as GameEvent[];

export function matchesConditions(state: GameState, c: EventConditions): boolean {
  if (c.yearMin !== undefined && state.year < c.yearMin) return false;
  if (c.yearMax !== undefined && state.year > c.yearMax) return false;
  if (c.phase && !c.phase.includes(state.phase)) return false;
  if (c.difficulty && !c.difficulty.includes(state.difficulty)) return false;
  if (c.party && !c.party.includes(state.ceo.party)) return false;
  if (c.currentPartyId && (!state.ceo.currentPartyId || !c.currentPartyId.includes(state.ceo.currentPartyId))) return false;
  if (c.rulingParty && !c.rulingParty.some(p => state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(p))) return false;
  if (c.mafiaMember !== undefined && state.ceo.inMafia !== c.mafiaMember) return false;
  if (c.minStanKonta !== undefined && state.company.finances.stanKonta < c.minStanKonta) return false;
  if (c.maxStanKonta !== undefined && state.company.finances.stanKonta > c.maxStanKonta) return false;
  if (c.legalForm && !c.legalForm.includes(state.company.legalForm)) return false;
  if (c.activities && !c.activities.includes(state.company.activity)) return false;
  if (c.flags && !c.flags.every(f => state.ceo.flags.includes(f))) return false;
  if (c.notFlags && c.notFlags.some(f => state.ceo.flags.includes(f))) return false;
  if (c.hasParty === true && !state.ceo.currentPartyId) return false;
  if (c.hasParty === false && state.ceo.currentPartyId) return false;

  if (c.minReputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(c.minReputation)) {
      if ((state.company.reputation as any)[k] < (v as number)) return false;
    }
  }
  if (c.maxReputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(c.maxReputation)) {
      if ((state.company.reputation as any)[k] > (v as number)) return false;
    }
  }
  if (c.hasItem && !state.company.inventory.includes(c.hasItem)) return false;
  return true;
}

export function rollEvents(state: GameState): GameEvent[] {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn);
  const eligible = ALL_EVENTS.filter(e => {
    if (state.history.includes(e.id)) return false;
    return matchesConditions(state, e.conditions);
  });
  if (eligible.length === 0) return [];

  const picked = rng.weighted(eligible);
  return [picked];
}

export function createInitialState(opts: {
  seed: string;
  difficulty: Difficulty;
  inPZPR: boolean;
  activity: string;
  activities?: string[];
  companyName: string;
  suffix: string;
  usePrefix?: boolean;

}): GameState {

    ? opts.activity.split(',').filter(Boolean)

    opts.activities && opts.activities.length > 0 ? opts.activities : parsedFromActivity;

      flags: [],
      lastMafiaInvitationTurn: null,
    },

    company: {
      name: opts.companyName,
      suffix: opts.suffix,
      usePrefix: opts.usePrefix ?? true,
      size: opts.size,
      legalForm: 'cywilna',
      activity: primaryActivity,
      activities: selectedActivities,
      employees: { ...sizeCfg.startingEmployees },

          przychodyZDzialalnosci: 0,
          srodkiZKredytow: 0,
          dofinansowania: 0,
          dofinansowaniaSzaraStrefa: 0,
          dochodPasywny: 0,
          splatyZobowiazan: 0,
        },
        wydatki: {
          splacaneKredyty: 0,
          pracownicy: 0,
          czynsz: 0,
          podatki: 0,
          kary: 0,
          wydatkiNieplanowane: 0,
          zakupy: 0,
          inne: 0,
        },
      },
      reputation: {
        branza,

        opiniaPracownicy: startingRep,
        opiniaBranza: startingRep,
        opiniaMafia: mafia,
        opiniaMedia: 50,

        opiniaSkarbowa: 50,
        bazowaDokladnosc: 50,
        dokladnoscPrzewidywan: 50,
        ogolna: 50,

      lastActivityChangeTurn: -999,
      branches: 0,
      resources: {},
      warehouseCapacity: 50,
      externalEquity: [],
      parentCompanyId: null,
      parentDispleasure: 0,
    },

    politics: {
      parties: [],
      president: {
        id: 'wroniecki_1989',
        name: 'Włodzimierz Wroniecki',
        partyId: null,
        termStart: 1989,
        termEnd: 1990,
      },
      premierId: null,

    lastElectionResult: null,
    lastPresidentialResult: null,
    skarbowkaVisitTurn: null,
    activeCombat: null,
    activeChase: null,
    news: [],
    showIntro: true,
    profitabilityScore: 50,
    profitabilityTrend: 'neutral',
    gameOver: null,
    pendingSales: [],

    competitionCategory,

    macroStats: {
      unemployment: 10,
      development: 40,

      priceIndex: INITIAL_PRICE_INDEX,
    },
  };

  state.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(state);
  return state;
}

export function getEligibleActivities(state: GameState): string[] {
  const all = activitiesData as unknown as Activity[];
  const eligible = all.filter(a => matchesConditions(state, a.conditions));
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 7 + 3);
  return eligible
    .sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5)
    .slice(0, 3)
    .map(a => a.id);
}

function generatePartySchedule(seed: string): Record<string, { from: number; to: number }> {
  const rng = makeRng(seed, 12345);
  const schedule: Record<string, { from: number; to: number }> = {};
  for (const p of (partiesData as Party[])) {
    if (p.isPSL) {
      schedule[p.id] = { from: 1989, to: 9999 };
    } else {
      const fromShift = rng.int(-2, 2);
      const toShift = p.activeTo >= 9000 ? 0 : rng.int(-2, 2);
      schedule[p.id] = {
        from: p.activeFrom + fromShift,
        to: p.activeTo >= 9000 ? 9999 : p.activeTo + toShift,
      };
    }
  }
  return schedule;
}

import type { GameState, NewsItem, GameEvent, Party } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import {
  RUMOR_TEMPLATES,
  MAX_NEWS,
  FAKE_RUMORS,
  MISLEADING_RUMOR_CHANCE,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_THRESHOLD,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_RETRY,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_FIRST,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_RETRY_CHANCE,
  MAFIA_INVITATION_EXPIRES_IN,
  SKARBOWKA_THRESHOLD,
  SKARBOWKA_PREPARE_TURNS,
  FLOWERS_COST,
  FLOWERS_BONUS,
} from './constants';
import { matchesConditions } from './events';
import { applyEffects } from './actions';
import eventsData from '../data/events.json';
import partiesData from '../data/parties.json';
import customNewsData from '../data/custom_news.json';
import { getPartySize, getActiveParties } from './politics';

interface CustomNews {
  id: string;
  headline: string;
  body?: string;
  weight: number;
  conditions: GameEvent['conditions'];
}

export function addNewsItem(state: GameState, item: Omit<NewsItem, 'read'>): void {
  const fullItem: NewsItem = { ...item, read: false };
  state.news.unshift(fullItem);
  if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
}

export function generatePassiveNews(state: GameState): void {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 13 + 7);

  // 1. Plotki o nadchodzących eventach
  const upcomingOnce = (eventsData as GameEvent[]).filter(e =>
    e.once &&
    !state.history.includes(e.id) &&
    e.conditions.yearMin !== undefined &&
    e.conditions.yearMin > state.year &&
    e.conditions.yearMin <= state.year + 2
  );

  if (rng.next() < 0.35) {
    const isFake = rng.next() < MISLEADING_RUMOR_CHANCE || upcomingOnce.length === 0;

    if (isFake) {
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `rumor_fake_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: rng.pick(FAKE_RUMORS),
        category: 'rumor',
      });
    } else {
      const ev = rng.pick(upcomingOnce);
      const template = rng.pick(RUMOR_TEMPLATES);
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `rumor_${state.turn}_${ev.id}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: template.replace('{topic}', ev.title),
        category: 'rumor',
      });
    }
  }

  // 2. Sondaże — co 4 tury w oknie 2 lat przed wyborami
  const electionIn = state.politics.nextParliamentaryElection - state.year;
  if (electionIn <= 2 && electionIn > 0 && state.turn % 4 === 0) {
    const active = getActiveParties(state);
    if (active.length > 0) {
      const sorted = [...active].sort((a, b) => sizeRank(b, state.year) - sizeRank(a, state.year));
      const leader = sorted[0];
      const second = sorted[1];
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `poll_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: `Sondaż: ${leader.name} prowadzi, ${second?.name ?? 'drugie miejsce nieznane'} tuż za nim`,
        body: 'Badanie przeprowadzone na zlecenie lokalnej gazety.',
        category: 'poll',
      });
    }
  }

  // 3. Partie pojawiają się / znikają
  for (const [id, sched] of Object.entries(state.politics.partySchedule)) {
    const p = (partiesData as Party[]).find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!p) continue;

    if (sched.from === state.year && !state.history.includes(`appeared_${id}`)) {
      state.history.push(`appeared_${id}`);
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `party_appear_${id}_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: `Powstaje nowe ugrupowanie: ${p.name}`,
        body: 'Grupa działaczy ogłasza utworzenie partii.',
        category: 'party',
      });
    }
    if (sched.to === state.year && sched.to < 9999 && !state.history.includes(`disappeared_${id}`)) {
      state.history.push(`disappeared_${id}`);
      addNewsItem(state, {
        id: `party_disappear_${id}_${state.turn}`,
        turn: state.turn,
        year: state.year,
        quarter: state.quarter,
        headline: `${p.name} rozwiązuje się`,
        body: 'Partia nie zdołała utrzymać struktur.',
        category: 'party',
      });
    }
  }

  // 4. Własne newsy (flavor)
  const eligibleCustom = (customNewsData as CustomNews[]).filter(c =>
    matchesConditions(state, c.conditions)
  );
  if (eligibleCustom.length > 0 && rng.next() < 0.4) {
    const picked = rng.weighted(eligibleCustom);
    addNewsItem(state, {
      id: `${picked.id}_${state.turn}`,
      turn: state.turn,
      year: state.year,
      quarter: state.quarter,
      headline: picked.headline,
      body: picked.body,
      category: 'custom',
    });
  }
}

function sizeRank(p: Party, year: number): number {
  const size = getPartySize(p, year);
  return size === 'very_large' ? 5 : size === 'large' ? 4 : size === 'medium' ? 3 : size === 'small' ? 2 : 1;
}

export function addActionableNewsItem(
  state: GameState,
  item: Omit<NewsItem, 'read' | 'resolved'>
): void {
  const fullItem: NewsItem = {
    ...item,
    read: false,
    resolved: false,
  };
  state.news.unshift(fullItem);
  if (state.news.length > MAX_NEWS) state.news.pop();
}

export function processExpiredActions(state: GameState): void {
  for (const item of state.news) {
    if (item.resolved) continue;
    if (!item.choices) continue;
    if (item.expiresAtTurn === undefined) continue;
    if (state.turn < item.expiresAtTurn) continue;

    item.resolved = true;
    item.resolvedChoiceId = '__expired__';

    if (item.onExpireEffects) {
      applyEffects(state, item.onExpireEffects);
    }
  }
}

export function checkMafiaInvitation(state: GameState): void {
  if (state.year < 1990) return;
  if (state.phase !== 'chaos') return;
  if (state.ceo.inMafia) return;
  if (state.ceo.flags.includes('zdradzil_mafie')) return;
  if (state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < MAFIA_INVITATION_THRESHOLD) return;

  const hasPending = state.news.some(
    n => n.id.startsWith('mafia_invitation_') && !n.resolved
  );
  if (hasPending) return;

  const refusedBefore =
    state.ceo.flags.includes('mafia_refused') ||
    state.ceo.flags.includes('mafia_ghosted');

  const lastTurn = state.ceo.lastMafiaInvitationTurn ?? -999;
  const gap = state.turn - lastTurn;
  const minGap = refusedBefore ? MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_RETRY : MAFIA_INVITATION_MIN_GAP_FIRST;
  if (gap < minGap) return;

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 17 + 5);
  const chance = refusedBefore ? MAFIA_INVITATION_RETRY_CHANCE : 1.0;
  if (rng.next() > chance) return;

  const isRetry = refusedBefore;
  addActionableNewsItem(state, {
    id: `mafia_invitation_${state.turn}`,
    turn: state.turn,
    year: state.year,
    quarter: state.quarter,
    headline: isRetry ? 'Kolejna propozycja' : 'Propozycja nie do odrzucenia',
    body: isRetry
      ? 'Mężczyzna w drogim garniturze znowu się zjawił. Tym razem pyta wprost, czy zmieniłeś zdanie.'
      : 'Do twojego biura przyszedł mężczyzna w drogim garniturze. Bez zapowiedzi. Mówi, że reprezentuje "ludzi, którzy mogą wiele". Proponuje współpracę.',
    category: 'custom',
    expiresAtTurn: state.turn + MAFIA_INVITATION_EXPIRES_IN,
    choices: [
      {
        id: 'accept',
        label: 'Wchodzę w to',
        description: 'Akceptujesz propozycję. Od teraz masz nowych partnerów.',
        effects: {
          joinMafia: true,
          reputation: { opiniaMafia: 10, opiniaPubliczna: -5 },
          flagsAdd: ['w_mafii'],
        },
      },
      {
        id: 'refuse',
        label: 'Odmawiam',
        description: 'Nie chcesz mieć z tym nic wspólnego. Ryzykujesz konsekwencje.',
        effects: {
          reputation: { opiniaMafia: -25 },
          flagsAdd: ['mafia_refused'],
        },
      },
    ],
    onExpireEffects: {
      reputation: { opiniaMafia: -10 },
      flagsAdd: ['mafia_ghosted'],
    },
  });

  state.ceo.lastMafiaInvitationTurn = state.turn;
}

export function checkTaxOfficeNotice(state: GameState): void {
  if (state.skarbowkaVisitTurn !== null) return;

  // Sojusze zmniejszają ryzyko kontroli Skarbówki
  const allyCount = (state.competitors ?? []).filter(
    (c) => (c.status ?? 'active') === 'active' && c.isAlliedWithPlayer
  ).length;

  const effectiveThreshold = Math.max(5, SKARBOWKA_THRESHOLD - allyCount * 3);
  if (state.company.reputation.opiniaSkarbowa >= effectiveThreshold) return;

  if (allyCount > 0) {
    const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 29 + 11);
    const blockChance = Math.min(0.45, allyCount * 0.15);
    if (rng.next() < blockChance) return;
  }

  addActionableNewsItem(state, {
    id: `skarbowka_notice_${state.turn}`,
    turn: state.turn,
    year: state.year,
    quarter: state.quarter,
    headline: 'Wiadomość od księgowej',
    body: 'Panie prezesie, mamy problem. Urząd skarbowy coś węszy. Słyszałam, że szykują kontrolę. Mamy jakieś 3 kwartały, zanim się zjawią.',
    category: 'custom',
    expiresAtTurn: state.turn + SKARBOWKA_PREPARE_TURNS,
    choices: [
      {
        id: 'send_flowers',
        label: 'Wysyłasz kwiaty do urzędu',
        description: `Kosztuje ${FLOWERS_COST} PLN, ale może poprawić nastroje.`,
        effects: {
          stanKonta: -FLOWERS_COST,
          reputation: { opiniaSkarbowa: FLOWERS_BONUS },
        },
      },
      {
        id: 'ignore',
        label: 'Ignorujesz sprawę',
        description: 'Może jakoś to będzie.',
        effects: {
          reputation: { opiniaSkarbowa: -5 },
        },
      },
    ],
    onExpireEffects: {
      reputation: { opiniaSkarbowa: -10 },
    },
  });

  state.skarbowkaVisitTurn = state.turn + SKARBOWKA_PREPARE_TURNS;
}

export function checkEmployeeUnrest(state: GameState): void {
  const hasPending = state.news.some(n => n.id.startsWith('employee_demand_') && !n.resolved);

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 11 + 4);

    expiresAtTurn: state.turn + 2,
    choices: [

        id: 'accept',
        label: 'Daję mu umowę o pracę',
        description: 'Zmieniasz formę zatrudnienia na UOP. Kosztuje to więcej, ale zyskujesz lojalność.',
        effects: {

      {

        label: 'Odmawiam',
        description: 'Wypadasz na chama. Pracownik odchodzi, a inni patrzą.',
        effects: {
          employeesDelta: { bezUmowy: -1 },
          reputation: { opiniaPracownicy: -20, opiniaPubliczna: -5 },
        },
      },
    ],
    onExpireEffects: {
      employeesDelta: { bezUmowy: -1 },
      reputation: { opiniaPracownicy: -10 },
    },

import type {
  GameState,
  ElectionResult,
  Party,
  Ideology,
  PresidentialCandidate,
  PresidentialResult,
  PartySize,
} from './types';
import { COALITION_RULES, CANDIDATE_FIRST_NAMES, CANDIDATE_LAST_NAMES, PARTY_SIZE_WEIGHT, ELECTION_YEARS } from './constants';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import partiesData from '../data/parties.json';
import presidentialElectionsData from '../data/presidential_elections.json';

const SEATS_TOTAL = 460;

interface PredefinedCandidate {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  partyId: string | null;
  partyLabel?: string;
  minPercent: number;
  maxPercent: number;
  typicalPercent: number;
}

interface PredefinedElection {
  year: number;
  candidates: PredefinedCandidate[];
  secondRoundWinnerId?: string;
  secondRoundChance?: number;
}

export function tickPolitics(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (state.year >= state.politics.nextParliamentaryElection) {
    state.politics = runParliamentaryElection(state);
    state.lastElectionResult = state.politics.lastElection;

    const nextElectionYear = ELECTION_YEARS.find(y => y > state.year);
    state.politics.nextParliamentaryElection = nextElectionYear ?? (state.year + 4);
  }

  if (state.year >= state.politics.nextPresidentialElection) {
    const result = computePresidentialElection(state);
    state.lastPresidentialResult = result;
    state.politics = {
      ...state.politics,
      president: {
        id: result.winner.id,
        name: result.winner.name,
        partyId: result.winner.partyId,
        termStart: state.year,
        termEnd: state.year + 5,
      },
      nextPresidentialElection: state.politics.nextPresidentialElection + 5,
    };
  }
  return state;
}

export function getPartySize(party: Party, year: number): PartySize {
  const sched = party.sizeSchedule.find(s => year >= s.from && year <= s.to);
  return sched ? sched.size : 'medium';
}

export function getActiveParties(state: GameState): Party[] {
  return (partiesData as Party[]).filter(p => {
    if (p.isPSL && !state.politics.pslAvailable) return false;
    const sched = state.politics.partySchedule[p.id];
    const isYearValid = sched
      ? state.year >= sched.from && state.year <= sched.to
      : state.year >= p.activeFrom && state.year <= p.activeTo;
    if (!isYearValid) return false;

    if (p.pzprAvailable !== 'both') {
      const playerIsPzpr = state.ceo.party === 'PZPR';
      if (playerIsPzpr && p.pzprAvailable === 'no') return false;
      if (!playerIsPzpr && p.pzprAvailable === 'yes') return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
}

function randomName(rng: ReturnType<typeof makeRng>): string {
  return `${rng.pick(CANDIDATE_FIRST_NAMES)} ${rng.pick(CANDIDATE_LAST_NAMES)}`;
}

function computePresidentialElection(state: GameState): PresidentialResult {
  const predefined = (presidentialElectionsData as PredefinedElection[]).find(e => e.year === state.year);
  if (predefined) return computeFromPredefined(state, predefined);
  return computeRandomElection(state);
}

function computeFromPredefined(state: GameState, pre: PredefinedElection): PresidentialResult {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 100 + 2);

  const candidates: PresidentialCandidate[] = pre.candidates.map(c => {
    const mid = (c.minPercent + c.maxPercent) / 2;
    const pull = c.typicalPercent - mid;
    const rolls = (rng.next() + rng.next() + rng.next()) / 3;
    const base = c.minPercent + (c.maxPercent - c.minPercent) * rolls;
    const value = Math.max(c.minPercent, Math.min(c.maxPercent, base + pull));

    return {
      id: c.id,
      name: c.name,
      partyId: c.partyId,
      partyLabel: c.partyLabel,
      votesPercent: value,
    };
  });

  const total = candidates.reduce((s, c) => s + c.votesPercent, 0);
  for (const c of candidates) c.votesPercent = (c.votesPercent / total) * 100;
  candidates.sort((a, b) => b.votesPercent - a.votesPercent);

  let winner = candidates[0];
  let secondRound = false;
  let secondRoundCandidates: PresidentialCandidate[] | undefined;

  if (candidates.length > 1 && candidates[0].votesPercent < 50) {
    secondRound = true;
    const c1 = candidates[0];
    const c2 = candidates[1];
    secondRoundCandidates = [c1, c2];

    if (pre.secondRoundWinnerId) {
      const forced = candidates.find(c => c.id === pre.secondRoundWinnerId);
      if (forced && (forced.id === c1.id || forced.id === c2.id)) {
        const chance = pre.secondRoundChance ?? 1.0;
        winner = rng.next() < chance ? forced : (forced.id === c1.id ? c2 : c1);
      }
    } else {
      const diff = (c1.votesPercent - c2.votesPercent) / 100;
      const c1Chance = Math.min(0.85, Math.max(0.5, 0.5 + diff + 0.1));
      winner = rng.next() < c1Chance ? c1 : c2;
    }
  }

  return { year: state.year, candidates, winner, secondRound, secondRoundCandidates };
}

function computeRandomElection(state: GameState): PresidentialResult {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 100 + 2);
  const parties = getActiveParties(state);
  const candidates: PresidentialCandidate[] = [];

  for (const p of parties) {
    const size = getPartySize(p, state.year);
    const base = PARTY_SIZE_WEIGHT[size] || 0.1;
    const noise = (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.08;
    const strength = Math.max(0.02, base + noise);
    candidates.push({
      id: `cand_${p.id}_${state.year}`,
      name: randomName(rng),
      partyId: p.id,
      votesPercent: strength * 100,
    });
  }

  const indepCount = rng.next() < 0.5 ? 1 : 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < indepCount; i++) {
    candidates.push({
      id: `cand_indep_${state.year}_${i}`,
      name: randomName(rng),
      partyId: null,
      votesPercent: (0.05 + rng.next() * 0.10) * 100,
    });
  }

  const total = candidates.reduce((s, c) => s + c.votesPercent, 0);
  for (const c of candidates) c.votesPercent = (c.votesPercent / total) * 100;
  candidates.sort((a, b) => b.votesPercent - a.votesPercent);

  let winner = candidates[0];
  let secondRound = false;
  let secondRoundCandidates: PresidentialCandidate[] | undefined;

  if (candidates.length > 1 && candidates[0].votesPercent < 50) {
    secondRound = true;
    const c1 = candidates[0];
    const c2 = candidates[1];
    secondRoundCandidates = [c1, c2];
    const diff = (c1.votesPercent - c2.votesPercent) / 100;
    const c1Chance = Math.min(0.85, Math.max(0.5, 0.5 + diff + 0.1));
    winner = rng.next() < c1Chance ? c1 : c2;
  }

  return { year: state.year, candidates, winner, secondRound, secondRoundCandidates };
}

function runParliamentaryElection(state: GameState) {
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 100 + 1);
  const parties = getActiveParties(state);
  const seats: Record<string, number> = {};
  let remaining = SEATS_TOTAL;

  for (const p of parties) {
    const size = getPartySize(p, state.year);
    const base = PARTY_SIZE_WEIGHT[size] || 0.1;
    const noise = (rng.next() - 0.5) * 0.08;
    const share = Math.max(0, base + noise);
    const s = Math.floor(share * SEATS_TOTAL);
    seats[p.id] = s;
    remaining -= s;
  }

  if (parties.length > 0) {
    const largest = parties.reduce((a, b) => (seats[a.id] > seats[b.id] ? a : b));
    seats[largest.id] += remaining;
  }

  const coalition = formCoalition(parties, seats);
  const premier = coalition[0] ?? null;

  const result: ElectionResult = { year: state.year, seats, coalition, premierId: premier };

  return {
    ...state.politics,
    parties,
    lastElection: result,
    rulingCoalition: coalition,
    premierId: premier,
  };
}

function formCoalition(parties: Party[], seats: Record<string, number>): string[] {
  const sorted = [...parties].sort((a, b) => seats[b.id] - seats[a.id]);
  const coalition: string[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  for (const p of sorted) {
    if (total >= SEATS_TOTAL / 2 + 1) break;
    if (coalition.length === 0) {
      coalition.push(p.id);
      total += seats[p.id];
      continue;
    }
    const canJoin = coalition.some(memberId => {
      const member = parties.find(x => x.id === memberId)!;
      return canCoalition(member.ideology, p.ideology);
    });
    if (canJoin) {
      coalition.push(p.id);
      total += seats[p.id];
    }
  }
  return coalition;
}

export function canCoalition(a: Ideology, b: Ideology): boolean {
  if (a === b) return true;
  return COALITION_RULES[a].includes(b) || COALITION_RULES[b].includes(a);
}

import type {
  GameState,
  TradeSupplier,
  TradeBuyer,
  Competitor,
  ProductionDataFile,
  ProductionIngredient,
  ProductionTool,
  CompanySize,
} from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import { getCompanyActivities, getActivityName } from './activityHelper';
import { generateTradeBuyers, getRevenueRange } from './tradeHelper';
import { getPriceIndex, getInflationRate, scaleByInflation } from './economy';
import { SIZE_ORDER, getAllianceTradeDiscount, getPlayerAllies } from './competition';
import { COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from './constants';
import productionDataRaw from '../data/production_data.json';

const productionData = productionDataRaw as unknown as ProductionDataFile;

const BASE_CEO_CAPACITY = 5;
const CAPACITY_PER_WORKER = 5;
const INGREDIENTS_PER_TURN = 4;

const BASE_COST_BY_QUALITY = [0, 25, 40, 65, 85, 110, 170, 240, 350, 480, 600];
const BASE_WHOLESALE_BY_QUALITY = [0, 60, 95, 150, 195, 240, 370, 520, 760, 1050, 1300];

export interface ProductionMarketFactors {
  demandScore: number;
  demandLabel: string;
  demandMultiplier: number;
  reputationScore: number;
  reputationMultiplier: number;
  branchRepScore: number;
  branchRepMultiplier: number;
  inflationRate: number;
  priceIndex: number;
  combinedMultiplier: number;
}

export interface EvaluatedIngredient extends ProductionIngredient {
  effectiveUnitCost: number;
  effectiveWholesalePrice: number;
  meetsRequirements: boolean;
  missingReasons: string[];
  isCompetitor?: boolean;
}

export interface EvaluatedTool extends ProductionTool {
  effectiveCost: number;
  owned: boolean;
  meetsRequirements: boolean;
  missingReasons: string[];
}

export interface ProductionBatchEstimate {
  ingredient: EvaluatedIngredient;
  tool: EvaluatedTool;
  factors: ProductionMarketFactors;
  workersCount: number;
  units: number;
  unitCost: number;
  totalCost: number;

/** Zwraca podkategorię produkcyjną gracza (np. 'prod_azbest'). */
export function getProductionActivityId(state: GameState): string {
  const activities = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  return activities.find(id => id.startsWith('prod_')) || state.company.activity;
}

/** Łączna liczba osób możliwych do przydzielenia do produkcji (Prezes + zatrudnieni pracownicy). */
export function getTotalProductionWorkers(state: GameState): number {
  const emp = state.company.employees;
  return 1 + emp.bezUmowy + emp.zlecenie + emp.uop;
}

/** Liczba wolnych osób do produkcji w bieżącym kwartale. */
export function getAvailableProductionWorkers(state: GameState): number {
  const total = getTotalProductionWorkers(state);
  const used = state.productionWorkersUsedThisTurn ?? 0;
  return Math.max(0, total - used);
}

/** Lista ID posiadanych na stałe narzędzi produkcyjnych (zawsze zawiera zestaw bazowy). */
export function getOwnedToolIds(state: GameState): string[] {
  const owned = Array.isArray(state.company.ownedTools) ? state.company.ownedTools : [];
  return owned.includes('tool_basic') ? owned : ['tool_basic', ...owned];
}

function getUopQualityBonus(state: GameState): number {
  const emp = state.company.employees;
  const totalWorkers = emp.bezUmowy + emp.zlecenie + emp.uop;
  return totalWorkers > 0 && emp.uop / totalWorkers >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
}

function describeDemand(score: number): string {
  if (score >= 80) return 'Bardzo wysoki';
  if (score >= 62) return 'Wysoki';
  if (score >= 40) return 'Umiarkowany';
  if (score >= 22) return 'Niski';
  return 'Załamanie popytu';
}

function shuffleWithRng<T>(items: T[], rng: ReturnType<typeof makeRng>): T[] {
  const copy = [...items];
  for (let i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = rng.int(0, i);
    [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]];
  }
  return copy;
}

/**
 * Wylicza bieżące czynniki wpływające na jedną orientacyjną kwotę zbytu produkcji:
 * popyt (rozwój, bezrobocie, koniunktura kwartalna), reputacja ogólna, reputacja w branży oraz inflacja.
 */
export function getProductionMarketFactors(state: GameState): ProductionMarketFactors {
  const prodActId = getProductionActivityId(state);
  const rng = makeRng(`${state.seed}:prod-demand:${prodActId}`, state.turn * 17 + 5);

  const dev = state.macroStats?.development ?? 40;
  const unemployment = state.macroStats?.unemployment ?? 10;
  const quarterlySwing = rng.int(-16, 16);

  const demandScore = Math.max(
    5,
    Math.min(100, Math.round(50 + (dev - 40) * 0.45 - (unemployment - 10) * 0.5 + quarterlySwing))
  );
  const demandMultiplier = 0.75 + (demandScore / 100) * 0.5;

  const reputationScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(state.company.reputation.ogolna ?? 50)));
  const reputationMultiplier = 0.85 + (reputationScore / 100) * 0.3;

  const branchRepScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza ?? 50)));
  const branchRepMultiplier = 0.85 + (branchRepScore / 100) * 0.3;

  const priceIndex = getPriceIndex(state);
  const inflationRate = getInflationRate(state);

  const combinedMultiplier = Math.max(
    0.55,
    demandMultiplier * reputationMultiplier * branchRepMultiplier
  );

  return {
    demandScore,
    demandLabel: describeDemand(demandScore),
    demandMultiplier,
    reputationScore,
    reputationMultiplier,
    branchRepScore,
    branchRepMultiplier,
    inflationRate,
    priceIndex,
    combinedMultiplier,
  };
}

/** Wycenia pełny katalog składników z JSON (uwzględnia reputację branżową oraz rabat sojuszniczy). */
function evaluateAllCatalogIngredients(state: GameState): EvaluatedIngredient[] {
  const ingredients = productionData.ingredients ?? [];
  const factors = getProductionMarketFactors(state);
  const dev = state.macroStats?.development ?? 40;
  const devFactor = 1 - Math.min(0.15, Math.max(-0.15, (dev - 40) * 0.003));
  const repDiscount = Math.min(0.12, (factors.branchRepScore / 100) * 0.12);
  const allianceDiscount = getAllianceTradeDiscount(state);
  const totalDiscount = Math.min(0.35, repDiscount + allianceDiscount);

  return ingredients.map((ing) => {
    const missingReasons: string[] = [];
    if (ing.req?.minRepBranza && state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza < ing.req.minRepBranza) {
      missingReasons.push(`Opinia w branży ≥ ${ing.req.minRepBranza}`);
    }
    if (ing.req?.minRepMafia && state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < ing.req.minRepMafia) {
      missingReasons.push(`Opinia mafii ≥ ${ing.req.minRepMafia}`);
    }

    const effectiveUnitCost = Math.max(
      5,
      Math.round(ing.unitCost * factors.priceIndex * devFactor * (1 - totalDiscount))
    );
    const effectiveWholesalePrice = Math.max(
      10,
      Math.round(ing.baseWholesalePrice * factors.priceIndex * factors.combinedMultiplier)
    );

    return {
      ...ing,
      effectiveUnitCost,
      effectiveWholesalePrice,
      meetsRequirements: missingReasons.length === 0,
      missingReasons,
      isCompetitor: false,
    };
  });
}

/** Zwraca wylosowaną na bieżącą turę pulę składników z katalogu + ofertę od aktywnej firmy (nie w stanie wojny). */
export function getProductionIngredients(state: GameState): EvaluatedIngredient[] {
  const allCatalog = evaluateAllCatalogIngredients(state);
  const factors = getProductionMarketFactors(state);
  const poolRng = makeRng(`${state.seed}:prod-ingredients-pool`, state.turn * 29 + 11);

  const unlocked = shuffleWithRng(
    allCatalog.filter(i => i.meetsRequirements),
    poolRng
  );
  const locked = shuffleWithRng(
    allCatalog.filter(i => !i.meetsRequirements),
    poolRng
  );

  const guaranteedUnlocked = unlocked.slice(0, Math.min(3, INGREDIENTS_PER_TURN));
  const remainingPool = shuffleWithRng(
    [...unlocked.slice(guaranteedUnlocked.length), ...locked],
    poolRng
  );

  const pickedCatalog = [
    ...guaranteedUnlocked,
    ...remainingPool.slice(0, Math.max(0, INGREDIENTS_PER_TURN - guaranteedUnlocked.length)),
  ].sort((a, b) => a.quality - b.quality || a.effectiveUnitCost - b.effectiveUnitCost);

  const list: EvaluatedIngredient[] = [...pickedCatalog];

  const eligibleComps = (state.competitors ?? []).filter(
    c => (c.status ?? 'active') === 'active' && !c.atWarWithPlayer
  );

  if (eligibleComps.length > 0) {
    const compRng = makeRng(`${state.seed}:prod-comp-ing`, state.turn * 37 + 19);
    const allies = getPlayerAllies(state);
    const friendsOfAllies = new Set<string>();
    for (const a of allies) {
      for (const fId of a.allies ?? []) friendsOfAllies.add(fId);
    }

    // Jeśli gracz ma sojuszników, mają większą szansę wystawić korzystną nadwyżkę
    const preferredPool =
      allies.length > 0 && compRng.next() < 0.55 ? allies : eligibleComps;
    const comp: Competitor | undefined = compRng.pick([...preferredPool]);

    if (comp) {
      const compQuality = Math.min(
        10,
        Math.max(2, Math.round((comp.reputation * 0.6 + comp.condition * 0.4) / 10))
      );
      const baseCost = BASE_COST_BY_QUALITY[compQuality] ?? 110;
      const baseWholesale = BASE_WHOLESALE_BY_QUALITY[compQuality] ?? 240;

      let compUnitCost = Math.round(baseCost * factors.priceIndex);
      let relationNote = `Opinia o Tobie: ${Math.round(comp.opinionOfPlayer)}%.`;

      if (comp.isAlliedWithPlayer) {
        compUnitCost = Math.round(compUnitCost * 0.78);
        relationNote = 'Cena sojusznicza (-22%).';
      } else if (friendsOfAllies.has(comp.id)) {
        compUnitCost = Math.round(compUnitCost * 0.88);
        relationNote = 'Zniżka po znajomości przez sojusznika (-12%).';
      } else if (comp.opinionOfPlayer < 40) {
        compUnitCost = Math.round(compUnitCost * (1 + (40 - comp.opinionOfPlayer) * 0.01));
      } else if (comp.opinionOfPlayer > 60) {
        compUnitCost = Math.round(compUnitCost * 0.9);
      }

      if (comp.condition < 35) {
        compUnitCost = Math.round(compUnitCost * 0.8);
      }

      const effectiveWholesalePrice = Math.max(
        10,
        Math.round(baseWholesale * factors.priceIndex * factors.combinedMultiplier)
      );

      list.push({
        id: `comp_ing_${comp.id}`,
        name: `${comp.name}`,
        desc: `Nadwyżki magazynowe składników. ${relationNote}`,
        quality: compQuality,
        unitCost: baseCost,
        baseWholesalePrice: baseWholesale,
        effectiveUnitCost: Math.max(5, compUnitCost),
        effectiveWholesalePrice,
        meetsRequirements: true,
        missingReasons: [],
        isCompetitor: true,
      });
    }
  }

  return list;
}

/** Zwraca katalog narzędzi produkcyjnych (kupowanych na stałe) z cenami i statusem wymagań. */
export function getProductionTools(state: GameState): EvaluatedTool[] {
  const tools = productionData.tools ?? [];
  const ownedIds = getOwnedToolIds(state);
  const currentSizeIdx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(state.company.size);

  return tools.map((tool) => {
    const missingReasons: string[] = [];
    if (tool.req?.minSize) {
      const reqIdx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(tool.req.minSize as CompanySize);
      if (currentSizeIdx < reqIdx) {
        const label = COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[tool.req.minSize as CompanySize]?.label ?? tool.req.minSize;
        missingReasons.push(`Rozmiar firmy: min. ${label}`);
      }
    }
    if (tool.req?.minRepBranza && state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza < tool.req.minRepBranza) {
      missingReasons.push(`Opinia w branży ≥ ${tool.req.minRepBranza}`);
    }

    const owned = ownedIds.includes(tool.id);
    const effectiveCost = tool.cost > 0 ? scaleByInflation(tool.cost, state) : 0;

    return {
      ...tool,
      effectiveCost,
      owned,
      meetsRequirements: missingReasons.length === 0,
      missingReasons,
    };
  });
}

/** Wylicza podgląd partii (liczba sztuk, koszt, jakość i orientacyjna kwota za sztukę). */
export function estimateProductionBatch(
  state: GameState,
  ingredientId: string,
  toolId: string,
  workersCount: number
): ProductionBatchEstimate | null {
  const ingredient =
    getProductionIngredients(state).find(i => i.id === ingredientId) ??
    evaluateAllCatalogIngredients(state).find(i => i.id === ingredientId);
  const tool = getProductionTools(state).find(t => t.id === toolId);
  if (!ingredient || !tool) return null;

  const factors = getProductionMarketFactors(state);
  const safeWorkers = Math.max(1, Math.floor(workersCount));
  const units = safeWorkers * tool.unitsPerWorker;
  const quality = Math.min(10, Math.max(1, ingredient.quality + tool.qualityBonus + getUopQualityBonus(state)));

  const qualityDelta = Math.max(0, quality - ingredient.quality);
  const qualityMultiplier = 1 + qualityDelta * 0.12;
  const estimatedUnitPrice = Math.max(
    10,
    Math.round(ingredient.effectiveWholesalePrice * qualityMultiplier)
  );

  const accuracy = state.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const unitRange = getRevenueRange(estimatedUnitPrice, accuracy);
  const estimatedTotalRevenue = estimatedUnitPrice * units;
  const totalRange = getRevenueRange(estimatedTotalRevenue, accuracy);

  return {
    ingredient,
    tool,
    factors,
    workersCount: safeWorkers,
    units,
    unitCost: ingredient.effectiveUnitCost,
    totalCost: ingredient.effectiveUnitCost * units,
    quality,
    estimatedUnitPrice,
    unitMinPrice: unitRange.min,
    unitMaxPrice: unitRange.max,
    estimatedTotalRevenue,
    totalMinRevenue: totalRange.min,
    totalMaxRevenue: totalRange.max,
    spreadPercent: unitRange.spreadPercent,
  };
}

/** Wycenia gotowy wyrób składowany w magazynie (`prod_*`) do zbytu lub samodzielnego handlu. */
export function estimateStoredProducedItem(
  state: GameState,
  resId: string
): StoredProducedItemEstimate {
  const stock = state.company.resources[resId] ?? 0;
  const parts = resId.split('_');
  const quality = Math.min(10, Math.max(1, parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10) || 1));

  const middleId = parts.slice(1, -1).join('_');
  const allIngredients = evaluateAllCatalogIngredients(state);
  const matchedIng = allIngredients.find(i => i.id === middleId);
  const factors = getProductionMarketFactors(state);

  const prodName = getActivityName(getProductionActivityId(state));

  let ingredientName = matchedIng?.name ?? 'Wyrób gotowy';
  if (!matchedIng && middleId.startsWith('comp_ing_')) {
    const compId = middleId.slice('comp_ing_'.length);
    const comp = (state.competitors ?? []).find(c => c.id === compId);
    ingredientName = comp ? `składniki od ${comp.name}` : 'składniki od konkurencji';
  }

  let estimatedUnitPrice: number;
  if (matchedIng) {
    const qualityDelta = Math.max(0, quality - matchedIng.quality);
    estimatedUnitPrice = Math.max(
      10,
      Math.round(matchedIng.effectiveWholesalePrice * (1 + qualityDelta * 0.12))
    );
  } else {
    const baseWholesale = BASE_WHOLESALE_BY_QUALITY[quality] ?? (quality * 90 + 60);
    estimatedUnitPrice = Math.max(
      10,
      Math.round(baseWholesale * factors.priceIndex * factors.combinedMultiplier)
    );
  }

  const accuracy = state.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const unitRange = getRevenueRange(estimatedUnitPrice, accuracy);
  const retailBaseUnitPrice = Math.max(10, Math.round(estimatedUnitPrice * 0.65));

  return {
    resId,
    label: `${prodName} (${ingredientName})`,
    ingredientName,
    quality,
    stock,
    estimatedUnitPrice,
    unitMinPrice: unitRange.min,
    unitMaxPrice: unitRange.max,
    retailBaseUnitPrice,
  };
}

// --- Eksporty zachowane dla kompatybilności wstecznej ---

export function getProductionCapacity(state: GameState): number {
  const emp = state.company.employees;
  const totalWorkers = emp.bezUmowy + emp.zlecenie + emp.uop;
  return BASE_CEO_CAPACITY + totalWorkers * CAPACITY_PER_WORKER;
}

export function generateProductionSuppliers(state: GameState): TradeSupplier[] {
  const branchData = productionData.default;
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 37 + 19);
  const result: TradeSupplier[] = [];

  const priceIndex = getPriceIndex(state);
  const dev = state.macroStats?.development ?? 40;
  const devFactor = 1 - Math.min(0.15, Math.max(-0.15, (dev - 40) * 0.003));
  const repBranza = state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza;
  const repDiscount = Math.min(0.12, (repBranza / 100) * 0.12);
  const allianceDiscount = getAllianceTradeDiscount(state);
  const totalDiscount = Math.min(0.35, repDiscount + allianceDiscount);

  if (branchData?.suppliers) {
    const valid = branchData.suppliers.filter(s => {
      if (s.req?.minRepBranza && state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza < s.req.minRepBranza) return false;
      if (s.req?.minRepMafia && state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < s.req.minRepMafia) return false;
      return true;
    });

    for (const sup of valid) {
      const calculatedPrice = Math.round(sup.price * priceIndex * devFactor * (1 - totalDiscount));
      result.push({
        id: sup.id,
        name: sup.name,
        desc: sup.desc,
        quality: sup.quality,
        price: Math.max(5, calculatedPrice),
        req: sup.req,
      });
    }
  }

  const eligibleComps = (state.competitors ?? []).filter(
    c => (c.status ?? 'active') === 'active' && !c.atWarWithPlayer
  );
  if (eligibleComps.length > 0) {
    const comp: Competitor = rng.pick([...eligibleComps]);
    if (comp) {
      const compQuality = Math.min(10, Math.max(2, Math.round((comp.reputation * 0.6 + comp.condition * 0.4) / 10)));
      let baseCompPrice = Math.round((compQuality * 110 + 70) * priceIndex);
      if (comp.isAlliedWithPlayer) {
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * 0.78);
      } else if (comp.opinionOfPlayer < 40) {
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * (1 + (40 - comp.opinionOfPlayer) * 0.01));
      } else if (comp.opinionOfPlayer > 60) {
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * 0.9);
      }
      if (comp.condition < 35) {
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * 0.8);
      }

      result.push({
        id: `comp_${comp.id}`,
        name: `${comp.name} (Surowce / Konkurent)`,
        desc: `Nadwyżki magazynowe półproduktów. Opinia o Tobie: ${Math.round(comp.opinionOfPlayer)}%.`,
        quality: compQuality,
        price: Math.max(5, baseCompPrice),
      });
    }
  }

  return result.sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, 3);
}

export function generateProductionBuyers(state: GameState): TradeBuyer[] {
  return generateTradeBuyers(state);
}

import type { GameState, Reputation, CompanySize } from './types';
import { REPUTATION_WEIGHTS, MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD, PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS } from './constants';

const clamp = (x: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(100, x));

const SIZE_ACCURACY_BONUS: Record<CompanySize, number> = {
  micro: 0,
  small: 3,
  medium: 6,
  large: 10,
  very_large: 14,
  corporation: 18,
};

export function politicianWeight(state: GameState): number {
  const ourParty = state.ceo.currentPartyId;
  if (!ourParty) return 0.2;
  const inCoalition = state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(ourParty);
  if (inCoalition) {
    const isPremier = state.politics.premierId === ourParty;
    const isPresident = state.politics.president?.partyId === ourParty;
    if (isPremier && isPresident) return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.bothPresidentAndPremier;
    if (isPresident) return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.onlyPresident;
    if (isPremier) return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.onlyPremier;
  }
  return PARTY_INFLUENCE_WEIGHTS.opposition;
}

export function recomputeReputation(state: GameState): Reputation {
  const r = { ...state.company.reputation };

  r.branza = clamp(r.branza);
  r.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(r.opiniaPubliczna);
  r.opiniaPracownicy = clamp(r.opiniaPracownicy);
  r.opiniaBranza = clamp(r.opiniaBranza);
  r.opiniaMafia = clamp(r.opiniaMafia);
  r.opiniaMedia = clamp(r.opiniaMedia);
  r.opiniaPolitycy = clamp(r.opiniaPolitycy);
  r.opiniaSkarbowa = clamp(r.opiniaSkarbowa);
  r.bazowaDokladnosc = clamp(r.bazowaDokladnosc ?? 50);

  const mediaInf = (r.opiniaMedia - 50) * 0.1;
  const polWeight = politicianWeight(state);
  const polInf = (r.opiniaPolitycy - 50) * polWeight * 0.15;

  r.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(r.opiniaPubliczna + mediaInf + polInf);
  r.opiniaBranza    = clamp(r.opiniaBranza    + mediaInf * 0.5 + polInf * 0.5);
  r.opiniaMafia     = clamp(r.opiniaMafia     + mediaInf * 0.3 + polInf * 0.3);

  if (r.opiniaMafia > MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD) {
    const overshoot = r.opiniaMafia - MAFIA_PUBLIC_THRESHOLD;
    r.opiniaPubliczna = clamp(r.opiniaPubliczna - overshoot * 0.3);
  }

  // Dynamiczne wyliczanie Dokładności Przewidywań (baza + pracownicy + księgowa + rozmiar + branża + biuro + cynk rynkowy od sojuszników)
  const emp = state.company.employees;
  const uopBonus = Math.min(15, emp.uop * 1.5);
  const zlecenieBonus = Math.min(6, emp.zlecenie * 0.5);
  const bezUmowyPenalty = Math.min(15, emp.bezUmowy * 1.5);
  const accountantBonus = state.company.hasAccountant ? 15 : 0;
  const sizeBonus = SIZE_ACCURACY_BONUS[state.company.size] ?? 0;
  const branzaMod = (r.opiniaBranza - 50) * 0.25;
  const inventoryBonus = Math.min(10, (state.company.inventory?.length ?? 0) * 2);

  // Cynk rynkowy: im wyższa reputacja konkretnego sojusznika, tym dokładniejsze informacje (0..6 pkt na sojusznika, max 18)
  const activeAllies = (state.competitors ?? []).filter(
    (c) => (c.status ?? 'active') === 'active' && c.isAlliedWithPlayer
  );
  const allyIntelBonus = Math.min(
    18,
    activeAllies.reduce((sum, ally) => sum + Math.round((clamp(ally.reputation) / 100) * 6), 0)
  );

  r.dokladnoscPrzewidywan = clamp(
    Math.round(
      r.bazowaDokladnosc +
      uopBonus +
      zlecenieBonus -
      bezUmowyPenalty +
      accountantBonus +
      sizeBonus +
      branzaMod +
      inventoryBonus +
      allyIntelBonus
    )
  );

  let sum = 0;
  for (const [key, weight] of Object.entries(REPUTATION_WEIGHTS)) {
    sum += (r as any)[key] * weight;
  }
  r.ogolna = clamp(sum);

  return r;
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\engine\rng.ts ===
import seedrandom from 'seedrandom';

export function makeRng(seed: string, salt: number) {
  const rng = seedrandom(`${seed}:${salt}`);
  return {
    next: () => rng(),
    int: (min: number, max: number) => Math.floor(rng() * (max - min + 1)) + min,
    pick: <T>(arr: T[]) => arr[Math.floor(rng() * arr.length)],
    weighted: <T extends { weight: number }>(arr: T[]) => {
      const total = arr.reduce((s, x) => s + x.weight, 0);
      let r = rng() * total;
      for (const item of arr) {
        r -= item.weight;
        if (r <= 0) return item;
      }
      return arr[arr.length - 1];
    },
  };
}

import type { GameState, ServiceDefinition, CompanySize } from './types';
import { getCompanyActivities } from './activityHelper';
import { getRevenueRange } from './tradeHelper';
import { getPriceIndex } from './economy';
import servicesData from '../data/services_data.json';

const BASE_CEO_SLOTS = 1;

const SIZE_ORDER: CompanySize[] = [
  'micro',
  'small',
  'medium',
  'large',
  'very_large',
  'corporation',
];

const SIZE_LABELS: Record<CompanySize, string> = {
  micro: 'Mikro',
  small: 'Mała',
  medium: 'Średnia',
  large: 'Duża',
  very_large: 'Bardzo duża',
  corporation: 'Korporacja',
};

export interface EvaluatedService extends ServiceDefinition {
  calculatedPayout: number;
  minPayout: number;
  maxPayout: number;
  isActive: boolean;
  meetsRequirements: boolean;
  missingReasons: string[];
}

/** Łączna liczba pracowników w firmie (bez prezesa). */
export function getTotalEmployeeCount(state: GameState): number {
  const emp = state.company.employees;
  return emp.bezUmowy + emp.zlecenie + emp.uop;
}

/** Łączna liczba slotów kadrowych na usługi (1 za prezesa + 1 za każdego pracownika). */
export function getTotalServiceWorkerSlots(state: GameState): number {
  return BASE_CEO_SLOTS + getTotalEmployeeCount(state);
}

/** Liczba pracowników aktualnie zaangażowanych w uruchomione na stałe usługi. */
export function getBusyServiceWorkers(state: GameState): number {
  const activeIds = state.activeServiceIds ?? [];
  const allServices = servicesData as ServiceDefinition[];
  return activeIds.reduce((sum, id) => {
    const srv = allServices.find(s => s.id === id);
    return sum + (srv ? srv.workersRequired : 0);
  }, 0);
}

/** Sprawdza, czy firma spełnia wymagania dla danej usługi (rozmiar, pracownicy, reputacja). */
export function checkServiceRequirements(
  state: GameState,
  service: ServiceDefinition
): { meets: boolean; reasons: string[] } {
  const reasons: string[] = [];
  const req = service.req;

  if (req) {
    if (req.minSize) {
      const currentIdx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(state.company.size);
      const reqIdx = SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(req.minSize);
      if (currentIdx < reqIdx) {
        reasons.push(`Wielkość firmy: min. ${SIZE_LABELS[req.minSize]}`);
      }
    }
    if (req.minWorkers !== undefined) {
      const totalEmp = getTotalEmployeeCount(state);
      if (totalEmp < req.minWorkers) {
        reasons.push(`Pracownicy: min. ${req.minWorkers} os.`);
      }
    }
    if (req.minRepPubliczna !== undefined && state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna < req.minRepPubliczna) {
      reasons.push(`Opinia publiczna: min. ${req.minRepPubliczna}`);
    }
    if (req.minRepBranza !== undefined && state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza < req.minRepBranza) {
      reasons.push(`Opinia w branży: min. ${req.minRepBranza}`);
    }
    if (req.minRepMafia !== undefined && state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < req.minRepMafia) {
      reasons.push(`Wpływy w mafii: min. ${req.minRepMafia}`);
    }
  }

  return {
    meets: reasons.length === 0,
    reasons,
  };
}

/** Zwraca pełny katalog usług dla podkategorii gracza wraz ze statusem odblokowania i widełkami przychodu. */
export function getAvailableServices(state: GameState): EvaluatedService[] {
  const allServices = servicesData as ServiceDefinition[];
  const companyActs = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  const serviceActs = companyActs.filter(id => id.startsWith('usl_') || id.startsWith('gast_'));
  const targetActs = serviceActs.length > 0 ? serviceActs : companyActs;

  const priceIndex = getPriceIndex(state);
  const dev = state.macroStats?.development ?? 40;
  const devMultiplier = 1 + Math.max(-0.15, (dev - 40) * 0.004);
  const repMultiplier = 1 + Math.max(-0.1, (state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza - 50) * 0.003);
  const accuracy = state.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const activeIds = state.activeServiceIds ?? [];

  return allServices
    .filter(srv => {
      const acts = srv.activities ?? ['*'];
      return acts.includes('*') || acts.some(a => targetActs.includes(a));
    })
    .map(srv => {
      const base = srv.basePayout ?? (srv as any).payout ?? 10000;
      const calculatedPayout = Math.round(base * priceIndex * devMultiplier * repMultiplier);
      const range = getRevenueRange(calculatedPayout, accuracy);
      const { meets, reasons } = checkServiceRequirements(state, srv);

      return {
        ...srv,
        basePayout: base,
        calculatedPayout,
        minPayout: range.min,
        maxPayout: range.max,
        isActive: activeIds.includes(srv.id),
        meetsRequirements: meets,
        missingReasons: reasons,
      };
    });
}

import type { GameState, TradeSupplier, TradeBuyer, Competitor } from './types';
import { makeRng } from './rng';
import { getCategoryId } from './competition';
import { getCompanyActivities } from './activityHelper';
import { getPriceIndex } from './economy';
import tradeData from '../data/trade_data.json';
import productionData from '../data/production_data.json';

interface TradeDataJson {
  suppliers: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    quality: number;
    price: number;
    desc: string;
    req?: { minRepBranza?: number; minRepMafia?: number; minRepPubliczna?: number };
  }>;
  buyers: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    multiplier: number;
    desc: string;
    req?: { minRepPubliczna?: number; minQuality?: number };
    complaints: boolean;
  }>;
}

/**
 * Wylicza przedział orientacyjnego przychodu [min, max] na podstawie Dokładności Przewidywań (0-100%).
 * Przy 0% rozstrzał wynosi ±45%, a przy 100% maleje do ±5%.
 */
export function getRevenueRange(
  estimatedRevenue: number,
  accuracy: number
): { min: number; max: number; spreadPercent: number } {
  const clampedAcc = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, accuracy ?? 50));
  const spread = 0.45 - (clampedAcc / 100) * 0.40;
  const min = Math.max(1, Math.round(estimatedRevenue * (1 - spread)));
  const max = Math.max(min, Math.round(estimatedRevenue * (1 + spread)));
  return {
    min,
    max,
    spreadPercent: Math.round(spread * 100),
  };
}

/** Zwraca podkategorię towarową gracza (Handel lub Produkcja + Handel). */
export function getGoodsActivityId(state: GameState): string {
  const acts = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  return acts.find(id => id.startsWith('handel_') || id.startsWith('prod_')) || state.company.activity;
}

export function generateTradeSuppliers(state: GameState): TradeSupplier[] {
  const goodsAct = getGoodsActivityId(state);
  const categoryId = getCategoryId(goodsAct) || '';
  const tradeRecord = tradeData as Record<string, TradeDataJson>;
  const branchData = tradeRecord[goodsAct] || tradeRecord[categoryId] || tradeRecord.odziez;

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 31 + 44);
  const result: TradeSupplier[] = [];

  const priceIndex = getPriceIndex(state);
  const dev = state.macroStats?.development ?? 40;
  const devFactor = 1 - Math.min(0.15, Math.max(-0.15, (dev - 40) * 0.003));
  const repBranza = state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza;
  const repDiscount = Math.min(0.12, (repBranza / 100) * 0.12);

  if (branchData?.suppliers) {
    const valid = branchData.suppliers.filter((s) => {
      if (s.req?.minRepBranza && state.company.reputation.opiniaBranza < s.req.minRepBranza) return false;
      if (s.req?.minRepMafia && state.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < s.req.minRepMafia) return false;
      return true;
    });

    for (const sup of valid) {
      const calculatedPrice = Math.round(sup.price * priceIndex * devFactor * (1 - repDiscount));
      result.push({
        id: sup.id,
        name: sup.name,
        desc: sup.desc,
        quality: sup.quality,
        price: Math.max(5, calculatedPrice),
        req: sup.req,
      });
    }
  }

  if (state.competitors && state.competitors.length > 0) {
    const compPool = [...state.competitors];
    const comp: Competitor = rng.pick(compPool);

    if (comp) {
      const compQuality = Math.min(10, Math.max(2, Math.round((comp.reputation * 0.6 + comp.condition * 0.4) / 10)));
      let baseCompPrice = Math.round((compQuality * 150 + 100) * priceIndex);

      if (comp.opinionOfPlayer < 40) {
        const spiteNarzut = 1 + (40 - comp.opinionOfPlayer) * 0.01;
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * spiteNarzut);
      } else if (comp.opinionOfPlayer > 60) {
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * 0.9);
      }

      if (comp.condition < 35) {
        baseCompPrice = Math.round(baseCompPrice * 0.8);
      }

      result.push({
        id: `comp_${comp.id}`,
        name: `${comp.name} (Konkurent)`,
        desc: comp.condition < 35
          ? `Wyprzedaż zapasów po kosztach z powodu kłopotów finansowych. Opinia o Tobie: ${comp.opinionOfPlayer}%.`
          : `Oferta hurtowa od konkurencyjnej spółki. Opinia o Tobie: ${comp.opinionOfPlayer}%.`,
        quality: compQuality,
        price: Math.max(5, baseCompPrice),
      });
    }
  }

  return result.sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, 3);
}

/**
 * Wspólna lista odbiorców dla Handlu oraz Produkcji + Handlu.
 */
export function generateTradeBuyers(state: GameState): TradeBuyer[] {
  const goodsAct = getGoodsActivityId(state);
  const categoryId = getCategoryId(goodsAct) || '';
  const tradeRecord = tradeData as Record<string, TradeDataJson>;
  const prodRecord = productionData as unknown as Record<string, TradeDataJson>;

  const branchData = goodsAct.startsWith('prod_')
    ? (prodRecord[goodsAct] || tradeRecord[goodsAct] || prodRecord.default || tradeRecord.odziez)
    : (tradeRecord[goodsAct] || tradeRecord[categoryId] || tradeRecord.odziez);

  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 43 + 71);
  const result: TradeBuyer[] = [];

  const dev = state.macroStats?.development ?? 40;
  const devBonus = Math.max(-0.1, (dev - 40) * 0.0025);

  if (branchData?.buyers) {
    const valid = branchData.buyers.filter((b) => {
      if (b.req?.minRepPubliczna && state.company.reputation.opiniaPubliczna < b.req.minRepPubliczna) return false;
      return true;
    });

    for (const buy of valid) {
      let minQuality = buy.req?.minQuality ?? 1;
      if (buy.multiplier >= 3.0) minQuality = Math.max(minQuality, 6);
      else if (buy.multiplier >= 2.2) minQuality = Math.max(minQuality, 3);

      const calculatedMult = Math.round((buy.multiplier + devBonus) * 100) / 100;

      result.push({
        id: buy.id,
        name: buy.name,
        desc: buy.desc,
        multiplier: Math.max(1.1, calculatedMult),
        complaints: buy.complaints,
        req: {
          minRepPubliczna: buy.req?.minRepPubliczna,
          minQuality,
        },
      });
    }
  }

  if (state.competitors && state.competitors.length > 0) {
    const compPool = [...state.competitors];
    const comp: Competitor = rng.pick(compPool);

    if (comp) {
      let compMinQuality = 1;
      if (comp.reputation >= 75) compMinQuality = 7;
      else if (comp.reputation >= 50) compMinQuality = 4;

      let compMult = 1.8 + (comp.condition / 100) * 1.2;
      if (comp.opinionOfPlayer < 35) {
        compMult *= 0.85;
      } else if (comp.opinionOfPlayer > 65) {
        compMult *= 1.15;
      }
      compMult = Math.round(compMult * 100) / 100;

      result.push({
        id: `comp_${comp.id}`,
        name: `${comp.name} (Skup / Konkurent)`,
        desc: comp.reputation >= 60
          ? `Prestiżowa spółka konkurencyjna. Wymaga wysokiej jakości towaru. Opinia: ${comp.opinionOfPlayer}%.`
          : `Konkurent szukający okazji hurtowych. Kondycja: ${comp.condition}%.`,
        multiplier: Math.max(1.2, compMult),
        complaints: comp.reputation > 40,
        req: {
          minQuality: compMinQuality,
        },
      });
    }
  }

  return result.sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5).slice(0, 3);
}

import type { GameState, EventEffects, Activity, ChoiceResult } from './types';

import { tickCompetitors, snapshotPlayer, INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN } from './competition';

const INSTANT_BANKRUPTCY_LIMIT = -100_000;
const ACTIVITIES_PER_TURN = 3;
const CHASE_MAFIA_THRESHOLD = 25;
const CHASE_CHANCE = 0.3;

const REPUTATION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  branza: 'Branża',
  opiniaPubliczna: 'Opinia publiczna',
  opiniaPracownicy: 'Opinia pracowników',
  opiniaBranza: 'Opinia w branży',
  opiniaMafia: 'Opinia wśród mafii',
  opiniaMedia: 'Opinia w mediach',
  opiniaPolitycy: 'Opinia wśród polityków',
  opiniaSkarbowa: 'Opinia skarbówki',
  ogolna: 'Reputacja ogólna',
};

const CONTRACT_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  bezUmowy: 'bez umowy',
  zlecenie: 'zlecenie',
  uop: 'umowa o pracę',
};

const signed = (v: number) => `${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v.toLocaleString('pl-PL')}`;

function describeEffects(e: EventEffects): string[] {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  if (e.stanKonta) lines.push(`Stan konta: ${signed(e.stanKonta)} PLN`);
  if (e.reputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.reputation)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`${REPUTATION_LABELS[k] ?? k}: ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.wydatki) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wydatki)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`Wydatki (${k}): ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.wplywy) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.wplywy)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`Wpływy (${k}): ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.employeesDelta) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.employeesDelta)) {
      if (!v) continue;
      lines.push(`Pracownicy (${CONTRACT_LABELS[k] ?? k}): ${signed(v)}`);
    }
  }
  if (e.divideFinances) lines.push('Podział finansów');
  if (e.addItem) lines.push(`Nowy przedmiot: ${e.addItem}`);
  if (e.setParty) lines.push(`Zmiana strony politycznej: ${e.setParty}`);
  if (e.joinMafia) lines.push('Dołączasz do mafii');
  if (e.leaveMafia) lines.push('Opuszczasz mafię');
  if (e.setCurrentPartyId) lines.push(`Wstępujesz do partii: ${e.setCurrentPartyId}`);
  if (e.flagsAdd?.length) lines.push(`Nowe znaczniki: ${e.flagsAdd.join(', ')}`);
  if (e.flagsRemove?.length) lines.push(`Usunięte znaczniki: ${e.flagsRemove.join(', ')}`);
  if (lines.length === 0) lines.push('Brak widocznych skutków.');
  return lines;
}

export function nextTurn(state: GameState): GameState {
  if (state.gameOver) return state;

  let s = structuredClone(state);
  const rng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn);
  const quarters = 1;
  s.pace = 'quarter';
  const oldPhase = s.phase;

  // Zdjęcie statystyk gracza przed nową turą (do wyliczania trendów w rankingu)
  s.playerPrev = snapshotPlayer(s);

  // 1. AKTUALIZACJA CZASU (przed ekonomią i eventami!)
  s.turn += 1;
  const totalQuarters = s.year * 4 + (s.quarter - 1) + quarters;
  s.year = Math.floor(totalQuarters / 4);
  s.quarter = ((totalQuarters % 4) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
  s.phase = s.year < 2005 ? 'chaos' : s.year < 2030 ? 'duopol' : 'invasion';

  if (s.phase !== oldPhase) {
    s.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[s.phase] = 0;
  }

  // 1b. WCIELENIE ZAKONTRAKTOWANYCH PRACOWNIKÓW Z POPRZEDNIEJ TURY
  if (Array.isArray(s.pendingHires) && s.pendingHires.length > 0) {
    const joinedByContract: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const hire of s.pendingHires) {
      s.company.employees[hire.contract] = (s.company.employees[hire.contract] ?? 0) + hire.count;
      joinedByContract[hire.contract] = (joinedByContract[hire.contract] ?? 0) + hire.count;
    }
    s.pendingHires = [];

    const summaryParts = Object.entries(joinedByContract).map(
      ([c, n]) => `+${n} (${CONTRACT_LABELS[c] ?? c})`
    );
    if (summaryParts.length > 0) {
      s.news.unshift({
        id: `hires_joined_${s.turn}_${s.news.length}`,
        turn: s.turn,
        year: s.year,
        quarter: s.quarter,
        headline: 'Nowi pracownicy rozpoczęli pracę',
        body: `Do załogi dołączyli zakontraktowani wcześniej pracownicy: ${summaryParts.join(', ')}. Są już dostępni do pracy i produkcji.`,
        category: 'economy',
        read: false,
      });
    }
  }

  // 2. EKONOMIA, ROZLICZENIA I KONKURENCJA
  resolvePendingSales(s);
  resolvePendingServices(s);
  s = tickEconomy(s);
  s = tickCompetitors(s, 0);

  // 3. POLITYKA I EVENTY
  s = tickPolitics(s);
  s.pendingEvents = rollEvents(s);
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  if (s.bankruptWarningTurns >= BANKRUPT_GRACE_TURNS && s.company.finances.stanKonta < 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Bankructwo — nie spłaciłeś długów' };
  }
  if (s.company.reputation.ogolna <= 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Utrata reputacji' };
  }
  if (s.gameOver) return s;

  // 4. AKTYWNOŚCI, REJESTR RUNDY I POWIADOMIENIA
  s.currentActivities = rollActivities(s, rng);
  s.chosenActivityThisTurn = null;
  s.productionWorkersUsedThisTurn = 0;
  s.productionUsedThisTurn = 0;
  s.turnActions = [];
  s.undoStack = [];
  s.lastTradeNotice = null;
  s.lastChoiceResult = null;
  s.interactionPoints = INTERACTIONS_PER_TURN;

  generatePassiveNews(s);
  checkMafiaInvitation(s);
  checkTaxOfficeNotice(s);
  processExpiredActions(s);
  checkEmployeeUnrest(s);

  if (s.gameOver) return s;

  if (s.skarbowkaVisitTurn !== null && s.turn === s.skarbowkaVisitTurn && !s.activeCombat) {
    startTaxCombat(s);
  }

  if (!s.activeChase && !s.activeCombat && s.ceo.inMafia === false && s.company.reputation.opiniaMafia < CHASE_MAFIA_THRESHOLD) {
    const chaseRng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn * 99 + 1);
    if (chaseRng.next() < CHASE_CHANCE) {
      startChase(s);
    }
  }

  return s;
}

function rollActivities(state: GameState, rng: ReturnType<typeof makeRng>): string[] {
  const pool = (activitiesData as Activity[])
    .filter((a) => matchesConditions(state, a.conditions))
    .map((a) => ({ id: a.id, weight: a.weight > 0 ? a.weight : 1 }));

  const picked: string[] = [];
  while (picked.length < ACTIVITIES_PER_TURN && pool.length > 0) {
    const item = rng.weighted(pool);
    picked.push(item.id);
    pool.splice(pool.findIndex((p) => p.id === item.id), 1);
  }
  return picked;
}

export function consumeActivity(state: GameState, activityId: string): GameState {
  if (state.gameOver) return state;
  if (state.chosenActivityThisTurn) return state;
  if (!state.currentActivities.includes(activityId)) return state;

  const activity = (activitiesData as Activity[]).find((a) => a.id === activityId);
  if (!activity) return state;

  const s = captureUndoSnapshot(state);
  applyEffects(s, activity.effects);
  s.chosenActivityThisTurn = activityId;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  recordTurnAction(s, {
    icon: '⚡',
    category: 'Aktywność',
    text: `Wykonano aktywność kwartalną: ${activity.label}.`,
    target: 'activity',
    canUndo: false,
  });

  if (s.company.reputation.ogolna <= 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Utrata reputacji' };
  }
  return s;
}

export function resolveEventChoice(state: GameState, eventId: string, choiceId: string): GameState {
  const ev = state.pendingEvents.find((e) => e.id === eventId);
  const choice = ev?.choices.find((c) => c.id === choiceId);
  if (!ev || !choice) return state;

  const s = structuredClone(state);
  const rng = makeRng(s.seed, s.turn * 31 + s.history.length);

  let gambleResult: 'win' | 'lose' | undefined;
  let effects: EventEffects | undefined;
  let outcomeDescription: string | undefined;

  if (choice.outcomes && choice.outcomes.length > 0) {
    const picked = rng.weighted(choice.outcomes);
    effects = picked.effects;
    outcomeDescription = picked.description;
  } else if (choice.gamble) {
    if (rng.next() < choice.gamble.winChance) {
      effects = choice.gamble.onWin;
      gambleResult = 'win';
      outcomeDescription = choice.gamble.winText;
    } else {
      effects = choice.gamble.onLose;
      gambleResult = 'lose';
      outcomeDescription = choice.gamble.loseText;
    }
  } else {
    effects = choice.effects;
  }
  if (effects) applyEffects(s, effects);

  const result: ChoiceResult = {
    eventId: ev.id,
    eventTitle: ev.title,
    choiceId: choice.id,
    choiceLabel: choice.label,
    consequenceLines: effects ? describeEffects(effects) : ['Brak efektów.'],
    gambleResult,
    outcomeDescription,
  };

  s.pendingEvents = [];
  s.history.push(eventId);
  s.lastChoiceResult = result;
  s.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(s);

  if (s.company.finances.stanKonta < INSTANT_BANKRUPTCY_LIMIT) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Bankructwo — nie spłaciłeś długów' };
  }
  if (s.company.reputation.ogolna <= 0) {
    s.gameOver = { reason: 'Utrata reputacji' };
  }

  return s;
}

export function dismissConsequence(state: GameState): GameState {
  const s = structuredClone(state);
  s.lastChoiceResult = null;
  return s;
}

export interface Competitor {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  /** Wylosowane motto z puli 3 haseł szablonu */
  motto: string;
  /** Charakter firmy wpływający na wojny, zdrady i werdykty */
  character: CompetitorCharacter;
  /** Branże firmy (np. ['handel'], ['produkcja', 'uslugi']) */
  categories: string[];
  /** Status rynkowy firmy */
  status: CompetitorStatus;
  size: CompanySize;
  reputation: number;
  value: number;
  condition: number;
  opinionOfPlayer: number;
  baseReputation: number;
  baseCondition: number;
  baseOpinion: number;
  trend: CompetitorTrend;
  /** Zgody z innymi firmami AI (ID firm) */
  allies: string[];
  /** Kosy z innymi firmami AI (ID firm) */
  rivals: string[];
  /** Formalny sojusz z graczem */
  isAlliedWithPlayer: boolean;
  /** Aktywna wojna z graczem */
  atWarWithPlayer: boolean;
  /** Czy firma jest uzależniona od gracza (lub innej firmy AI) */
  dependentOn?: 'player' | string | null;

import type { StateCreator } from 'zustand';
import { movePlayer, dismissChase as dismissChaseEngine } from '../../engine/chase';
import { autosave } from '../autosave';

export interface ChaseSlice {
  chaseMove: (dx: number, dy: number) => void;
  dismissChase: () => void;
}

export const createChaseSlice: StateCreator<any, [], [], ChaseSlice> = (set, get) => ({
  chaseMove: (dx, dy) => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s || !s.activeChase) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    movePlayer(next, dx, dy);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
  },
  dismissChase: () => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    dismissChaseEngine(next);
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
  },
});

import type {
  ContractType,
