import {
  nextTurn,
  consumeActivity as consumeActivityEngine,
  resolveEventChoice,
  dismissConsequence,
} from '../../engine/turn';
import {
  undoLastAction as undoLastActionEngine,
  applyHire,
  applyLoan,
  applyChangeActivity,
  applyJoinParty,
  applyLeaveParty,
  applyEmergencyLoan,
  applySetPace,
  buyOfficeItem as buyOfficeItemEngine,
  toggleAccountant,
  changeCompanySize as changeCompanySizeEngine,
  buildBranch as buildBranchEngine,
  buyAd as buyAdEngine,

export interface GameSlice {
  doTurn: () => void;
  undoLastAction: () => void;
  consumeActivity: (activityId: string) => void;
  resolveEvent: (eventId: string, choiceId: string) => void;
  dismissConsequence: () => void;
  dismissIntro: () => void;
  joinParty: (partyId: string) => void;
  leaveParty: () => void;
  changeActivity: (activity: string | string[]) => void;
  takeEmergencyLoan: () => void;
  hireWorker: (contract: ContractType, delta: number) => void;
  takeLoan: (source: 'bank' | 'szara_strefa', amount: number) => void;
  setPace: (pace: Pace) => void;
  buyOfficeItem: (itemId: string, price: number) => void;
  toggleAccountant: () => void;
  dismissElection: () => void;

  sellGoods: (
    buyer: TradeBuyer,
    resId: string,
    amount: number,
    unitPrice: number,
    installments?: InstallmentOption
  ) => void;
  resolveComplaint: (id: string, accept: boolean) => void;
  // --- Konkurencja, sojusze, wojny i przejęcia ---
  interactCompetitor: (compId: string, type: 'positive' | 'negative') => void;
  proposeAlliance: (compId: string) => void;
  breakAlliance: (compId: string) => void;
  takeAllyLoan: (compId: string, amount: number) => void;
  declareWar: (compId: string) => void;
  performWarAction: (compId: string, warActionId: string) => void;
  surrenderWar: (compId: string) => void;
  resolvePlayerWarVictory: (
    warId: string,
    demands: WarDemandType[],
    acquisitionMode?: AcquiredMode
  ) => void;
  acquireCompetitor: (compId: string, postMode: AcquiredMode) => void;
  buyBackEquity: (holderId: string, percent: number) => void;
  requestParentBailout: () => void;
  // --- Produkcja ---
  buyProductionTool: (toolId: string) => void;
  runProductionBatch: (
    ingredientId: string,
    toolId: string,
    workersCount: number,
    sellNowAmount?: number
  ) => void;

  acceptServiceOrder: (order: ServiceOrder) => void;
  // --- Debug ---
  debugSendTestMessage: () => void;
  debugStartTaxCombat: () => void;
  debugStartChase: () => void;
}

interface CommitOptions {
  allowAfterGameOver?: boolean;
}

export const createGameSlice: StateCreator<any, [], [], GameSlice> = (set, get) => {
  const commit = (fn: (s: GameState) => GameState, opts: CommitOptions = {}) => {
    const s: GameState | null = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    if (s.gameOver && !opts.allowAfterGameOver) return;

    const next = fn(s);
    if (next === s) return;

    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    try {
      autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[autosave] zapis nie powiódł się:', err);
    }
  };

  return {
    // --- Tura i Okna ---
    doTurn: () => commit(nextTurn),
    undoLastAction: () => commit(undoLastActionEngine),
    consumeActivity: (id) => commit((s) => consumeActivityEngine(s, id)),
    resolveEvent: (eventId, choiceId) => commit((s) => resolveEventChoice(s, eventId, choiceId)),
    dismissConsequence: () => commit(dismissConsequence, { allowAfterGameOver: true }),
    dismissIntro: () => commit((s) => ({ ...s, showIntro: false }), { allowAfterGameOver: true }),

    // --- Polityka ---
    joinParty: (partyId) => commit((s) => applyJoinParty(s, partyId)),
    leaveParty: () => commit(applyLeaveParty),
    dismissElection: () =>
      commit((s) => ({ ...s, lastElectionResult: null }), { allowAfterGameOver: true }),
    dismissPresidentialResult: () =>
      commit((s) => ({ ...s, lastPresidentialResult: null }), { allowAfterGameOver: true }),

    // --- Firma ---
    changeActivity: (activity) => commit((s) => applyChangeActivity(s, activity)),
    hireWorker: (contract, delta) => commit((s) => applyHire(s, contract, delta)),
    setPace: () => commit((s) => applySetPace(s, 'quarter')),
    buyOfficeItem: (itemId, price) => commit((s) => buyOfficeItemEngine(s, itemId, price)),
    toggleAccountant: () => commit(toggleAccountant),
    changeCompanySize: (direction) => commit((s) => changeCompanySizeEngine(s, direction)),
    buildBranch: () => commit(buildBranchEngine),
    buyAd: (type) => commit((s) => buyAdEngine(s, type)),
    rentWarehouse: () => commit(rentWarehouseEngine),

    // --- Finanse ---
    takeEmergencyLoan: () => commit(applyEmergencyLoan),
    takeLoan: (source, amount) => commit((s) => applyLoan(s, source, amount)),

    // --- Handel ---
    buyGoods: (supplier, amount, installments = 1) =>
      commit((s) => buyGoodsEngine(s, supplier, amount, installments)),
    sellGoods: (buyer, resId, amount, unitPrice, installments = 1) =>
      commit((s) => sellGoodsEngine(s, buyer, resId, amount, unitPrice, installments)),
    resolveComplaint: (id, accept) => commit((s) => resolveComplaintEngine(s, id, accept)),

    // --- Konkurencja, sojusze, wojny i przejęcia ---
    interactCompetitor: (compId, type) =>
      commit((s) => interactWithCompetitorEngine(s, compId, type)),
    proposeAlliance: (compId) => commit((s) => proposeAllianceEngine(s, compId)),
    breakAlliance: (compId) => commit((s) => breakAllianceEngine(s, compId)),
    takeAllyLoan: (compId, amount) => commit((s) => takeAllyLoanEngine(s, compId, amount)),
    declareWar: (compId) => commit((s) => declareWarEngine(s, compId)),
    performWarAction: (compId, warActionId) =>
      commit((s) => performWarActionEngine(s, compId, warActionId)),
    surrenderWar: (compId) => commit((s) => surrenderWarEngine(s, compId)),
    resolvePlayerWarVictory: (warId, demands, acquisitionMode = 'subsidiary') =>
      commit((s) => resolvePlayerWarVictoryEngine(s, warId, demands, acquisitionMode)),
    acquireCompetitor: (compId, postMode) =>
      commit((s) => acquireCompetitorEngine(s, compId, postMode)),
    buyBackEquity: (holderId, percent) =>
      commit((s) => buyBackEquityEngine(s, holderId, percent)),
    requestParentBailout: () => commit(requestParentBailoutEngine),

    // --- Produkcja ---
    buyProductionTool: (toolId) => commit((s) => buyProductionToolEngine(s, toolId)),
    runProductionBatch: (ingredientId, toolId, workersCount, sellNowAmount = 0) =>
      commit((s) =>
        runProductionBatchEngine(s, ingredientId, toolId, workersCount, sellNowAmount)
      ),
    sellProducedStock: (resId, amount) =>
      commit((s) => sellProducedStockEngine(s, resId, amount)),
    buyRawMaterials: (supplier, amount, installments = 1) =>
      commit((s) => buyRawMaterialsEngine(s, supplier, amount, installments)),
    produceGoods: (rawResId, amount) =>
      commit((s) => produceGoodsEngine(s, rawResId, amount)),

    // --- Usługi ---
    toggleService: (serviceId) => commit((s) => toggleServiceEngine(s, serviceId)),
    acceptServiceOrder: (order) => commit((s) => acceptServiceOrderEngine(s, order)),

    // --- Tryb debugowania ---
    debugSendTestMessage: () =>
      commit((s) => {
        const next = structuredClone(s);
        const msg: NewsItem = {
          id: `debug_msg_${next.turn}_${Date.now()}`,
          turn: next.turn,
          year: next.year,
          quarter: next.quarter,
          headline: '[DEBUG] Testowa wiadomość',
          body: `To jest testowa wiadomość wygenerowana w trybie debugowania (${next.year} Q${next.quarter}, tura ${next.turn}).`,
          category: 'economy',
          read: false,
        };
        next.news.unshift(msg);
        if (next.news.length > MAX_NEWS) next.news.pop();
        return next;
      }),

    debugStartTaxCombat: () =>
      commit((s) => {
        const next = structuredClone(s);
        startTaxCombat(next);
        return next;
      }),

    debugStartChase: () =>
      commit((s) => {
        const next = structuredClone(s);
        startChase(next);
        return next;
      }),
  };
};

import type { StateCreator } from 'zustand';
import { applyEffects } from '../../engine/actions';
import { recomputeReputation } from '../../engine/reputation';
import { MAX_NEWS } from '../../engine/constants';
import { autosave } from '../autosave';
import type { NewsItem, NewsChoice } from '../../engine/types';

export interface NewsSlice {
  markNewsRead: (id: string) => void;
  resolveNewsChoice: (newsId: string, choiceId: string) => void;
  devAddTestNews: () => void;
}

export const createNewsSlice: StateCreator<any, [], [], NewsSlice> = (set, get) => ({
  markNewsRead: (id) => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = {
      ...s,
      news: s.news.map((n: NewsItem) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n)),
    };
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
  },
  resolveNewsChoice: (newsId, choiceId) => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    const item = next.news.find((n: NewsItem) => n.id === newsId);
    if (!item || item.resolved || !item.choices) return;
    const choice = item.choices.find((c: NewsChoice) => c.id === choiceId);
    if (!choice) return;

    if (choice.effects) applyEffects(next, choice.effects);
    item.resolved = true;
    item.resolvedChoiceId = choiceId;
    item.read = true;
    next.company.reputation = recomputeReputation(next);

    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
  },
  devAddTestNews: () => {
    const s = get().state;
    if (!s) return;
    const next = structuredClone(s);
    next.news.unshift({
      id: `test_action_${next.turn}_${next.news.length}`,
      turn: next.turn,
      year: next.year,
      quarter: next.quarter,
      headline: 'Testowa wiadomość z akcją',
      body: 'To jest test systemu ActionableNews.\nWybierz jedną z opcji poniżej.',
      category: 'custom',
      read: false,
      resolved: false,
      expiresAtTurn: next.turn + 3,
      choices: [
        { id: 'a', label: 'Opcja A — dostajesz 10 000 PLN', effects: { stanKonta: 10000 } },
        { id: 'b', label: 'Opcja B — tracisz 5 000 PLN', effects: { stanKonta: -5000 } },
      ],
      onExpireEffects: { reputation: { opiniaPubliczna: -3 } },
    });
    if (next.news.length > MAX_NEWS) next.news.pop();
    set({ state: next, savesVersion: Date.now() });
    autosave(next, get().currentSlotId);
  },
});
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\store\autosave.ts ===
import type { GameState } from '../engine/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from './settingsStore';

const SAVES_KEY = 'pp-saves-v1';

export interface SaveSlot {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: number;
  lastPlayed: number;
  state: GameState;
}

export type SaveSlots = Record<string, SaveSlot>;

export function loadSlotsFromStorage(): SaveSlots {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVES_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function persistSlots(slots: SaveSlots) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SAVES_KEY, JSON.stringify(slots));
  } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

export function autosave(state: GameState, slotId: string | null) {
  if (!useSettingsStore.getState().autosave || !slotId) return;
  const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
  const existing = slots[slotId];
  slots[slotId] = {
    id: slotId,
    name: existing?.name ?? (state.company.name + state.company.suffix),
    createdAt: existing?.createdAt ?? Date.now(),
    lastPlayed: Date.now(),
    state,
  };
  persistSlots(slots);
}

  activities?: string[];

  suffix: string;

export const useGameStore = create<GameStore>()((set, get, store) => ({
  ...createCombatSlice(set, get, store),
  ...createNewsSlice(set, get, store),
  ...createGameSlice(set, get, store),
  ...createChaseSlice(set, get, store),

  state: null,
  currentSlotId: null,
  screen: 'menu' as AppScreen,
  savesMode: 'load' as 'new' | 'load',
  pendingSlotId: null,
  savesVersion: 0,

  setScreen: (s) => set({ screen: s }),
  openSaves: (mode) => set({ screen: 'saves', savesMode: mode }),
  setPendingSlotId: (id) => set({ pendingSlotId: id }),

  newGame: (opts, slotId) => {
    const s = createInitialState({ seed: String(Date.now()), ...opts });
    s.pace = 'quarter';
    s.currentActivities = getEligibleActivities(s);
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    slots[slotId] = {
      id: slotId,
      name: formatCompanyName(s.company) || 'Firma',
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      lastPlayed: Date.now(),
      state: s,
    };
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ state: s, currentSlotId: slotId, screen: 'game', pendingSlotId: null, savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },

  saveGame: () => {
    const s = get().state;
    const slotId = get().currentSlotId;
    if (!s || !slotId) return;
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    const existing = slots[slotId];
    slots[slotId] = {
      id: slotId,
      name: formatCompanyName(s.company) || existing?.name || 'Firma',
      createdAt: existing?.createdAt ?? Date.now(),
      lastPlayed: Date.now(),
      state: s,
    };
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },

  loadGame: (slotId) => {
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    const slot = slots[slotId];
    if (!slot) return false;
    const migratedState = ensureCompetitors(slot.state);
    migratedState.pace = 'quarter';

    if (!Array.isArray(migratedState.company.ownedTools) || migratedState.company.ownedTools.length === 0) {
      migratedState.company.ownedTools = ['tool_basic'];
    }
    if (typeof migratedState.company.usePrefix !== 'boolean') {
      migratedState.company.usePrefix = true;
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(migratedState.turnActions)) {
      migratedState.turnActions = [];
    }
    if (typeof migratedState.productionWorkersUsedThisTurn !== 'number') {
      migratedState.productionWorkersUsedThisTurn = 0;
    }

    slot.state = migratedState;
    slot.name = formatCompanyName(migratedState.company) || slot.name;
    slot.lastPlayed = Date.now();
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ state: migratedState, currentSlotId: slotId, screen: 'game', savesVersion: Date.now() });
    return true;
  },

  deleteSave: (slotId) => {
    const slots = loadSlotsFromStorage();
    delete slots[slotId];
    persistSlots(slots);
    set({ savesVersion: Date.now() });
  },

  getSlots: () => loadSlotsFromStorage(),
  hasAnySave: () => Object.keys(loadSlotsFromStorage()).length > 0,
}));
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\store\settingsStore.ts ===
import { create } from 'zustand';

const SETTINGS_KEY = 'pp-settings-v1';
const DEBUG_PASSWORD = 'BALCEROWICZ';

export type ThemeId = 'default' | 'teletext' | 'newspaper' | 'gg' | 'brat';

export interface Settings {
  autosave: boolean;
  debugMode: boolean;
  /** Synchronizowane z debugMode dla kompatybilności z ekranem statystyk. */
  showHiddenStats: boolean;
  theme: ThemeId;
}

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: Settings = {
  autosave: true,
  debugMode: false,
  showHiddenStats: false,
  theme: 'default',
};

function loadSettings(): Settings {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    const debugMode = Boolean(parsed.debugMode);
    return {
      autosave: typeof parsed.autosave === 'boolean' ? parsed.autosave : DEFAULT_SETTINGS.autosave,
      debugMode,
      showHiddenStats: debugMode,
      theme: parsed.theme ?? DEFAULT_SETTINGS.theme,
    };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
  }
}

function persist(s: Settings) {
  try {
    const toSave: Settings = {
      autosave: s.autosave,
      debugMode: s.debugMode,
      showHiddenStats: s.debugMode,
      theme: s.theme,
    };
    localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(toSave));
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

interface SettingsStore extends Settings {
  set: <K extends keyof Settings>(key: K, value: Settings[K]) => void;
  unlockDebug: (password: string) => boolean;
  disableDebug: () => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

export const useSettingsStore = create<SettingsStore>((set, get) => ({
  ...loadSettings(),
  set: (key, value) => {
    const current = get();
    const next: Settings = {
      autosave: current.autosave,
      debugMode: current.debugMode,
      showHiddenStats: current.debugMode,
      theme: current.theme,
      [key]: value,
    };
    if (key === 'debugMode') {
      next.showHiddenStats = Boolean(value);
    }
    persist(next);
    set(next);
  },
  unlockDebug: (password: string) => {
    if (password.trim().toUpperCase() !== DEBUG_PASSWORD) {
      return false;
    }
    const current = get();
    const next: Settings = {
      autosave: current.autosave,
      debugMode: true,
      showHiddenStats: true,
      theme: current.theme,
    };
    persist(next);
    set(next);
    return true;
  },
  disableDebug: () => {
    const current = get();
    const next: Settings = {
      autosave: current.autosave,
      debugMode: false,
      showHiddenStats: false,
      theme: current.theme,
    };
    persist(next);
    set(next);
  },
  reset: () => {
    persist(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    set(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  },
}));

interface CompetitorProfileProps {
  compId: string;
  onBack: () => void;
  onSelectCompany: (id: string) => void;
}

const CHARACTER_META: Record<
  CompetitorCharacter,
  { label: string; badgeClass: string; hint: string }
> = {
  aggressive: {
    label: 'Agresywny',
    badgeClass: 'bg-red-100 text-red-900 border-red-300',
    hint: 'Chętnie wywołuje wojny, częściej zdradza zgody i narzuca twarde warunki pokoju.',
  },

    hint: 'Unika konfliktów, lojalny w zgodach i łagodniejszy przy rozstrzygnięciach.',
  },
};

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  handel: 'Handel',
  produkcja: 'Produkcja',
  uslugi: 'Usługi',
  '*': 'Wielobranżowa',
};

const ACQUIRED_MODE_LABELS: Record<AcquiredMode, string> = {
  branch: 'Wchłonięta jako filia',
  liquidated: 'Zlikwidowana (wyprzedaż majątku)',
  subsidiary: 'Spółka zależna (dochód pasywny)',
};

function TrendMark({ t }: { t: Trend }) {
  if (t === 1) return <span title="Wzrost">⬆️</span>;
  if (t === -1) return <span title="Spadek">⬇️</span>;
  return <span title="Bez zmian" className="opacity-40">➡️</span>;
}

function opinionBadge(opinion: number): { label: string; className: string } {
  if (opinion >= 75) return { label: 'Bardzo przychylna', className: 'text-emerald-700' };
  if (opinion >= 55) return { label: 'Przychylna', className: 'text-emerald-600' };
  if (opinion >= 40) return { label: 'Neutralna', className: 'text-stone-700' };
  if (opinion >= 20) return { label: 'Niechętna', className: 'text-amber-700' };
  return { label: 'Wroga', className: 'text-red-700' };
}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">

          <h2 className="font-bold text-stone-900 mb-3">Relacje na rynku (Kosy i zgody)</h2>

            </div>
            {allyCompanies.length === 0 ? (

            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {allyCompanies.map((a) => (
                  <button
                    key={a.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelectCompany(a.id)}
                    className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-900 font-medium transition"
                  >
                    {a.name} →
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mb-3">
            <div className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-red-700 mb-1.5">
              🔪 Kosy ({rivalCompanies.length})
            </div>
            {rivalCompanies.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-400">Brak otwartych kos.</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {rivalCompanies.map((r) => (
                  <button
                    key={r.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelectCompany(r.id)}
                    className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 border border-red-300 text-red-900 font-medium transition"
                  >
                    {r.name} →
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {aiWarsInvolvingComp.length > 0 && (
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-stone-100">
              <div className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-amber-800 mb-1">
                ⚔️ Aktywne wojny z innymi firmami
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs">
                {aiWarsInvolvingComp.map((w) => {
                  const otherId = w.attackerId === comp.id ? w.defenderId : w.attackerId;
                  const other = compById.get(otherId);
                  return (
                    <li key={w.id} className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span>
                        Przeciwko:{' '}
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => onSelectCompany(otherId)}
                          className="font-semibold underline text-stone-900"
                        >
                          {other?.name ?? otherId}
                        </button>
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-mono text-stone-500">wynik: {w.warScore}</span>
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Status przejęć i udziałów */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-xl shadow-sm p-4">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-stone-900 mb-3">Przejęcia i zależności kapitałowe</h2>

          {/* Jeśli ta firma posiada udziały w firmie gracza */}
          {stakeInUs && stakeInUs.percent > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg p-3 mb-3 text-xs">
              <div className="font-bold text-purple-950 mb-1">
                Ta firma posiada {stakeInUs.percent}% udziałów w Twojej firmie
              </div>
              {isOurParent && (
                <div className="mb-2">
                  <div className="text-purple-900 mb-1">
                    Irytacja centrali: <span className="font-mono font-bold">{Math.round(state.company.parentDispleasure ?? 0)} / 100</span>
                    {parentInfo?.isControlling && ' (przy 100 utrata fotela CEO)'}
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full h-2 bg-purple-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-purple-700"
                      style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, state.company.parentDispleasure ?? 0)}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < buybackStepCost}
                  onClick={() => buyBackEquity(comp.id, buybackStep)}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold transition ${
                    state.company.finances.stanKonta < buybackStepCost
                      ? 'bg-stone-200 text-stone-500 cursor-not-allowed'
                      : 'bg-purple-700 text-white hover:bg-purple-800'
                  }`}
                >
                  Odkup {buybackStep}% ({formatMoney(buybackStepCost, state)})
                </button>
                {stakeInUs.percent > buybackStep && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < buybackAllCost}
                    onClick={() => buyBackEquity(comp.id, stakeInUs.percent)}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold transition ${
                      state.company.finances.stanKonta < buybackAllCost
                        ? 'bg-stone-200 text-stone-500 cursor-not-allowed'
                        : 'bg-stone-900 text-white hover:bg-stone-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Odkup całość ({formatMoney(buybackAllCost, state)})
                  </button>
                )}
                {isOurParent && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => requestParentBailout()}
                    className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-bold bg-white border border-purple-400 text-purple-900 hover:bg-purple-100"
                  >
                    Poproś o dokapitalizowanie (+15% udziałów dla nich)
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Jeśli firma była przejęta lub zależna */}
          {comp.status === 'acquired' && (
            <div className="text-xs text-stone-700 bg-stone-50 border border-stone-200 rounded-lg p-3 mb-2">
              <div className="font-bold">
                Przejęta przez:{' '}
                {comp.acquiredBy === 'player'
                  ? 'Twoją firmę'
                  : compById.get(comp.acquiredBy ?? '')?.name ?? comp.acquiredBy}
              </div>
              {comp.acquiredMode && (
                <div className="text-stone-600 mt-0.5">
                  Tryb: {ACQUIRED_MODE_LABELS[comp.acquiredMode]}
                </div>
              )}
              {(comp.subsidiaryIncome ?? 0) > 0 && (
                <div className="text-emerald-700 font-semibold mt-1">
                  Dochód pasywny: +{formatMoney(comp.subsidiaryIncome!, state)} / kw.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {comp.status === 'active' && comp.dependentOn && (
            <div className="text-xs bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-3 mb-2 text-blue-950">
              Firma jest kapitałowo uzależniona od:{' '}
              <strong>
                {comp.dependentOn === 'player'
                  ? 'Twojej firmy'

              </strong>

                  Płaci Ci pasywnie: +{formatMoney(comp.subsidiaryIncome!, state)} / kw.
                </div>
              )}

          )}

          {/* Możliwość przejęcia aktywnej firmy */}

                <span className="font-mono font-bold">
                  {formatMoney(acquisitionInfo.cost, state)}
                </span>
              </div>
              {!acquisitionInfo.canAcquire ? (

              ) : (
                <div className="mt-2">
                  {!showAcquirePanel ? (
                    <button

                            { id: 'subsidiary', label: 'Spółka zależna (stały dochód pasywny co kwartał)' },

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { NewsCategory } from '../../engine/types';

const CATEGORY_COLORS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800',
  poll: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800',
  party: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
  economy: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800',
  custom: 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-700',
};

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'Plotka',
  poll: 'Sondaż',
  party: 'Partia',
  economy: 'Gospodarka',
  custom: 'Ciekawostka',
};

export function NewsTicker({ onOpenArchive }: { onOpenArchive: () => void }) {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  const items = state.news.slice(0, 3);

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border-l-4 border-stone-900 mb-6">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center px-4 py-2 border-b border-stone-100">
        <h2 className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 font-semibold">
          Najnowsze doniesienia
        </h2>
        <button
          onClick={onOpenArchive}
          className="text-xs text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-900 underline"
        >
          Archiwum →
        </button>
      </div>
      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-stone-400 italic">
          Brak doniesień. Świat jeszcze nie zauważył twojej firmy.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul>
          {items.map(item => (
            <li
              key={item.id}
              className="px-4 py-2 border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 flex items-start gap-3"
            >
              <span className={`text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${CATEGORY_COLORS[item.category]}`}>
                {CATEGORY_LABELS[item.category]}
              </span>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-stone-900 truncate">
                  {item.headline}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-mono">
                  {item.year} Q{item.quarter}
                </p>
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { AchievementsScreen } from '../screens/AchievementsScreen';

export function OptionsModal({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { saveGame, setScreen } = useGameStore();
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [showAchievements, setShowAchievements] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-md w-full p-6 max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto">
        {showAchievements ? (
          <AchievementsScreen onBack={() => setShowAchievements(false)} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Opcje</h2>
              <button
                onClick={onClose}
                className="text-stone-400 hover:text-stone-900 text-2xl leading-none"
              >
                ×
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowAchievements(true)}
                className="border py-2 rounded hover:bg-stone-50"
              >
                🏆 Osiągnięcia
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => { saveGame(); setMsg('Gra zapisana.'); }}
                className="border py-2 rounded hover:bg-stone-50"
              >
                💾 Zapisz grę
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  if (confirm('Wrócić do menu głównego? Gra zostanie automatycznie zapisana.')) {
                    saveGame();
                    setScreen('menu');
                    onClose();
                  }
                }}
                className="border py-2 rounded hover:bg-stone-50 text-red-700"
              >
                ← Powrót do menu głównego
              </button>

              {msg && <p className="text-sm text-green-700 text-center mt-2">{msg}</p>}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function StatBar({ label, value, hidden }: { label: string; value: number; hidden?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
        <span>{label}</span>
        <span>{hidden ? '???' : `${Math.round(value)}%`}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded">
        <div
          className="h-full bg-stone-800 rounded"
          style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value))}%` }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';
import type { AcquiredMode, WarDemandType } from '../../engine/types';

export function WarVictoryModal() {
  const { state, resolvePlayerWarVictory } = useGameStore();
  const pending = state?.pendingWarResolution;

  const [selectedDemands, setSelectedDemands] = useState<WarDemandType[]>(['reparations']);
  const [acquireMode, setAcquireMode] = useState<AcquiredMode>('subsidiary');

  if (!state || !pending) return null;

  const isAcquireSelected = selectedDemands.includes('acquire');

  const toggleStandardDemand = (demand: 'reparations' | 'tribute' | 'subjugate') => {
    setSelectedDemands((prev) => {
      const withoutAcquire = prev.filter((d) => d !== 'acquire');
      if (withoutAcquire.includes(demand)) {
        return withoutAcquire.filter((d) => d !== demand);
      }
      return [...withoutAcquire, demand];
    });
  };

  const selectAcquire = () => {
    setSelectedDemands((prev) => (prev.includes('acquire') ? ['reparations'] : ['acquire']));
  };

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    resolvePlayerWarVictory(pending.warId, selectedDemands, acquireMode);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white border border-stone-300 rounded-xl shadow-xl max-w-xl w-full p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
            🏆 Zwycięstwo w wojnie biznesowej
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-stone-500">
            Przewaga końcowa: +{pending.finalWarScore}
          </span>
        </div>

        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-stone-900 mb-1">
          Pokonałeś firmę {pending.loserName}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-5">
          Jako zwycięzca dyktujesz warunki zakończenia konfliktu. Możesz połączyć kilka kar albo całkowicie przejąć pokonanego rywala.
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-2.5 mb-5">
          <label
            className={`flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition ${
              selectedDemands.includes('reparations')
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-400'
                : 'bg-white border-stone-200 hover:bg-stone-50'
            }`}
          >
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={selectedDemands.includes('reparations')}
              onChange={() => toggleStandardDemand('reparations')}
              className="mt-1"
            />
            <div>
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm text-stone-900">
                Jednorazowa kontrybucja ({formatMoney(pending.reparationsAmount, state)})
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-600">
                Natychmiastowy przelew odszkodowania z kasy pokonanej firmy.
              </div>
            </div>
          </label>

          <label
            className={`flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition ${
              selectedDemands.includes('tribute')
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-400'
                : 'bg-white border-stone-200 hover:bg-stone-50'
            }`}
          >
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={selectedDemands.includes('tribute')}
              onChange={() => toggleStandardDemand('tribute')}
              className="mt-1"
            />
            <div>
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm text-stone-900">
                Danina kwartalna ({formatMoney(pending.tributePerQuarter, state)} / kw. przez {pending.tributeQuarters} tur)
              </div>

                        {opt.desc}
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleConfirm}
          className="w-full bg-stone-900 text-white py-2.5 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-stone-800 transition"
        >
          {selectedDemands.length === 0
            ? 'Zakończ wojnę bez roszczeń'
            : 'Zatwierdź warunki pokoju'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import activitiesData from '../../data/activities.json';
import type { Activity, EventEffects } from '../../engine/types';
import { REPUTATION_LABELS } from '../ui-labels';

function describeEffects(e: EventEffects | undefined): string[] {
  if (!e) return [];
  const lines: string[] = [];
  if (e.stanKonta) {
    lines.push(`${e.stanKonta > 0 ? '+' : ''}${e.stanKonta.toLocaleString('pl-PL')} PLN`);
  }
  if (e.reputation) {
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(e.reputation)) {
      if (v) lines.push(`${REPUTATION_LABELS[k] ?? k}: ${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v}`);
    }
  }
  return lines;
}

export function ActivityScreen() {
  const { state, consumeActivity } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  const all = activitiesData as Activity[];
  const current = all.filter(a => state.currentActivities.includes(a.id));
  const alreadyChosen = !!state.chosenActivityThisTurn;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1">Aktywności</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
        {alreadyChosen
          ? `W tej turze wybrałeś już aktywność. Kliknij „Następna tura", aby przejść dalej.`
          : 'Wybierz jedną aktywność na tę turę. Wybór jest ostateczny.'}
      </p>
      <div className="grid gap-3">
        {current.map(a => {
          const chosen = state.chosenActivityThisTurn === a.id;
          const disabled = alreadyChosen;
          const effects = describeEffects(a.effects);
          return (
            <button
              key={a.id}
              onClick={() => !disabled && consumeActivity(a.id)}
              disabled={disabled}
              className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border-2 p-4 text-left transition ${
                chosen
                  ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-50'
                  : disabled
                  ? 'opacity-40 cursor-not-allowed border-transparent'
                  : 'border-transparent hover:border-stone-300'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="font-bold">
                {chosen ? '✓ ' : ''}
                {a.label}
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm text-stone-600 mt-1">{a.description}</div>
              {effects.length > 0 && (
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-2">
                  Skutki: {effects.join(' · ')}
                </div>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';

import { generateTradeSuppliers } from '../../engine/tradeHelper';
import { calculateInstallmentPrice } from '../../engine/actions';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import type { InstallmentOption } from '../../engine/types';

const INSTALLMENT_OPTIONS: InstallmentOption[] = [1, 2, 6, 12, 24];

export function BuyScreen() {
  const { state, buyGoods, rentWarehouse } = useGameStore();
  const [installmentsBySupplier, setInstallmentsBySupplier] = useState<Record<string, InstallmentOption>>({});

  if (!state) return null;

  const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources || {}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const capacity =
    state.company.warehouseCapacity ||
    COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.warehouseCapacity ||
    50;
  const isFull = currentStock >= capacity;
  const warehouseCost = scaleByInflation(10000, state);

  const suppliers = useMemo(() => {
    return generateTradeSuppliers(state);
  }, [
    state.seed,
    state.turn,
    state.company.reputation,
    state.company.activity,
    state.company.activities,
    state.macroStats?.development,
    state.macroStats?.priceIndex,
    state.competitors,
  ]);

  const getInst = (supId: string): InstallmentOption => installmentsBySupplier[supId] ?? 1;
  const setInst = (supId: string, val: InstallmentOption) =>
    setInstallmentsBySupplier(prev => ({ ...prev, [supId]: val }));

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-6 gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">🛒 Kupuj Towar (Dostawcy)</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
            Wybierz dostawcę oraz formę płatności (gotówka lub raty) oddzielnie dla każdego zamówienia.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <div className="bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2 shadow-sm text-right">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">Magazyn</div>
            <div className={`text-base font-bold font-mono ${isFull ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-stone-900'}`}>
              {currentStock} / {capacity} szt.
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={rentWarehouse}
            disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < warehouseCost}
            className="bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 text-white px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            +50 miejsc ({formatMoney(warehouseCost, state)})
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
        {suppliers.map((sup) => {
          const isCompetitor = sup.id.startsWith('comp_');
          const supInst = getInst(sup.id);

          const cost1 = calculateInstallmentPrice(sup.price, supInst);
          const cost10 = calculateInstallmentPrice(Math.round(sup.price * 10 * 0.90), supInst);
          const cost50 = calculateInstallmentPrice(Math.round(sup.price * 50 * 0.75), supInst);

          return (
            <div
              key={sup.id}
              className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border p-4 flex flex-col justify-between ${
                isCompetitor ? 'border-amber-300 bg-amber-50/20' : 'border-stone-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2 mb-1">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-stone-900">{sup.name}</h3>
                  {isCompetitor && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase">
                      Konkurencja
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-3 min-h-[32px]">{sup.desc}</p>

                <div className="bg-stone-50 border border-stone-200 rounded p-2.5 mb-3 space-y-1">

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import {
  ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN,
  COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG,
  BRANCH_COST,
  COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG,
} from '../../engine/constants';
import {
  getCompanyActivities,
  getCategoryIdsForActivities,
  computeStartingActivityStats,
  getRequiredWorkers,
  getCompanyPrefix,
  canSelfSellProducedGoods,
} from '../../engine/activityHelper';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';
import type { ActivityCategory, CompanySize, Complaint } from '../../engine/types';

interface ChangeActivityScreenProps {
  onNavigateToBuy?: () => void;
  onNavigateToSell?: () => void;
  onNavigateToProduction?: () => void;
  onNavigateToServices?: () => void;
}

const RAW_CATEGORIES = branchesData as ActivityCategory[];

export function ChangeActivityScreen({
  onNavigateToBuy,
  onNavigateToSell,
  onNavigateToProduction,
  onNavigateToServices,
}: ChangeActivityScreenProps) {
  const {
    state,
    changeActivity,
    changeCompanySize,
    buildBranch,
    resolveComplaint,
    buyAd,
  } = useGameStore();

  if (!state) return null;

  const currentActivities = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  const activeCategories = getCategoryIdsForActivities(currentActivities);

  const hasTrade = activeCategories.includes('handel');
  const hasProduction = activeCategories.includes('produkcja');
  const hasServices = activeCategories.includes('uslugi');
  const canSelfSellNow = canSelfSellProducedGoods(currentActivities);

  const totalWorkers =
    state.company.employees.bezUmowy +
    state.company.employees.zlecenie +
    state.company.employees.uop;
  const currentRequiredWorkers = getRequiredWorkers(state.company.size, activeCategories.length);

  const availableCategories = useMemo(() => {
    return RAW_CATEGORIES.map(cat => ({
      ...cat,
      activities: cat.activities.filter(
        act => !act.locked || state.ceo.flags.includes(`unlock_${act.id}`)
      ),
    }));
  }, [state.ceo.flags]);

  const [checkedCategories, setCheckedCategories] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [subByCategory, setSubByCategory] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const nextChecked: Record<string, boolean> = {
      handel: false,
      produkcja: false,
      uslugi: false,
    };
    const nextSubs: Record<string, string> = {};

    for (const cat of availableCategories) {
      if (cat.activities[0]) {
        nextSubs[cat.id] = cat.activities[0].id;
      }
      const matchingAct = cat.activities.find(a => currentActivities.includes(a.id));
      if (matchingAct) {
        nextChecked[cat.id] = true;
        nextSubs[cat.id] = matchingAct.id;
      }
    }

    setCheckedCategories(nextChecked);
    setSubByCategory(nextSubs);
  }, [state.company.activity, state.company.activities, availableCategories]);

  const draftCategoryIds = availableCategories
    .map(c => c.id)
    .filter(id => checkedCategories[id]);
  const draftActivities = draftCategoryIds
    .map(catId => subByCategory[catId])
    .filter(Boolean);

  const hasChanges =
    draftActivities.length !== currentActivities.length ||
    draftActivities.some(id => !currentActivities.includes(id));

  const toggleDraftCategory = (catId: string) => {
    setCheckedCategories(prev => {
      const nextVal = !prev[catId];
      const nextState = { ...prev, [catId]: nextVal };

      const activeCount = Object.values(nextState).filter(Boolean).length;
      if (activeCount === 0) return prev;
      return nextState;
    });
  };

  const draftStats = computeStartingActivityStats(draftActivities);
  const draftRequiredWorkers = getRequiredWorkers(state.company.size, draftCategoryIds.length);
  const draftPrefix = getCompanyPrefix(draftActivities);
  const draftHasBothProdAndTrade =
    draftCategoryIds.includes('produkcja') && draftCategoryIds.includes('handel');
  const draftCanSelfSell =
    draftHasBothProdAndTrade && canSelfSellProducedGoods(draftActivities);

  const sizes: CompanySize[] = ['micro', 'small', 'medium', 'large', 'very_large', 'corporation'];
  const currentSizeIndex = sizes.indexOf(state.company.size);
  const nextSize = sizes[currentSizeIndex + 1];
  const nextConfig = nextSize ? COMPANY_EXPANSION_CONFIG[nextSize] : null;
  const nextExpansionCost = nextConfig ? scaleByInflation(nextConfig.cost, state) : 0;
  const nextRequiredWorkers = nextSize
    ? getRequiredWorkers(nextSize, activeCategories.length)
    : 0;
  const canAffordNextSize = nextConfig ? state.company.finances.stanKonta >= nextExpansionCost : false;
  const hasWorkersForNextSize = totalWorkers >= nextRequiredWorkers;

  const branchCost = scaleByInflation(BRANCH_COST, state);
  const complaintCost = scaleByInflation(5000, state);
  const radioAdCost = scaleByInflation(5000, state);
  const internetAdCost = scaleByInflation(15000, state);
  const tvAdCost = scaleByInflation(50000, state);

  const turnsSinceChange = state.turn - state.company.lastActivityChangeTurn;
  const canChange = turnsSinceChange >= ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN;
  const turnsLeft = Math.max(0, ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN - turnsSinceChange);

  const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources || {}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const capacity =
    state.company.warehouseCapacity ||
    COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.warehouseCapacity ||
    50;

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-2 mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Zarządzanie Działalnością</h1>
        <div
          className={`text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded font-semibold ${
            totalWorkers >= currentRequiredWorkers
              ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900'
              : 'bg-red-100 text-red-900'
          }`}
        >
          Pracownicy: {totalWorkers} / {currentRequiredWorkers} wymaganych ({activeCategories.length}{' '}
          {activeCategories.length === 1 ? 'filar' : 'filary'})
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 1. ROZMIAR FIRMY I FILIE */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 mb-6 border-l-4 border-stone-900">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4 mb-2">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">
              Rozmiar firmy: {COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.label}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">Filie: {state.company.branches}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {currentSizeIndex > 0 && (
              <button
                onClick={() => changeCompanySize('down')}
                className="px-4 py-2 border border-red-200 text-red-700 bg-red-50 rounded hover:bg-red-100 transition text-sm font-semibold"
              >
                Zmniejsz
              </button>
            )}
            {nextConfig && nextSize && (
              <button
                onClick={() => changeCompanySize('up')}
                disabled={!canAffordNextSize || !hasWorkersForNextSize}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-stone-900 text-white rounded hover:bg-stone-800 transition text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                Zwiększ na {COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[nextSize]?.label} ({formatMoney(nextExpansionCost, state)}, min. {nextRequiredWorkers} prac.)
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        {['large', 'very_large', 'corporation'].includes(state.company.size) && (
          <div className="border-t border-stone-200 pt-4 mt-2 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-sm">Ekspansja (Filie)</h3>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={buildBranch}
              disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < branchCost}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-green-700 text-white rounded hover:bg-green-800 text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Zbuduj filię ({formatMoney(branchCost, state)})
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* REKLAMACJE */}
      {state.pendingComplaints?.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-red-800 mb-2">⚠ Reklamacje klientów</h2>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {state.pendingComplaints.map((comp: Complaint) => (
              <div
                key={comp.id}
                className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center bg-white p-3 rounded shadow-sm gap-3"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="font-semibold text-sm">{comp.buyerName} zwraca towar</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">
                    Zbyt niska jakość. Żądają zwrotu pieniędzy.
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => resolveComplaint(comp.id, true)}
                    className="bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold"
                  >
                    Uznaj (-{formatMoney(complaintCost, state)})
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => resolveComplaint(comp.id, false)}
                    className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold"
                  >
                    Odrzuć (-{comp.penalty} rep.)
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 2. AKTYWNE FILARY OPERACYJNE (ROZDZIELONY HANDEL I PRODUKCJA) */}
      <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
        {hasTrade && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 border-l-4 border-blue-600">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">🛒 Dział Handlowy i Magazyn</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
                  Stan magazynu: <strong>{currentStock} / {capacity} szt.</strong>
                  {canSelfSellNow && ' (Pasujący profil — sprzedasz tu także własne wyroby z produkcji).'}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-3 w-full md:w-auto">
                <button
                  onClick={onNavigateToBuy}
                  className="flex-1 md:flex-initial px-5 py-2.5 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white font-bold rounded shadow transition text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  🛒 KUPUJ
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={onNavigateToSell}
                  className="flex-1 md:flex-initial px-5 py-2.5 bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold rounded shadow transition text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  💰 SPRZEDAWAJ
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {hasProduction && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 border-l-4 border-amber-600">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">🏭 Zakład Produkcyjny</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
                  Dobór składników, narzędzi na stałe i załogi. Zbyt całości lub części produkcji albo składowanie w magazynie ({currentStock} / {capacity} szt.).
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={onNavigateToProduction}
                className="w-full md:w-auto px-5 py-2.5 bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white font-bold rounded shadow transition text-sm"
              >
                🏭 PRODUKCJA →
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {hasServices && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 border-l-4 border-purple-600">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">🛎️ Katalog Świadczonych Usług</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
                  Włączaj i wyłączaj usługi oferowane na stałe w zależności od reputacji, rozmiaru firmy i kadry.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={onNavigateToServices}
                className="w-full md:w-auto px-5 py-2.5 bg-purple-700 hover:bg-purple-800 text-white font-bold rounded shadow transition text-sm"
              >
                🛎️ USŁUGI →
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 3. REKLAMY */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Kampanie Reklamowe</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => buyAd('radio')}
            disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < radioAdCost}
            className="bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-left flex flex-col disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <span className="text-lg">📻 Radio</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">{formatMoney(radioAdCost, state)}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => buyAd('internet')}
            disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < internetAdCost}
            className="bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-left flex flex-col disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <span className="text-lg">🌐 Internet</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">{formatMoney(internetAdCost, state)}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => buyAd('tv')}
            disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < tvAdCost}
            className="bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-left flex flex-col disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <span className="text-lg">📺 Telewizja</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">{formatMoney(tvAdCost, state)}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4. ZMIANA PROFILU DZIAŁALNOŚCI */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Profil działalności (Przebranżowienie)</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500">
              Możesz dowolnie łączyć Handel, Produkcję i Usługi. Zmiana możliwa raz na {ACTIVITY_CHANGE_COOLDOWN} kwartały.
            </p>
          </div>
          {!canChange && (
            <span className="text-xs bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-semibold">
              Dostępne za {turnsLeft} {turnsLeft === 1 ? 'turę' : 'tury'}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3 mb-4">
          {availableCategories.map(cat => {
            const isChecked = !!checkedCategories[cat.id];
            const isLastActive = isChecked && draftCategoryIds.length <= 1;

            return (
              <div
                key={cat.id}
                className={`border rounded-lg p-3 transition ${
                  isChecked ? 'border-stone-900 bg-stone-50' : 'border-stone-200 bg-white opacity-75'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-3">
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold cursor-pointer min-w-[140px]">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={isChecked}
                      disabled={isLastActive || !canChange}
                      onChange={() => toggleDraftCategory(cat.id)}
                      className="w-4 h-4 accent-stone-900 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    />
                    <span>{cat.name}</span>
                  </label>

                  {isChecked ? (
                    <select
                      value={subByCategory[cat.id] ?? ''}
                      disabled={!canChange}
                      onChange={e =>
                        setSubByCategory(prev => ({ ...prev, [cat.id]: e.target.value }))
                      }
                      className="flex-1 border p-2 rounded bg-white text-sm disabled:opacity-60"
                    >
                      {cat.activities.map(act => (
                        <option key={act.id} value={act.id}>
                          {act.name} (Rep. branży: {act.rep}, Mafia: {act.mafia})
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-xs text-stone-400 italic">
                      Zaznacz, aby rozszerzyć firmę o: {cat.name}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div
          className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-xs mb-4 space-y-1.5 ${
            draftStats.related
              ? 'bg-stone-100 border-stone-300 text-stone-800'
              : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-900'
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              {draftActivities.length === 1 ? (
                <span>Wybrano <b>1 filar</b> działalności (skrót: <b>{draftPrefix}</b>).</span>
              ) : draftStats.related ? (
                <span>
                  ✓ <b>Powiązane branże ({draftPrefix}):</b> brak kary do reputacji branżowej.
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span>
                  ⚠ <b>Niepowiązane branże ({draftPrefix}):</b> <b>-20%</b> do reputacji branżowej i reset opinii u mafii na <b>50%</b>.
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 font-semibold shrink-0">
              <span>Nowa rep. branży: {draftStats.branza}%</span>
              <span>Mafia: {draftStats.mafia}%</span>
              <span>Wymagani pracownicy: {draftRequiredWorkers}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {draftHasBothProdAndTrade && (
            <div className={`pt-1 border-t ${draftStats.related ? 'border-stone-200' : 'border-amber-200'}`}>
              {draftCanSelfSell ? (
                <span className="text-emerald-800 font-semibold">
                  ✓ Profil handlowy pasuje do produkcji — odblokowana samodzielna sprzedaż wyrobów w zakładce Sprzedawaj.
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span>
                  ℹ Profil handlowy nie pokrywa się z produkcją — własne wyroby zbędziesz hurtowo na ekranie Produkcja.
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => changeActivity(draftActivities)}
          disabled={!canChange || !hasChanges}
          className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-stone-900 text-white rounded font-semibold text-sm hover:bg-stone-800 transition disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
        >
          {!hasChanges
            ? 'Profil aktualnie aktywny'
            : !canChange
              ? `Odczekaj ${turnsLeft} tur`
              : 'Zatwierdź nowy profil działalności'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';

export function ChaseScreen() {
  const { state, chaseMove, dismissChase } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.activeChase) return null;
  const c = state.activeChase;

  let paintedCount = 0;
  for (let y = 0; y < c.gridSize; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < c.gridSize; x++) {
      if (c.grid[y][x] === 1) paintedCount++;
    }
  }
  const paintedPct = Math.round((paintedCount / (c.gridSize * c.gridSize)) * 100);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (c.finished) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
          e.preventDefault();
          dismissChase();
        }
        return;
      }

      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'W') {
        e.preventDefault();
        chaseMove(0, -1);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {
        e.preventDefault();
        chaseMove(0, 1);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
        e.preventDefault();
        chaseMove(-1, 0);
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'd' || e.key === 'D') {
        e.preventDefault();
        chaseMove(1, 0);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [c.finished, chaseMove, dismissChase]);

  const renderCell = (x: number, y: number) => {
    const isPlayer = c.player.x === x && c.player.y === y;
    const isEnemy = c.enemies.some(e => e.x === x && e.y === y);
    const isPainted = c.grid[y][x] === 1;

    let content = '';
    let bg = isPainted ? 'bg-green-800' : 'bg-stone-800';

    if (isPlayer) {
      content = '🏃';
      bg = 'bg-blue-600';
    } else if (isEnemy) {
      content = '🥷';
      bg = 'bg-red-700';
    }

    return (
      <div
        key={`${x}-${y}`}
        className={`aspect-square flex items-center justify-center text-lg md:text-2xl border border-stone-900 ${bg}`}
      >
        {content}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/90 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
      <div className="bg-stone-900 text-stone-100 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 border-4 border-red-900">
        <div className="text-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            Odstrasz mafię
          </h2>
          <p className="text-stone-400 text-sm">
            Tura {Math.min(c.turn, c.maxTurns)} / {c.maxTurns} · Zamalowano: <b>{paintedPct}%</b> / 40%
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1">
            Zamaluj 40% planszy, zanim cię dopadną! (Strzałki lub WASD)
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Plansza */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-8 gap-0.5 mb-6 bg-stone-950 p-1 rounded border border-stone-800">
          {Array.from({ length: c.gridSize }).map((_, y) =>
            Array.from({ length: c.gridSize }).map((__, x) => renderCell(x, y))
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Sterowanie */}
        {!c.finished ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => chaseMove(0, -1)}
              className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl"
            >
              ↑
            </button>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => chaseMove(-1, 0)}
                className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl"
              >
                ←
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => chaseMove(0, 1)}
                className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl"
              >
                ↓
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => chaseMove(1, 0)}
                className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 w-16 h-12 rounded font-bold text-xl"
              >
                →
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center">
            <div
              className={`text-xl font-bold mb-2 ${
                c.result === 'escaped' ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400'
              }`}
            >
              {c.result === 'escaped' ? 'Zamalowałeś ich! Uciekli.' : 'Dopadli cię...'}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 mb-4">
              {c.result === 'escaped'
                ? 'Zyskałeś +10 opinii publicznej, ale podpadłeś mafii (-10) i poniosłeś koszty ucieczki.'
                : 'Wysoka kara finansowa, spadek opinii publicznej (-15) i mocne pogorszenie relacji z mafią (-20).'}
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={dismissChase}
              className="bg-stone-100 text-stone-900 w-full py-3 rounded font-bold hover:bg-white"
            >
              Wróć do biura
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\CombatScreen.tsx ===
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';

export function CombatScreen() {
  const { state, combatMove, dismissCombat } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.activeCombat) return null;
  const c = state.activeCombat;

  const canLawyer = state.company.finances.stanKonta >= 20000;
  const canInfluence = state.company.reputation.opiniaPolitycy > 60;
  const canMafia = state.ceo.inMafia;  

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
      <div className="bg-stone-900 text-stone-100 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full p-6 border-4 border-stone-700">
        
        {/* Nagłówek */}
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
            Wizyta Urzędnika
          </h2>
          <p className="text-stone-400 text-sm">Runda {c.round} / 10</p>
        </div>

        {/* Paski życia */}
        <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 mb-6">
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
              <span className="font-bold text-green-400">Twoja Siła Argumentów</span>
              <span>{c.playerHp} / {c.playerHpMax}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-4 bg-stone-800 rounded overflow-hidden">
              <div className="h-full bg-green-500 transition-all" style={{ width: `${(c.playerHp / c.playerHpMax) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
              <span className="font-bold text-red-400">Cierpliwość Urzędnika</span>
              <span>{c.enemyHp} / {c.enemyHpMax}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-4 bg-stone-800 rounded overflow-hidden">
              <div className="h-full bg-red-500 transition-all" style={{ width: `${(c.enemyHp / c.enemyHpMax) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Log walki */}
        <div className="bg-black/50 p-4 rounded h-48 overflow-y-auto mb-6 text-sm font-mono space-y-2">
          {c.log.map((entry, i) => (
            <div key={i} className={
              entry.type === 'player' ? 'text-green-300' :
              entry.type === 'enemy' ? 'text-red-300' : 'text-stone-400'
            }>
              {entry.type === 'player' ? '> ' : entry.type === 'enemy' ? '< ' : '* '}
              {entry.text}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Akcje / Koniec */}
        {!c.finished ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('wspolpraca')} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left">
              <div className="font-bold">Współpraca</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Zawsze działa. (-10 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('adwokat')} disabled={!canLawyer} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left disabled:opacity-50">
              <div className="font-bold">Wezwij adwokata</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Silny cios. (-20k PLN, -25 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('klamstwo')} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left">
              <div className="font-bold">Kłamstwo</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Bardzo silny cios. 30% szans na obrażenia własne. (-30 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('wplywy')} disabled={!canInfluence} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left disabled:opacity-50">
              <div className="font-bold">Wpływy</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Cios ostateczny. Wymaga opinii polityków &gt;60. (-40 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => combatMove('mafia')} disabled={!canMafia} className="bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-600 p-3 rounded text-left disabled:opacity-50">
              <div className="font-bold">Wpływy mafii</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-stone-400">Cios ostateczny. Wymaga bycia w mafii. (-50 cierpliwości)</div>
            </button>
          </div>
