import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import type { Party } from '../../engine/types';
import { partyLabel } from '../ui-labels';

export function ElectionScreen({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.lastElectionResult) return null;

  const result = state.lastElectionResult;
  const allParties = partiesData as Party[];

  const sorted = Object.entries(result.seats)
    .sort(([, a], [, b]) => b - a)
    .map(([id, seats]) => ({
      id,
      seats,
      party: allParties.find(p => p.id === id),
    }));

  const totalSeats = Object.values(result.seats).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full p-8 my-8">
          <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-1">
          Wyniki wyborów
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-1">
          Wybory parlamentarne {result.year}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
          Nowy Sejm wybrany
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
          {sorted.map(({ id, seats, party }) => {
            const percent = (seats / totalSeats) * 100;
            const isMine = state.ceo.currentPartyId === id;
            const inCoalition = result.coalition.includes(id);
            return (
              <div key={id}>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className="font-semibold">
                    {party?.name ?? id}
                    {isMine && <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-stone-500">(twoja partia)</span>}
                    {inCoalition && <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-amber-600">· w koalicji</span>}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono">
                    {seats} mandatów · {percent.toFixed(1)}%
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-3 bg-stone-100 rounded overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full ${isMine ? 'bg-stone-900' : inCoalition ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-stone-400'}`}
                    style={{ width: `${percent}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="bg-stone-100 rounded p-4 mb-6 text-sm">
          <p className="mb-1">
            <b>Koalicja rządząca:</b> {result.coalition.map(partyLabel).join(', ') || 'brak'}
          </p>
          <p>
            <b>Nowy premier:</b> {partyLabel(result.premierId)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full bg-stone-900 text-white py-3 rounded font-bold hover:bg-stone-800"
        >
          OK
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\EmployeesScreen.tsx ===
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { ContractType } from '../../engine/types';
import { WORKER_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';

export function EmployeesScreen() {
  const { state, hireWorker } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;
  const workers = state.company.employees;
  const pendingHires = state.pendingHires ?? [];

  const getEfficiency = (count: number) => Math.max(20, Math.round((1 - count * 0.05) * 100));

  const getPendingCount = (contract: ContractType) =>
    pendingHires
      .filter(h => h.contract === contract)
      .reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.count, 0);

  const totalPending = pendingHires.reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.count, 0);

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2">Pracownicy</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
        Każdy kolejny pracownik w danej grupie pracuje z mniejszą wydajnością (prawo malejących przychodów). Nowo zakontraktowani pracownicy dołączają do załogi w <b>kolejnej turze</b>.
      </p>

      {totalPending > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 text-amber-950 rounded-lg p-3.5 mb-5 text-xs sm:text-sm">
          ⏳ Oczekujący na rozpoczęcie pracy w następnym kwartale: <strong>+{totalPending} os.</strong>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 mb-6">
        <h2 className="font-bold mb-3">Zatrudnienie</h2>
        {(['bezUmowy', 'zlecenie', 'uop'] as ContractType[]).map(c => {
          const count = workers[c];
          const pending = getPendingCount(c);
          const nextWorkerEff = getEfficiency(count + pending);
          const scaledWage = scaleByInflation(WORKER_CONFIG[c].wage, state);
          const scaledRevenue = scaleByInflation(WORKER_CONFIG[c].revenue, state);

          return (
            <div key={c} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 gap-3">
              <div>
                <div className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  <span>
                    {c === 'bezUmowy' ? 'Bez umowy' : c === 'zlecenie' ? 'Umowa zlecenie' : 'Umowa o pracę'}
                  </span>
                  {pending > 0 && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      +{pending} od nast. tury
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-600">
                  Koszt: {formatMoney(scaledWage, state)} | Baza: {formatMoney(scaledRevenue, state)}
                </div>
                <div className={`text-xs font-semibold mt-1 ${nextWorkerEff < 50 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-green-700'}`}>
                  Wydajność kolejnego: {nextWorkerEff}%
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <button
                  onClick={() => hireWorker(c, -1)}
                  disabled={count === 0 && pending === 0}
                  className="w-8 h-8 rounded border bg-white hover:bg-stone-50 disabled:opacity-30"
                >
                  −
                </button>
                <span className="w-10 text-center font-mono">{count}</span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => hireWorker(c, 1)}
                  className="w-8 h-8 rounded border bg-white hover:bg-stone-50"
                >
                  +
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">

              className="border p-4 rounded text-left hover:bg-stone-50 transition"

                <div className="text-sm text-stone-600 mt-1">{c.description}</div>

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { isCompanyInboxItem } from './NewsScreen';
import type { NewsCategory, NewsItem } from '../../engine/types';

type PeriodicalId = 'all' | 'politics' | 'economy' | 'rumors';

interface PeriodicalDef {
  id: PeriodicalId;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  badge: string;
  categories: NewsCategory[];
}

const PERIODICALS: PeriodicalDef[] = [
  {
    id: 'all',
    title: 'Przegląd Prasy',
    subtitle: 'Wszystkie bieżące wydania i doniesienia z kraju',
    badge: 'Wszystkie',
    categories: ['poll', 'party', 'economy', 'rumor'],
  },
  {
    id: 'politics',
    title: 'Kurier Polityczny',
    subtitle: 'Sondaże, przetasowania i nowe ugrupowania',
    badge: 'Polityka',
    categories: ['poll', 'party'],
  },
  {
    id: 'economy',
    title: 'Gazeta Gospodarcza',
    subtitle: 'Sytuacja rynkowa i wiadomości ekonomiczne',
    badge: 'Gospodarka',
    categories: ['economy'],
  },
  {
    id: 'rumors',
    title: 'Kuluary & Plotki',
    subtitle: 'Nieoficjalne doniesienia i pogłoski z miasta',
    badge: 'Plotki',
    categories: ['rumor'],
  },
];

const CATEGORY_COLORS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800',
  poll: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800',
  party: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
  economy: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800',
  custom: 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-700',
};

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'Plotka',
  poll: 'Sondaż',
  party: 'Partia',
  economy: 'Gospodarka',
  custom: 'Prasa',
};

function getPeriodicalNameForItem(item: NewsItem): string {
  if (item.category === 'rumor') return 'Kuluary & Plotki';
  if (item.category === 'economy') return 'Gazeta Gospodarcza';
  if (item.category === 'poll' || item.category === 'party') return 'Kurier Polityczny';
  return 'Przegląd Prasy';
}

export function GlobalNewsScreen() {
  const { state, markNewsRead } = useGameStore();
  const [activePeriodical, setActivePeriodical] = useState<PeriodicalId>('all');
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const publicNews = state ? state.news.filter(n => !isCompanyInboxItem(n)) : [];
  const currentDef = PERIODICALS.find(p => p.id === activePeriodical) ?? PERIODICALS[0];
  const filtered = publicNews.filter(n => currentDef.categories.includes(n.category));
  const selected: NewsItem | null =
    filtered.find(n => n.id === selectedId) ?? filtered[0] ?? null;

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedId(null);
  }, [activePeriodical]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selected && !selected.read) {
      markNewsRead(selected.id);
    }
  }, [selected?.id, selected?.read, markNewsRead]);

  if (!state) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1">📰 Newsy i Periodyki</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
          Kiosk prasowy: doniesienia z kraju, sondaże, zmiany na scenie politycznej oraz kuluarowe plotki.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Wybór periodyku */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {PERIODICALS.map(per => {
          const isActive = activePeriodical === per.id;
          const itemsForPer = publicNews.filter(n => per.categories.includes(n.category));
          const unreadForPer = itemsForPer.filter(n => !n.read).length;

          return (
            <button
              key={per.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActivePeriodical(per.id)}
              className={`text-left p-3.5 rounded-lg border-2 transition flex flex-col justify-between ${
                isActive
                  ? 'border-stone-900 bg-stone-900 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'border-stone-200 bg-white hover:border-stone-400 text-stone-900'
              }`}
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm">{per.title}</span>
                  {unreadForPer > 0 && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-red-500 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                      {unreadForPer} nowe
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className={`text-xs ${isActive ? 'text-stone-300' : 'text-stone-500'}`}>
                  {per.subtitle}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className={`text-[11px] font-mono mt-2 pt-2 border-t ${isActive ? 'border-stone-700 text-stone-300' : 'border-stone-100 text-stone-400'}`}>
                Artykuły: {itemsForPer.length}
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Czytelnia wybranego periodyku */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 p-8 text-center text-stone-500">
          Brak wydań w dziale <strong>{currentDef.title}</strong>. Zajrzyj tu w kolejnym kwartale.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {/* Spis artykułów */}
          <div className="md:col-span-1 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div className="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-stone-100 border-b border-stone-200 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-stone-700">
              {currentDef.title} · Wydania ({filtered.length})
            </div>
            <div className="max-h-[540px] overflow-y-auto">
              {filtered.map(item => {
                const isSelected = selected?.id === item.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedId(item.id)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3.5 py-3 border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 transition ${
                      isSelected ? 'bg-stone-100 border-l-4 border-l-stone-900' : 'hover:bg-stone-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold ${CATEGORY_COLORS[item.category]}`}>
                        {CATEGORY_LABELS[item.category]}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-mono">
                        {item.year} Q{item.quarter}
                      </span>
                      {!item.read && (
                        <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500 ml-auto" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className={`text-sm ${isSelected || !item.read ? 'font-bold' : 'font-medium'} text-stone-900`}>
                      {item.headline}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Artykuł w stylu periodyku */}
          <div className="md:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 p-5 md:p-7">
            {selected ? (
              <article>
                <div className="border-b-2 border-stone-900 pb-3 mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-stone-700">
                    {getPeriodicalNameForItem(selected)}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono text-stone-500">
                    Wydanie: {selected.year} · Kwartał {selected.quarter}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mb-3">
                  <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold ${CATEGORY_COLORS[selected.category]}`}>
                    {CATEGORY_LABELS[selected.category]}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-stone-900 mb-4 leading-snug">
                  {selected.headline}
                </h2>

                <p className="text-stone-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line text-sm md:text-base">
                  {selected.body ?? 'Krótka notatka agencyjna — brak dodatkowych szczegółów.'}
                </p>
              </article>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-stone-500 text-sm">Wybierz artykuł z listy po lewej.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

Chętnie ci pomoże w rozkręceniu twojego pierwszego biznesu w Wolnej Polsce.';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { NewsCategory, NewsItem } from '../../engine/types';

const CATEGORY_COLORS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800',
  poll: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800',
  party: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
  economy: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800',
  custom: 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-700',
};

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<NewsCategory, string> = {
  rumor: 'Plotka',
  poll: 'Sondaż',
  party: 'Partia',
  economy: 'Raport',
  custom: 'Wiadomość',
};

/**
 * Rozdziela korespondencję prywatną/firmową (Skrzynka odbiorcza w Wiadomościach)
 * od prasy i doniesień publicznych (Newsy / Periodyki w Globalnych).
 */
export function isCompanyInboxItem(item: NewsItem): boolean {
  if (item.choices && item.choices.length > 0) return true;
  if (item.category === 'custom') return true;
  if (
    item.id.startsWith('sales_report_') ||
    item.id.startsWith('services_report_') ||
    item.id.startsWith('hires_joined_') ||
    item.id.startsWith('debug_msg_')
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function NewsScreen() {
  const { state, markNewsRead, resolveNewsChoice } = useGameStore();
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const inboxItems = state ? state.news.filter(isCompanyInboxItem) : [];
  const selected: NewsItem | null =
    inboxItems.find(n => n.id === selectedId) ?? inboxItems[0] ?? null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selected && !selected.read && !(selected.choices && !selected.resolved)) {
      markNewsRead(selected.id);
    }
  }, [selected?.id, selected?.read, selected?.resolved, markNewsRead]);

  if (!state) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1">✉ Skrzynka odbiorcza</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
            Korespondencja firmowa, raporty rozliczeń i sprawy wymagające decyzji. Prasę i plotki znajdziesz w zakładce <b>Globalne → Newsy</b>.
          </p>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold bg-stone-200 text-stone-800 px-3 py-1 rounded-full self-start sm:self-auto">
          Wiadomości: {inboxItems.length}
        </span>
      </div>

      {inboxItems.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-8 text-center text-stone-500">
          Skrzynka odbiorcza jest pusta.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {/* Lista po lewej */}
          <div className="md:col-span-1 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
            <div className="max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto">
              {inboxItems.map(item => {
                const isSelected = selected?.id === item.id;
                const hasPendingAction = !!(item.choices && !item.resolved);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.id}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedId(item.id)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-3 border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 transition ${
                      isSelected ? 'bg-stone-100 border-l-4 border-l-stone-900' : 'hover:bg-stone-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold ${CATEGORY_COLORS[item.category]}`}>
                        {CATEGORY_LABELS[item.category]}
                      </span>
                      {hasPendingAction && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold bg-red-500 text-white">
                          AKCJA
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="text-xs text-stone-400 font-mono">
                        {item.year} Q{item.quarter}
                      </span>
                      {(!item.read || hasPendingAction) && (
                        <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ml-auto ${hasPendingAction ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-blue-500'}`} />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className={`text-sm ${isSelected ? 'font-bold' : item.read && !hasPendingAction ? 'font-medium' : 'font-bold'} text-stone-900`}>
                      {item.headline}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Treść po prawej */}
          <div className="md:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 md:p-6">
            {selected ? (
              <article>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 flex-wrap">
                  <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold ${CATEGORY_COLORS[selected.category]}`}>
                    {CATEGORY_LABELS[selected.category]}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-mono">
                    {selected.year} Q{selected.quarter}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-3">{selected.headline}</h2>
                <p className="text-stone-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                  {selected.body ?? 'Brak dodatkowych szczegółów.'}
                </p>

                {/* Decyzje — jeśli są i nie rozwiązane */}
                {selected.choices && !selected.resolved && (
                  <div className="mt-6 border-t border-stone-200 pt-4">
                    <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-3">
                      Wymaga decyzji
                      {selected.expiresAtTurn !== undefined && (
                        <span className="ml-2 text-red-600">
                          (wygasa za {Math.max(0, selected.expiresAtTurn - state.turn)} tur)
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                      {selected.choices.map(c => (
                        <button
                          key={c.id}
                          onClick={() => resolveNewsChoice(selected.id, c.id)}
                          className="text-left border-2 border-stone-200 rounded-lg p-3 hover:border-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-50 transition"
                        >
                          <div className="font-semibold">{c.label}</div>
                          {c.description && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-stone-600 mt-1">{c.description}</div>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Po decyzji — pokaż co wybrałeś */}
                {selected.resolved && selected.resolvedChoiceId && (
                  <div className="mt-6 border-t border-stone-200 pt-4">
                    {selected.resolvedChoiceId === '__expired__' ? (
                      <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 italic">
                        Zignorowano. Sprawa się przedawniła.
                      </p>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-sm text-stone-500 italic">
                        Decyzja: {selected.choices?.find(c => c.id === selected.resolvedChoiceId)?.label ?? selected.resolvedChoiceId}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </article>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-stone-500 text-sm">Wybierz wiadomość z listy po lewej.</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { ACCOUNTANT_COST } from '../../engine/constants';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';
import officeItems from '../../data/office_items.json';

export function OfficeScreen() {
  const { state, toggleAccountant, buyOfficeItem } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  const accountantCost = scaleByInflation(ACCOUNTANT_COST, state);

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Biuro</h1>

      {/* KSIĘGOWA */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 mb-6">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">
              Pani Grażyna (Księgowa)
              {state.company.hasAccountant && (
                <span className="ml-2 text-xs bg-green-100 text-green-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  Zatrudniona
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-stone-600 mt-1">
              Koszt: {formatMoney(accountantCost, state)} / kwartał. Zwiększa opinię w Urzędzie Skarbowym o +3 co turę.
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={toggleAccountant}
            className={`px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-semibold ${
              state.company.hasAccountant ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800' : 'bg-stone-900 text-white'
            }`}
          >
            {state.company.hasAccountant ? 'Zwolnij' : 'Zatrudnij'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SKLEP Z PRZEDMIOTAMI */}
      <h2 className="font-bold mb-3">Wyposażenie biura</h2>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {(officeItems as Array<{ id: string; name: string; price: number; description: string }>).map(item => {
          const scaledPrice = scaleByInflation(item.price, state);
          const owned = state.company.inventory.includes(item.id);
          const canAfford = state.company.finances.stanKonta >= scaledPrice;

          return (
            <div key={item.id} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div>
                <div className="font-semibold flex justify-between">
                  <span>{item.name}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-stone-600">{formatMoney(scaledPrice, state)}</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1 mb-3">{item.description}</p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => buyOfficeItem(item.id, scaledPrice)}
                disabled={owned || !canAfford}
                className={`w-full py-2 rounded text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                  owned
                    ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 cursor-default'
                    : canAfford
                      ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white hover:bg-stone-800'
                      : 'bg-stone-200 text-stone-400 cursor-not-allowed'
                }`}
              >
                {owned ? '✓ Posiadasz' : canAfford ? 'Kup' : 'Brak środków'}
              </button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
=== PLIK: C:\Users\Laptop\Desktop\przedsiebiorca-sim\src\ui\screens\OptionsScreen.tsx ===
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { useSettingsStore } from '../../store/settingsStore';
import { THEMES } from '../themes';

export function OptionsScreen() {
  const { setScreen } = useGameStore();
  const { autosave, debugMode, theme, set, unlockDebug, disableDebug, reset } = useSettingsStore();
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');
  const [showDebugInput, setShowDebugInput] = useState(false);
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [debugMsg, setDebugMsg] = useState<{ text: string; ok: boolean } | null>(null);

  const handleUnlockDebug = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   e.preventDefault();
   if (unlockDebug(password)) {
     setPassword('');
     setShowDebugInput(false);
     setDebugMsg({ text: 'Tryb debugowania włączony.', ok: true });
   } else {
     setDebugMsg({ text: 'Nieprawidłowe hasło.', ok: false });
   }
  };


  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 p-6 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg p-8 max-w-2xl w-full my-8">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Opcje</h1>
          <button
            onClick={() => setScreen('menu')}
            className="text-sm text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-900"
          >
            ← Powrót
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-5">
          <OptionRow
            label="Autozapis"
            description="Automatycznie zapisuje stan gry po każdej akcji."
          >
            <Toggle value={autosave} onChange={v => set('autosave', v)} />
          </OptionRow>

          <hr className="border-stone-200" />

          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-stone-900 mb-1">Skórka</label>
            <select
              value={theme}
              onChange={e => set('theme', e.target.value as any)}
              className="w-full border border-stone-300 rounded px-3 py-2 bg-white text-stone-900"
            >
              {THEMES.map(t => (
                <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                  {t.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-2 italic">
              {THEMES.find(t => t.id === theme)?.description}
            </p>
          </div>

          <hr className="border-stone-200" />

          <div>
            <OptionRow
              label="Tryb debugowania"
              description="Odsłania ukryte statystyki oraz włącza pasek debugowania (testowe wiadomości i minigry)."
            >
              {debugMode ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    disableDebug();
                    setDebugMsg({ text: 'Tryb debugowania wyłączony.', ok: true });
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm border border-amber-400 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-100 font-semibold"
                >
                  Wyłącz
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    setShowDebugInput(v => !v);
                    setDebugMsg(null);
                  }}
                  className="px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm border hover:bg-stone-50"
                >
                  {showDebugInput ? 'Anuluj' : 'Odblokuj'}
                </button>
              )}
            </OptionRow>

            {!debugMode && showDebugInput && (
              <form onSubmit={handleUnlockDebug} className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Wpisz hasło..."
                  autoFocus
                  className="flex-1 border border-stone-300 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm bg-white text-stone-900"
                />
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="bg-stone-900 text-white px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-stone-800"
                >
                  OK
                </button>
              </form>
            )}

            {debugMsg && (
              <p className={`text-xs mt-2 ${debugMsg.ok ? 'text-green-700' : 'text-red-600'}`}>
                {debugMsg.text}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <hr className="border-stone-200" />

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                reset();
                setDebugMsg(null);
                setShowDebugInput(false);
                setMsg('Ustawienia przywrócone do domyślnych.');
              }}
              className="border py-2 rounded hover:bg-stone-50"
            >
              Przywróć domyślne ustawienia
            </button>
          </div>

          {msg && <p className="text-sm text-green-700 text-center">{msg}</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function OptionRow({
  label,
  description,
  children, 
}: {
  label: string;
  description: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-4">
      <div className="flex-1">
        <div className="font-semibold text-stone-900">{label}</div>
        <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">{description}</div>
      </div>
      <div>{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Toggle({ value, onChange }: { value: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => onChange(!value)}
      className={`w-12 h-6 rounded-full transition relative ${
        value ? 'bg-stone-900' : 'bg-stone-300'
      }`}
    >
      <span
        className={`absolute top-0.5 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white transition ${
          value ? 'left-6' : 'left-0.5'
        }`}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { getPartySize, getActiveParties } from '../../engine/politics';
import { MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE } from '../../engine/constants';
import { makeRng } from '../../engine/rng';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import type { Party } from '../../engine/types';

const IDEOLOGY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  radical_right: 'Skrajna prawica',
  moderate_right: 'Umiarkowana prawica',
  center: 'Centrum',
  moderate_left: 'Umiarkowana lewica',
  radical_left: 'Skrajna lewica',
};

const SIZE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  very_small: 'Bardzo mała',
  small: 'Mała',
  medium: 'Średnia',
  large: 'Duża',
  very_large: 'Bardzo duża',
};

export function PartyScreen() {
  const { state, joinParty } = useGameStore();

  if (!state) return null;

  const is1989 = state.year <= 1989;
  const switchesUsed = state.ceo.partySwitchesByPhase[state.phase];
  const switchesLeft = MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE - switchesUsed;
  const myParty = (partiesData as Party[]).find(p => p.id === state.ceo.currentPartyId) ?? null;

  // Ustalamy limit partii (bazowo 4, z komputerem 6)
  const maxChoices = state.company.inventory.includes('komputer') ? 6 : 4;

  // Logika losowania partii (zapewniająca różnorodność)
  const visibleParties = useMemo(() => {
    if (is1989) return [];

    const active = getActiveParties(state);

    const left = active.filter(p => ['radical_left', 'moderate_left'].includes(p.ideology));
    const center = active.filter(p => p.ideology === 'center');
    const right = active.filter(p => ['radical_right', 'moderate_right'].includes(p.ideology));

    const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 13 + 7);
    const selected: Party[] = [];

    const addFromPool = (pool: Party[]) => {
      if (selected.length >= maxChoices || pool.length === 0) return;
      const shuffled = [...pool].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);
      const pick = shuffled.find(p => !selected.some(s => s.id === p.id));
      if (pick) selected.push(pick);
    };

    addFromPool(left);
    addFromPool(center);
    addFromPool(right);

    const remaining = active.filter(p => !selected.some(s => s.id === p.id));
    const shuffledRemaining = [...remaining].sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);

    while (selected.length < maxChoices && shuffledRemaining.length > 0) {
      selected.push(shuffledRemaining.pop()!);
    }

    return selected.sort(() => rng.next() - 0.5);
  }, [state.seed, state.year, state.turn, state.ceo.party, state.politics.pslAvailable, state.politics.partySchedule, maxChoices, is1989]);

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Partie polityczne</h1>

      {is1989 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-100 border border-amber-300 text-amber-900 p-4 rounded-lg mb-6">
          <p className="font-bold">Okres przejściowy</p>
          <p className="text-sm">
            W 1989 roku trwa Sejm kontraktowy. Dołączanie do partii politycznych będzie możliwe po pierwszych wolnych wyborach w 1990 roku.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {myParty && (
        <section className="mb-8">
          <h2 className="text-sm uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-2">Twoja partia</h2>
          <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white rounded-lg p-5">
            <div className="font-bold text-lg">{myParty.name}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-stone-300 mb-3">
              {IDEOLOGY_LABELS[myParty.ideology]} · {SIZE_LABELS[getPartySize(myParty, state.year)]}
              {state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(myParty.id) && ' · w koalicji rządzącej'}
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-400 italic mb-4">"{myParty.descriptionAfterJoin}"</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 border-t border-stone-700 pt-2">
              W tej fazie zmieniłeś partię {switchesUsed} / {MAX_PARTY_SWITCHES_PER_PHASE} razy.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {!myParty && !is1989 && (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 mb-8 text-stone-600">
          <p>Nie należysz do żadnej partii.</p>
          <p className="text-xs mt-1">Możesz zmienić partię jeszcze {switchesLeft} razy w tej fazie.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {!is1989 && (
        <section>
          <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-sm uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500">
              Dostępne opcje (rok {state.year})
            </h2>
            {maxChoices > 4 && (
              <span className="text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold bg-green-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                Dzięki komputerowi widzisz więcej partii
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {visibleParties.map((p, idx) => {
              const isMine = p.id === state.ceo.currentPartyId;
              const isRuling = state.politics.rulingCoalition.includes(p.id);
              const currentSize = getPartySize(p, state.year);
              const descRng = makeRng(state.seed, state.turn * 19 + idx);
              const randomDesc = p.descriptions.length > 0 ? descRng.pick(p.descriptions) : '';

              return (
                <div
                  key={p.id}
                  className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 border-2 flex flex-col justify-between transition ${
                    isMine
                      ? 'border-stone-900 ring-2 ring-stone-900/20'
                      : isRuling
                      ? 'border-amber-500'
                      : 'border-transparent hover:border-stone-300'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                      <h3 className="font-bold leading-tight">{p.name}</h3>
                      <div className="flex gap-1 shrink-0">
                        {isMine && <span className="text-[10px] bg-stone-900 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">twoja</span>}
                        {isRuling && !isMine && <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-500 text-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">rządzi</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-semibold mb-2">
                      {IDEOLOGY_LABELS[p.ideology]} · {SIZE_LABELS[currentSize]}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 italic mb-4">
                      "{randomDesc}"
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => joinParty(p.id)}
                    disabled={switchesLeft <= 0 || isMine}
                    className="text-sm bg-stone-900 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded disabled:opacity-30 w-full mt-auto"
                  >
                    {isMine ? 'Należysz' : 'Dołącz'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import type { Party } from '../../engine/types';

export function PresidentialElectionScreen({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state || !state.lastPresidentialResult) return null;

  const r = state.lastPresidentialResult;
  const allParties = partiesData as Party[];

  const firstRoundSorted = [...r.candidates].sort((a, b) => b.votesPercent - a.votesPercent);

  const partyNameFor = (partyId: string | null, partyLabel?: string) => {
    if (partyLabel) return partyLabel;
    if (!partyId) return 'niezależny';
    const p = allParties.find(x => x.id === partyId);
    if (p) return p.name;
    return partyId.replace(/_/g, ' ');
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full p-8 my-8">
        <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 mb-1">
          Wyniki wyborów
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-1">
          Wybory prezydenckie {r.year}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-6">
          {r.secondRound ? 'Rozstrzygnięcie w II turze' : 'Rozstrzygnięcie w I turze'}
        </p>

        <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white rounded-lg p-5 mb-6">
          <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-400 mb-1">
            {r.secondRound ? 'Zwycięzca II tury' : 'Zwycięzca'}
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{r.winner.name}</div>
          <div className="text-sm text-stone-300">
            {partyNameFor(r.winner.partyId, r.winner.partyLabel)}
          </div>
        </div>

        <h2 className="font-bold mb-3 text-stone-800">I tura</h2>
        <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
          {firstRoundSorted.map(c => {
            const isWinner = c.id === r.winner.id;
            const inSecondRound = r.secondRoundCandidates?.some(x => x.id === c.id);
            return (
              <div key={c.id}>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className={isWinner ? 'font-bold' : ''}>
                    {c.name}
                    <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-stone-500">
                      {partyNameFor(c.partyId, c.partyLabel)}
                    </span>
                    {inSecondRound && (
                      <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-amber-600">· II tura</span>
                    )}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono">{c.votesPercent.toFixed(1)}%</span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-2 bg-stone-100 rounded overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full ${isWinner ? 'bg-stone-900' : inSecondRound ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-stone-400'}`}
                    style={{ width: `${c.votesPercent}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {r.secondRound && r.secondRoundCandidates && (
          <>
            <h2 className="font-bold mb-3 text-stone-800">II tura</h2>
            <div className="bg-stone-50 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6 text-sm">
              <p className="mb-2 text-stone-600">
                Żaden kandydat nie zdobył ponad 50% w I turze. Do II tury weszli:
              </p>
              {r.secondRoundCandidates.map(c => (
                <div key={c.id} className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-stone-200 last:border-b-0">
                  <span className={c.id === r.winner.id ? 'font-bold' : ''}>
                    {c.name} {c.id === r.winner.id && '(zwycięzca)'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono">{partyNameFor(c.partyId, c.partyLabel)}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="w-full bg-stone-900 text-white py-3 rounded font-bold hover:bg-stone-800"
        >
          OK
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { useSettingsStore } from '../../store/settingsStore';
import { COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';
import {
  getProductionActivityId,
  getProductionIngredients,
  getProductionTools,
  getTotalProductionWorkers,
  getAvailableProductionWorkers,
  getProductionMarketFactors,
  estimateProductionBatch,
  estimateStoredProducedItem,
} from '../../engine/productionHelper';
import {
  getActivityName,
  getCompanyActivities,
  getMatchingTradeActivitiesForProduction,
} from '../../engine/activityHelper';
import { getRevenueRange } from '../../engine/tradeHelper';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import { formatMoney, formatMoneyRange } from '../../engine/currency';

interface ProductionScreenProps {
  onNavigateToSell?: () => void;
}

export function ProductionScreen({ onNavigateToSell }: ProductionScreenProps) {
  const {
    state,
    buyProductionTool,
    runProductionBatch,
    sellProducedStock,
    rentWarehouse,
    undoLastAction,
  } = useGameStore();

  const debugMode = useSettingsStore(s => s.debugMode);

  const [selectedIngredientId, setSelectedIngredientId] = useState<string>('ing_krajowe');
  const [selectedToolId, setSelectedToolId] = useState<string>('tool_basic');
  const [workersCount, setWorkersCount] = useState<number>(1);
  const [sellPercentMode, setSellPercentMode] = useState<'all' | 'half' | 'store' | 'custom'>('all');
  const [customSellAmount, setCustomSellAmount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [stockSellAmounts, setStockSellAmounts] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});

  if (!state) return null;

  const prodActivityName = getActivityName(getProductionActivityId(state));
  const companyActivities = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  const matchingTradeItems = getMatchingTradeActivitiesForProduction(companyActivities);
  const canSelfSell = matchingTradeItems.length > 0 && Boolean(onNavigateToSell);

  const totalWorkers = getTotalProductionWorkers(state);
  const availableWorkers = getAvailableProductionWorkers(state);

  const currentStock = Object.values(state.company.resources || {}).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const capacity =
    state.company.warehouseCapacity ||
    COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.warehouseCapacity ||
    50;
  const freeWarehouseSpace = Math.max(0, capacity - currentStock);
  const warehouseRentCost = scaleByInflation(10000, state);

  const marketFactors = useMemo(() => getProductionMarketFactors(state), [state]);
  const ingredients = useMemo(() => getProductionIngredients(state), [state]);
  const tools = useMemo(() => getProductionTools(state), [state]);

  const activeIngredient =
    ingredients.find(i => i.id === selectedIngredientId && i.meetsRequirements) ??
    ingredients.find(i => i.meetsRequirements) ??
    ingredients[0];

  const activeTool =
    tools.find(t => t.id === selectedToolId && t.owned) ??
    tools.find(t => t.owned) ??
    tools[0];

  const safeWorkers =
    availableWorkers > 0 ? Math.min(Math.max(1, workersCount), availableWorkers) : 1;

  const estimate =
    activeIngredient && activeTool
      ? estimateProductionBatch(state, activeIngredient.id, activeTool.id, safeWorkers)
      : null;

  const sellNowAmount = useMemo(() => {
    if (!estimate) return 0;
    if (sellPercentMode === 'all') return estimate.units;
    if (sellPercentMode === 'store') return 0;
    if (sellPercentMode === 'half') return Math.floor(estimate.units / 2);
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(estimate.units, Math.floor(customSellAmount)));
  }, [estimate, sellPercentMode, customSellAmount]);

  const toStoreAmount = estimate ? estimate.units - sellNowAmount : 0;
  const canFitInWarehouse = toStoreAmount <= freeWarehouseSpace;
  const canAffordBatch = estimate ? state.company.finances.stanKonta >= estimate.totalCost : false;

  const accuracy = state.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const sellNowRange = estimate
    ? getRevenueRange(Math.round(estimate.estimatedUnitPrice * sellNowAmount), accuracy)
    : null;

  const emp = state.company.employees;
  const hiredWorkers = emp.bezUmowy + emp.zlecenie + emp.uop;
  const hasUopBonus = hiredWorkers > 0 && emp.uop / hiredWorkers >= 0.5;

  const storedProducedItems = Object.entries(state.company.resources || {})
    .filter(([resId, amt]) => resId.startsWith('prod_') && (amt as number) > 0)
    .map(([resId]) => estimateStoredProducedItem(state, resId));

  const getStockQty = (resId: string, maxStock: number) => {
    const val = stockSellAmounts[resId];
    if (val === undefined) return maxStock;
    return Math.max(1, Math.min(maxStock, Math.floor(val)));
  };

  const setStockQty = (resId: string, qty: number) => {
    setStockSellAmounts(prev => ({ ...prev, [resId]: qty }));
  };

  const pendingProdSales = (state.pendingSales ?? []).filter(s => s.resId.startsWith('prod_'));
  const undoCount = state.undoStack?.length ?? 0;

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
      {/* NAGŁÓWEK I ZASOBY */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold uppercase tracking-wide">🏭 PRODUKCJA</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
            Zakład: <strong className="text-stone-900">{prodActivityName}</strong>
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0">
          {undoCount > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => undoLastAction()}
              className="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 border border-amber-400 text-amber-950 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition"
            >
              ↩ Cofnij ostatni ruch
            </button>
          )}
          <div className="bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2 shadow-sm text-right">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">Wolna załoga</div>
            <div className={`text-base font-bold font-mono ${availableWorkers === 0 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-amber-800'}`}>
              {availableWorkers} / {totalWorkers} os.
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2 shadow-sm text-right flex items-center gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">Magazyn</div>
              <div className={`text-base font-bold font-mono ${freeWarehouseSpace === 0 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-stone-900'}`}>
                {currentStock} / {capacity} szt.
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={rentWarehouse}
              disabled={state.company.finances.stanKonta < warehouseRentCost}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 border border-stone-300 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold text-stone-800 disabled:opacity-40"
              title={`Powiększ magazyn o +50 szt. za ${formatMoney(warehouseRentCost, state)}`}
            >
              +50 szt. ({formatMoney(warehouseRentCost, state)})
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CZYNNIKI RYNKOWE — WIDOCZNE WYŁĄCZNIE W TRYBIE DEBUG */}
      {debugMode && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 text-amber-950 rounded-lg p-3.5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">

                Czynniki ceny zbytu

            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-2 text-xs">

              hasUopBonus ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900' : 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-600'
            }`}

        </div>

        {/* KROK A: SKŁADNIK (LOSOWA PULA W DANYM KWARTALE) */}
        <div>

            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-700">

                      ? 'border-stone-900 bg-amber-50/40 shadow-sm'
                      : tool.owned
                        ? 'border-stone-200 bg-white'
                        : 'border-dashed border-stone-300 bg-stone-50'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-1 mb-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-sm text-stone-900">{tool.name}</span>
                      {tool.owned && (
                        <span
                          className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold shrink-0 ${
                            isSelected ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {isSelected ? 'AKTYWNE' : 'POSIADANE'}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-2.5">{tool.desc}</p>
                    <div className="text-xs space-y-0.5 mb-3">
                      <div className="flex justify-between">
                        <span className="text-stone-500">Wydajność:</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-stone-900">{tool.unitsPerWorker} szt. / os.</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between">
                        <span className="text-stone-500">Premia jakości:</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-amber-800">+{tool.qualityBonus}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {tool.owned ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedToolId(tool.id)}
                      className={`w-full py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-bold transition ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white'
                          : 'bg-stone-100 hover:bg-stone-200 text-stone-800 border border-stone-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isSelected ? '✓ Wybrane' : 'Wybierz'}
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <div>
                      {!tool.meetsRequirements && (
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-red-700 font-semibold mb-1.5">
                          Wymaga: {tool.missingReasons.join(', ')}
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={!canBuy}
                        onClick={() => {
                          buyProductionTool(tool.id);
                          setSelectedToolId(tool.id);
                        }}
                        className="w-full py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-bold bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white transition disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                      >
                        Kup ({formatMoney(tool.effectiveCost, state)})
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KROK C: LICZBA PRACOWNIKÓW */}
        <div className="bg-stone-50 border border-stone-200 rounded-lg p-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-700">
                Pracownicy
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500">
                Dostępni w tym kwartale: {availableWorkers} os. (wliczając Prezesa).
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={availableWorkers <= 0 || safeWorkers <= 1}
                onClick={() => setWorkersCount(w => Math.max(1, w - 1))}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded border border-stone-300 bg-white font-bold hover:bg-stone-100 disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                -
              </button>
              <span className="min-w-[64px] text-center font-mono font-bold text-base">
                {availableWorkers > 0 ? `${safeWorkers} os.` : '0 os.'}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={availableWorkers <= 0 || safeWorkers >= availableWorkers}
                onClick={() => setWorkersCount(w => Math.min(availableWorkers, w + 1))}
                className="w-8 h-8 rounded border border-stone-300 bg-white font-bold hover:bg-stone-100 disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                +
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={availableWorkers <= 0}
                onClick={() => setWorkersCount(Math.max(1, Math.floor(availableWorkers / 2)))}
                className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded border border-stone-300 bg-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-stone-100 disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                50%
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={availableWorkers <= 0}
                onClick={() => setWorkersCount(availableWorkers)}
                className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded border border-stone-900 bg-stone-900 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-stone-800 disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                Max ({availableWorkers})
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {availableWorkers > 1 && (
            <input
              type="range"
              min={1}
              max={availableWorkers}
              value={safeWorkers}
              onChange={e => setWorkersCount(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full accent-stone-900 cursor-pointer"
            />
          )}
        </div>

        {/* PODSUMOWANIE PARTII */}
        {estimate && (
          <div className="bg-amber-50/70 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-lg p-4 space-y-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
              <div className="bg-white rounded-md p-3 border border-amber-200">
                <div className="text-[11px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">
                  Orientacyjna kwota za sztukę
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-emerald-800 mt-0.5">
                  {formatMoneyRange(estimate.unitMinPrice, estimate.unitMaxPrice, state)}
                </div>
                {debugMode && (
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500">
                    DEBUG: środek {formatMoney(estimate.estimatedUnitPrice, state)} (±{estimate.spreadPercent}%)
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white rounded-md p-3 border border-amber-200">
                <div className="text-[11px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">
                  Wielkość partii
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-stone-900 mt-0.5">
                  {estimate.units} szt.
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500">
                  {safeWorkers} os. × {estimate.tool.unitsPerWorker} szt./os.
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white rounded-md p-3 border border-amber-200">
                <div className="text-[11px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">
                  Jakość wyrobu
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-amber-800 mt-0.5">
                  {estimate.quality} / 10
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500">
                  Składnik {estimate.ingredient.quality} + narzędzie +{estimate.tool.qualityBonus}
                  {hasUopBonus ? ' + UoP +1' : ''}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white rounded-md p-3 border border-amber-200">
                <div className="text-[11px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">
                  Koszt składników
                </div>
                <div className={`text-lg font-mono font-bold mt-0.5 ${canAffordBatch ? 'text-stone-900' : 'text-red-700'}`}>
                  {formatMoney(estimate.totalCost, state)}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500">
                  {formatMoney(estimate.unitCost, state)} × {estimate.units} szt.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ZBYT LUB MAGAZYN */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-md p-3.5 border border-amber-200 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-700">
                  Przeznaczenie partii ({estimate.units} szt.):
                </span>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSellPercentMode('all')}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
                      sellPercentMode === 'all'
                        ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
                        : 'bg-stone-50 hover:bg-stone-100 border-stone-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Zbyj całość
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSellPercentMode('half')}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
                      sellPercentMode === 'half'
                        ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
                        : 'bg-stone-50 hover:bg-stone-100 border-stone-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Po połowie (50% / 50%)

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSellPercentMode('store')}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
                      sellPercentMode === 'store'
                        ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
                        : 'bg-stone-50 hover:bg-stone-100 border-stone-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Do magazynu
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSellPercentMode('custom');
                      setCustomSellAmount(Math.min(estimate.units, Math.max(1, Math.floor(estimate.units / 2))));
                    }}
                    className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
                      sellPercentMode === 'custom'
                        ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
                        : 'bg-stone-50 hover:bg-stone-100 border-stone-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Własna ilość
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {sellPercentMode === 'custom' && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-1">
                  <span className="text-xs text-stone-600 shrink-0">Do zbytu:</span>
                  <input
                    type="range"
                    min={0}
                    max={estimate.units}
                    value={sellNowAmount}
                    onChange={e => setCustomSellAmount(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="flex-1 accent-stone-900"
                  />
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-xs min-w-[60px] text-right">
                    {sellNowAmount} / {estimate.units}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs pt-1 border-t border-stone-100">
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  <div>
                    Zbyt: <strong>{sellNowAmount} szt.</strong>
                    {sellNowAmount > 0 && sellNowRange && (
                      <span className="ml-1.5 font-mono font-bold text-emerald-800">
                        (ok. {formatMoneyRange(sellNowRange.min, sellNowRange.max, state)}, rozliczenie w nast. turze)
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    Do magazynu: <strong>{toStoreAmount} szt.</strong>{' '}
                    <span className={canFitInWarehouse ? 'text-stone-500' : 'text-red-700 font-bold'}>
                      (wolne miejsce: {freeWarehouseSpace} szt.)
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={
                    availableWorkers <= 0 ||
                    !canAffordBatch ||
                    !canFitInWarehouse ||
                    !activeIngredient?.meetsRequirements ||
                    !activeTool?.owned
                  }
                  onClick={() => {
                    runProductionBatch(
                      activeIngredient.id,
                      activeTool.id,
                      safeWorkers,
                      sellNowAmount
                    );
                  }}
                  className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-stone-900 hover:bg-stone-800 text-white rounded font-bold text-sm shadow transition disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shrink-0"
                >
                  {availableWorkers <= 0
                    ? 'Brak wolnej załogi'
                    : !canAffordBatch
                      ? 'Za mało gotówki'
                      : !canFitInWarehouse
                        ? 'Brak miejsca w magazynie'
                        : `⚙️ Produkuj ${estimate.units} szt. (-${formatMoney(estimate.totalCost, state)})`}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MAGAZYN PRODUKCJI */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 p-5 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 border-b border-stone-200">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">2. Magazyn produkcji</h2>
          {canSelfSell && onNavigateToSell && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onNavigateToSell}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800 text-white rounded text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition shrink-0"
            >
              💰 Sprzedawaj samodzielnie →
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {storedProducedItems.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-6 text-stone-500 text-sm bg-stone-50 rounded border border-dashed border-stone-300">
            Brak wyprodukowanych towarów w magazynie.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {storedProducedItems.map((item) => {
              const qty = getStockQty(item.resId, item.stock);
              const totalRange = getRevenueRange(
                Math.round(item.estimatedUnitPrice * qty),
                accuracy
              );

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.resId}
                  className="p-4 bg-stone-50 border border-stone-200 rounded-lg flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-4"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm text-stone-900">
                      {item.label} — Jakość {item.quality}/10
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-stone-600 mt-0.5">
                      W magazynie: <strong>{item.stock} szt.</strong> · Orientacyjnie:{' '}
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-800">
                        {formatMoneyRange(item.unitMinPrice, item.unitMaxPrice, state)} / szt.
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStockQty(item.resId, Math.max(1, Math.floor(item.stock * 0.25)))}
                        className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] bg-white border border-stone-300 rounded hover:bg-stone-100 font-semibold"
                      >
                        25%
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStockQty(item.resId, Math.max(1, Math.floor(item.stock * 0.5)))}
                        className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] bg-white border border-stone-300 rounded hover:bg-stone-100 font-semibold"
                      >
                        50%
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStockQty(item.resId, item.stock)}
                        className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] bg-white border border-stone-300 rounded hover:bg-stone-100 font-semibold"
                      >
                        Całość
                      </button>
                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min={1}
                        max={item.stock}
                        value={qty}
                        onChange={e =>
                          setStockQty(
                            item.resId,
                            Math.max(1, Math.min(item.stock, Number(e.target.value) || 1))
                          )
                        }
                        className="w-16 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono border border-stone-300 rounded bg-white text-center"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => sellProducedStock(item.resId, qty)}
                      className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white rounded text-xs font-bold transition whitespace-nowrap"
                    >
                      🚛 Zbyj {qty} szt. ({formatMoneyRange(totalRange.min, totalRange.max, state)})
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ZBYT OCZEKUJĄCY NA ROZLICZENIE */}
        {pendingProdSales.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-3.5">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-900">
                🚛 Wysłane do zbytu (rozliczenie w nast. turze):
              </div>
              {undoCount > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => undoLastAction()}
                  className="text-[11px] px-2.5 py-1 bg-white hover:bg-amber-100 border border-amber-400 text-amber-950 rounded font-bold transition"
                >
                  ↩ Cofnij ostatnią operację
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {pendingProdSales.map((sale) => {
                const fallback = getRevenueRange(sale.estimatedRevenue, accuracy);
                return (
                  <div key={sale.id} className="text-xs flex justify-between gap-2 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded border border-amber-100">
                    <span>
                      <strong>{sale.amount} szt.</strong> (Jakość {sale.quality}/10)
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-900">
                      {formatMoneyRange(sale.minRevenue ?? fallback.min, sale.maxRevenue ?? fallback.max, state)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
