import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import {
  computeRanking,
  SIZE_ORDER,
  MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES,
  getPlayerAllies,
  getPlayerParentInfo,
} from '../../engine/competition';
import type { RankingRow } from '../../engine/competition';
import { COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';
import { CompetitorProfile } from '../components/CompetitorProfile';
import type { Trend } from '../../engine/types';

type SortKey = 'rank' | 'name' | 'size' | 'value';
type SortDir = 'asc' | 'desc';
type FilterMode = 'all' | 'branch' | 'inactive';

const COLUMNS: { key: SortKey; label: string; align: 'left' | 'right' }[] = [
  { key: 'rank', label: '#', align: 'left' },
  { key: 'name', label: 'Firma', align: 'left' },
  { key: 'size', label: 'Rozmiar', align: 'left' },
  { key: 'value', label: 'Wartość / Stan konta', align: 'right' },
];

const CATEGORY_SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
  handel: 'H',
  produkcja: 'P',
  uslugi: 'U',
};

function sortValue(row: RankingRow, key: SortKey): number | string {
  switch (key) {
    case 'rank':
      return row.rank;
    case 'name':
      return row.name.toLocaleLowerCase('pl');
    case 'size':
      return SIZE_ORDER.indexOf(row.size);
    case 'value':
      return row.value;
  }
}

function TrendMark({ t }: { t: Trend }) {
  if (t === 1) return <span aria-label="Wzrost">⬆️</span>;
  if (t === -1) return <span aria-label="Spadek">⬇️</span>;
  return <span aria-label="Bez zmian" className="opacity-40">➡️</span>;
}

export function RankingsScreen() {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<{ key: SortKey; dir: SortDir }>({ key: 'rank', dir: 'asc' });
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<FilterMode>('all');
  const [selectedCompId, setSelectedCompId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const rows = useMemo(() => (state ? computeRanking(state) : []), [state]);

  const filteredRows = useMemo(() => {
    if (filter === 'branch') {
      return rows.filter((r) => r.isPlayer || r.inPlayerBranch);
    }
    return rows;
  }, [rows, filter]);

  const sortedRows = useMemo(() => {
    const factor = sort.dir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
    return [...filteredRows].sort((a, b) => {
      const va = sortValue(a, sort.key);
      const vb = sortValue(b, sort.key);
      const cmp =
        typeof va === 'string' && typeof vb === 'string'
          ? va.localeCompare(vb, 'pl')
          : (va as number) - (vb as number);
      return cmp * factor || a.rank - b.rank;
    });
  }, [filteredRows, sort]);

  if (!state) return null;

  if (selectedCompId) {
    return (
      <CompetitorProfile
        compId={selectedCompId}
        onBack={() => setSelectedCompId(null)}
        onSelectCompany={(id) => setSelectedCompId(id)}
      />
    );
  }

  const me = rows.find((r) => r.isPlayer);
  const prevRank = state.playerPrev?.rank ?? null;
  const rankDelta = me && prevRank !== null ? prevRank - me.rank : 0;

  const allies = getPlayerAllies(state);
  const parentInfo = getPlayerParentInfo(state);
  const playerWars = (state.activeWars ?? []).filter(
    (w) => w.attackerId === 'player' || w.defenderId === 'player'
  );
  const inactiveComps = (state.competitors ?? []).filter(
    (c) => (c.status ?? 'active') !== 'active'
  );

  const toggleSort = (key: SortKey) => {
    setSort((cur) =>
      cur.key === key
        ? { key, dir: cur.dir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' }
        : { key, dir: key === 'rank' || key === 'name' ? 'asc' : 'desc' }
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between md:items-end gap-3 mb-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Rankingi i firmy</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-0.5">
            Kliknij nazwę dowolnej firmy, aby otworzyć jej profil, relacje, sojusze, wojny i przejęcia.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="bg-white border border-stone-300 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold">
            Interakcje: <span className="font-mono font-bold">{state.interactionPoints}</span>
          </span>
          <span className="bg-white border border-stone-300 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold">
            Sojusze: <span className="font-mono font-bold">{allies.length}/{MAX_PLAYER_ALLIANCES}</span>
          </span>
          {me && (
            <div className="bg-stone-900 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold">
              Twoje miejsce: {me.rank} / {rows.length}
              {rankDelta !== 0 && (
                <span className="ml-2">{rankDelta > 0 ? `⬆️ +${rankDelta}` : `⬇️ ${rankDelta}`}</span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Paski szybkiego podglądu aktywnych wojen lub spółki-matki */}
      {(playerWars.length > 0 || parentInfo) && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4">
          {playerWars.map((w) => {
            const enemyId = w.attackerId === 'player' ? w.defenderId : w.attackerId;
            const enemy = state.competitors.find((c) => c.id === enemyId);
            if (!enemy) return null;
            return (
              <button
                key={w.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedCompId(enemy.id)}
                className="text-xs bg-red-100 hover:bg-red-200 border border-red-300 text-red-950 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold transition"
              >
                ⚔️ Wojna z {enemy.name} (stan: {w.warScore > 0 ? `+${w.warScore}` : w.warScore}) →
              </button>
            );
          })}
          {parentInfo && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSelectedCompId(parentInfo.parent.id)}
              className="text-xs bg-purple-100 hover:bg-purple-200 border border-purple-300 text-purple-950 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold transition"
            >
              👑 Spółka-matka: {parentInfo.parent.name} ({parentInfo.percent}% udziałów, irytacja {Math.round(state.company.parentDispleasure ?? 0)}/100) →
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Filtry */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setFilter('all')}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
            filter === 'all'
              ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
              : 'bg-white text-stone-700 border-stone-300 hover:bg-stone-50'
          }`}
        >
          Wszystkie firmy ({rows.length})
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setFilter('branch')}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
            filter === 'branch'
              ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
              : 'bg-white text-stone-700 border-stone-300 hover:bg-stone-50'
          }`}
        >
          Moja branża ({rows.filter((r) => r.isPlayer || r.inPlayerBranch).length})
        </button>
        {inactiveComps.length > 0 && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter('inactive')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
              filter === 'inactive'
                ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
                : 'bg-white text-stone-700 border-stone-300 hover:bg-stone-50'
            }`}
          >
            Przejęte / Bankruci ({inactiveComps.length})
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Tabela aktywnych firm lub archiwum */}
      {filter === 'inactive' ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 divide-y divide-stone-100">
          {inactiveComps.map((c) => (
            <button
              key={c.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSelectedCompId(c.id)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-stone-50 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition"
            >
              <div>
                <div className="font-semibold text-sm text-stone-900">{c.name}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">
                  {c.status === 'bankrupt'
                    ? '💀 Zbankrutowała'
                    : c.acquiredBy === 'player'
                      ? `🏛️ Przejęta przez Ciebie${c.subsidiaryIncome ? ` (+${formatMoney(c.subsidiaryIncome, state)}/kw.)` : ''}`
                      : '🏛️ Przejęta przez rywala'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-semibold">Profil →</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-stone-200 overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[520px]">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-stone-200 text-stone-600">
                {COLUMNS.map((col) => {
                  const active = sort.key === col.key;
                  return (
                    <th
                      key={col.key}
                      scope="col"
                      aria-sort={active ? (sort.dir === 'asc' ? 'ascending' : 'descending') : 'none'}
                      className={`p-0 font-semibold ${col.align === 'right' ? 'text-right' : 'text-left'}`}
                    >
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => toggleSort(col.key)}
                        className={`w-full px-3 py-2.5 hover:bg-stone-50 ${
                          col.align === 'right' ? 'text-right' : 'text-left'
                        } ${active ? 'text-stone-900' : ''}`}

                        {col.label}
                        {active && <span className="ml-1">{sort.dir === 'asc' ? '▲' : '▼'}</span>}
                      </button>
                    </th>
                  );
                })}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {sortedRows.map((row) => (

                  key={row.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (!row.isPlayer) setSelectedCompId(row.id);
                  }}
                  className={`border-b border-stone-100 last:border-b-0 transition ${
                    row.isPlayer

                      : 'hover:bg-stone-50 cursor-pointer'
                  }`}
                >

                          row.isPlayer
                            ? 'text-stone-950 font-bold'
                            : 'text-stone-900 font-medium underline-offset-2 hover:underline'
                        }

                        {row.name}
                      </span>

                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-200 text-amber-950">
                          Ty
                        </span>
                      )}

                          ⚔️ Wojna

                      )}

                  </td>

import { useGameStore, SLOT_IDS, MAX_SLOTS } from '../../store/gameStore';
import type { SaveSlot } from '../../store/autosave';

export function SavesScreen() {
  const {
    savesMode, pendingSlotId, savesVersion,
    setScreen, setPendingSlotId,
    loadGame, deleteSave, getSlots,
  } = useGameStore();

  // savesVersion wymusza rerender po delete/save
  void savesVersion;

  const slots = getSlots();
  const title = savesMode === 'new' ? 'Wybierz slot na nową grę' : 'Wczytaj zapis';

  const formatDate = (ts: number) => {
    const d = new Date(ts);
    const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${pad(d.getDate())}.${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}.${d.getFullYear()} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
  };

  const handleClick = (slotId: string) => {
    const slot = slots[slotId];
    if (savesMode === 'new') {
      if (slot) {
        const confirmed = confirm(`Slot "${slot.name}" jest zajęty (${slot.state.year} Q${slot.state.quarter}). Nadpisać?`);
        if (!confirmed) return;
      }
      setPendingSlotId(slotId);
      setScreen('start');
    } else {
      if (!slot) return;
      loadGame(slotId);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = (e: React.MouseEvent, slotId: string) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const slot = slots[slotId];
    if (!slot) return;
    const confirmed = confirm(`Usunąć zapis "${slot.name}"? Nie da się cofnąć.`);
    if (confirmed) deleteSave(slotId);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 p-6 flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="max-w-3xl w-full bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg p-6 md:p-8">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{title}</h1>
          <button
            onClick={() => setScreen('menu')}
            className="text-sm text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-900"
          >
            ← Powrót
          </button>
        </div>

        {savesMode === 'new' && pendingSlotId && (
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mb-3">
            Wybrano slot — kontynuuj w ekranie startowym.
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {SLOT_IDS.map((slotId, idx) => {
            const slot: SaveSlot | null = slots[slotId] ?? null;
            return (
              <div
                key={slotId}
                className={`border-2 rounded-lg p-4 transition ${
                  slot
                    ? savesMode === 'load'
                      ? 'border-stone-300 hover:border-stone-900 cursor-pointer'
                      : 'border-stone-300 hover:border-stone-900'
                    : 'border-dashed border-stone-300'
                }`}
                onClick={() => handleClick(slotId)}
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-stone-500 font-semibold">
                    Slot {idx + 1}
                  </div>
                  {slot && (
                    <button
                      onClick={(e) => handleDelete(e, slotId)}
                      className="text-xs text-red-600 hover:text-red-800"
                    >
                      Usuń
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {slot ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="font-bold text-stone-900">
                      {slot.state.company.name}{slot.state.company.suffix}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-1">
                      Rok {slot.state.year} Q{slot.state.quarter} · {slot.state.phase}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 font-mono">
                      Ostatnio: {formatDate(slot.lastPlayed)}
                    </div>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <div className="text-sm text-stone-400 italic">
                    Pusty slot
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-stone-400 mt-4 text-center">
          Maksymalnie {MAX_SLOTS} zapisów.
          {savesMode === 'load' && !Object.keys(slots).length && ' Brak zapisów do wczytania.'}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { generateTradeBuyers, getRevenueRange } from '../../engine/tradeHelper';
import { estimateStoredProducedItem } from '../../engine/productionHelper';
import {
  getCompanyActivities,
  canSelfSellProducedGoods,
} from '../../engine/activityHelper';
import { calculateInstallmentPrice } from '../../engine/actions';
import { formatMoneyRange } from '../../engine/currency';
import { scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import type { InstallmentOption, PendingSale } from '../../engine/types';

const INSTALLMENT_OPTIONS: InstallmentOption[] = [1, 2, 6, 12, 24];

export function SellScreen() {
  const { state, sellGoods } = useGameStore();
  // Osobny stan rat dla każdej pary (odbiorca + towar): klucz `${buy.id}_${resId}`
  const [installmentsMap, setInstallmentsMap] = useState<Record<string, InstallmentOption>>({});

  if (!state) return null;

  const accuracy = state.company.reputation.dokladnoscPrzewidywan ?? 50;
  const companyActivities = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  const canSelfSellProduction = canSelfSellProducedGoods(companyActivities);

  const buyers = useMemo(() => {
    return generateTradeBuyers(state);
  }, [
    state.seed,
    state.turn,
    state.company.reputation,
    state.company.activity,
    state.company.activities,
    state.macroStats?.development,
    state.competitors,
  ]);

  const allNonRawEntries = Object.entries(state.company.resources || {}).filter(
    ([resId, amount]) => !resId.startsWith('raw_') && (amount as number) > 0
  );

  const blockedProducedCount = canSelfSellProduction
    ? 0
    : allNonRawEntries
        .filter(([resId]) => resId.startsWith('prod_'))
        .reduce((sum, [, amt]) => sum + (amt as number), 0);

  const stockEntries = allNonRawEntries.filter(([resId]) => {
    if (resId.startsWith('prod_')) return canSelfSellProduction;
    return true;
  });

  const getInst = (key: string): InstallmentOption => installmentsMap[key] ?? 1;
  const setInst = (key: string, val: InstallmentOption) =>
    setInstallmentsMap(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: val }));

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mb-6 gap-2">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">
            💰 Sprzedawaj {canSelfSellProduction ? 'Towar i Wyroby Własne' : 'Towar'} (Odbiorcy)
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
            Przy kontrahentach widnieje orientacyjny przedział przychodu. Warunki ratalne ustalasz osobno dla każdej transakcji.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {blockedProducedCount > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-950 rounded-lg p-3.5 mb-6 text-xs sm:text-sm">
          ℹ W magazynie znajduje się <strong>{blockedProducedCount} szt.</strong> wyrobów z własnej produkcji, ale obecny profil handlowy nie pasuje do profilu produkcyjnego — możesz zbyć je hurtowo na ekranie <b>🏭 Produkcja</b>.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TRANSAKCJE W TOKU */}
      {state.pendingSales?.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-900 mb-2">
            🚚 Zamówienia wysłane do kontrahentów (ostateczne rozliczenie w wiadomości w następnej turze)
          </h2>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            {state.pendingSales.map((sale: PendingSale) => {
              const fallback = getRevenueRange(sale.estimatedRevenue, accuracy);
              const minRev = sale.minRevenue ?? fallback.min;
              const maxRev = sale.maxRevenue ?? fallback.max;

              return (
                <div
                  key={sale.id}
                  className="text-xs sm:text-sm bg-white p-2.5 rounded shadow-sm border border-amber-100 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-1"
                >
                  <span>
                    Sprzedaż <strong>{sale.amount} szt.</strong> (Jakość {sale.quality}) do: <strong>{sale.buyerName}</strong>
                    {sale.installments && sale.installments > 1 && (
                      <span className="ml-2 text-stone-500 text-xs">({sale.installments} raty)</span>
                    )}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-800">
                    Orientacyjnie: {formatMoneyRange(minRev, maxRev, state)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Lista odbiorców */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {buyers.map((buy) => {
          const isCompetitor = buy.id.startsWith('comp_');
          const minReqQuality = buy.req?.minQuality ?? 1;

          return (
            <div
              key={buy.id}
              className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border p-4 flex flex-col justify-between ${
                isCompetitor ? 'border-amber-300 bg-amber-50/20' : 'border-stone-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2 mb-1">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-stone-900">{buy.name}</h3>
                  {isCompetitor && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase">
                      Konkurencja
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-3 min-h-[32px]">{buy.desc}</p>

                <div className="bg-stone-50 border border-stone-200 rounded p-2.5 mb-4 space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="text-stone-500">Mnożnik zbytu:</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-green-700">x{buy.multiplier.toFixed(2)}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="text-stone-500">Wymagana jakość:</span>
                    <span className={`font-bold ${minReqQuality > 1 ? 'text-amber-800' : 'text-stone-600'}`}>
                      {minReqQuality > 1 ? `Min. ${minReqQuality}/10` : 'Dowolna (brak wymagań)'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {buy.complaints && (
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-red-600 italic">
                      ⚠ Ryzyko reklamacji przy marnej jakości!
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-stone-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
                  {canSelfSellProduction ? 'Towary i wyroby własne w magazynie:' : 'Zmagazynowane towary:'}
                </div>

                {stockEntries.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 italic bg-stone-50 p-3 rounded text-center border border-dashed border-stone-200">
                    Brak towarów do sprzedaży. Sprowadź towar w zakładce KUPUJ
                    {canSelfSellProduction ? ' lub złóż wyroby w magazynie na ekranie PRODUKCJA.' : '.'}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                    {stockEntries.map(([resId, amount]) => {
                      const amt = amount as number;
                      const isProduced = resId.startsWith('prod_');

                      let quality = 1;
                      let baseVal = 100;
                      let itemTitle = '';

                      if (isProduced) {
                        const prodInfo = estimateStoredProducedItem(state, resId);
                        quality = prodInfo.quality;
                        baseVal = prodInfo.retailBaseUnitPrice;
                        itemTitle = `🏭 ${prodInfo.label}`;
                      } else {
                        const parts = resId.split('_');
                        quality = parseInt(parts[parts.length - 1], 10) || 1;
                        baseVal = Math.max(5, scaleByInflation(quality * 120 + 80, state));
                        itemTitle = '📦 Towar';
                      }

                      const paymentKey = `${buy.id}_${resId}`;
                      const itemInstallments = getInst(paymentKey);

                      const estUnit = calculateInstallmentPrice(
                        Math.round(baseVal * buy.multiplier),
                        itemInstallments
                      );
                      const unitRange = getRevenueRange(estUnit, accuracy);
                      const isRefused = quality < minReqQuality;

                      return (
                        <div
                          key={resId}
                          className={`p-2.5 rounded border text-xs ${
                            isRefused ? 'bg-red-50/40 border-red-200' : 'bg-stone-50 border-stone-200'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1 gap-2">
                            <span className="font-semibold text-stone-800">
                              {itemTitle} · J.{quality}/10 ({amt} szt.)
                            </span>
                            {isRefused && (
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-red-700 font-bold uppercase bg-red-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded shrink-0">
                                Odrzuca jakość
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {isRefused ? (
                            <div className="text-[11px] text-red-600 italic">
                              Kontrahent nie przyjmuje partii o jakości poniżej {minReqQuality}.
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <div className="text-[11px] text-stone-600 mb-2">
                                Orientacyjnie za 1 szt.:{' '}
                                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-green-800">
                                  {formatMoneyRange(unitRange.min, unitRange.max, state)}

                                <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-1">
                                  {INSTALLMENT_OPTIONS.map((inst) => (
                                    <button
                                      key={inst}
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() => setInst(paymentKey, inst)}
                                      className={`py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold border transition ${
                                        itemInstallments === inst
                                          ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white border-stone-900'
                                          : 'bg-white text-stone-700 border-stone-300 hover:bg-stone-100'
                                      }`}
                                    >
                                      {inst === 1 ? '1x' : `${inst}r.`}
                                    </button>
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              </div>

                              <div className="flex gap-1.5 pt-1">
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => sellGoods(buy, resId, 1, baseVal, itemInstallments)}
                                  disabled={amt < 1}
                                  className="flex-1 bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800 text-white py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-40"
                                >
                                  Sprzedaj 1x
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => sellGoods(buy, resId, 10, baseVal, itemInstallments)}
                                  disabled={amt < 10}

import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import {
  getAvailableServices,
  getTotalServiceWorkerSlots,
  getBusyServiceWorkers,
} from '../../engine/servicesHelper';
import { formatMoney, formatMoneyRange } from '../../engine/currency';

export function ServicesScreen() {
  const { state, toggleService } = useGameStore();

  if (!state) return null;

  const totalSlots = getTotalServiceWorkerSlots(state);
  const busySlots = getBusyServiceWorkers(state);
  const freeSlots = Math.max(0, totalSlots - busySlots);

  const services = useMemo(
    () => getAvailableServices(state),
    [
      state.company.size,
      state.company.employees,
      state.company.reputation,
      state.company.activity,
      state.company.activities,
      state.macroStats?.development,
      state.macroStats?.priceIndex,
      state.activeServiceIds,
    ]
  );

  const activeServices = services.filter(s => s.isActive);
  const totalMinPayout = activeServices.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.minPayout, 0);
  const totalMaxPayout = activeServices.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.maxPayout, 0);

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6">
      {/* Nagłówek i zasoby kadrowe */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mb-6 gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">🛎️ Katalog Świadczonych Usług</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
            Wybierz usługi oferowane na stałe przez Twoją firmę. Dostępność usług zależy od reputacji, rozmiaru firmy oraz liczby zatrudnionych pracowników.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 shadow-sm text-right shrink-0">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-stone-500 uppercase font-semibold">Wolne moce kadrowe</div>
          <div className={`text-lg font-bold font-mono ${freeSlots === 0 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-purple-800'}`}>
            {freeSlots} / {totalSlots} osób
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* PODSUMOWANIE AKTYWNYCH USŁUG */}
      <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-3">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-purple-950">
            📋 Aktywne usługi stałe: {activeServices.length}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-purple-800">
            Przychód ze stałych usług rozliczany jest automatycznie na początku każdego kwartału.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
          <div className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-purple-700">
            Łączny orientacyjny przychód / kwartał
          </div>
          <div className="font-mono font-bold text-base text-green-700">
            {activeServices.length > 0
              ? formatMoneyRange(totalMinPayout, totalMaxPayout, state)
              : formatMoney(0, state)}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* KATALOG USŁUG DO WYBORU */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
        {services.map(srv => {
          const hasEnoughSlots = freeSlots >= srv.workersRequired;
          const canActivate = srv.meetsRequirements && hasEnoughSlots;
          const riskPercent = Math.round(srv.risk * 100);

          return (
            <div
              key={srv.id}
              className={`bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border p-5 flex flex-col justify-between transition ${
                srv.isActive
                  ? 'border-purple-600 ring-1 ring-purple-600 bg-purple-50/10'
                  : !srv.meetsRequirements
                    ? 'border-stone-200 opacity-75'
                    : 'border-stone-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2 mb-2">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-stone-900">{srv.title}</h3>
                  {srv.isActive ? (
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-purple-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase shrink-0">
                      Aktywna na stałe
                    </span>
                  ) : !srv.meetsRequirements ? (
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-stone-200 text-stone-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase shrink-0">
                      Zablokowana
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold uppercase shrink-0">
                      Dostępna
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-4">{srv.desc}</p>

                <div className="bg-stone-50 border border-stone-200 rounded p-3 mb-4 space-y-1.5 text-xs">
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-stone-500">Orientacyjny dochód / kw.:</span>
                    <span className="font-mono font-bold text-green-700">
                      {formatMoneyRange(srv.minPayout, srv.maxPayout, state)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-stone-500">Angażuje kadrę:</span>
                    <span
                      className={`font-bold ${
                        srv.isActive || hasEnoughSlots ? 'text-stone-900' : 'text-red-600'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {srv.workersRequired} {srv.workersRequired === 1 ? 'etat' : 'etaty'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-stone-500">Wpływ na reputację:</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-blue-700">+{srv.repGain} rep. branży / kw.</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between">
                    <span className="text-stone-500">Ryzyko reklamacji:</span>
                    <span className={`font-semibold ${riskPercent >= 25 ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-stone-600'}`}>
                      {riskPercent}%
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Niespełnione wymagania */}
                {!srv.meetsRequirements && srv.missingReasons.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded p-2.5 mb-4 text-xs text-amber-900">
                    <div className="font-bold mb-1">Wymagania do odblokowania:</div>
                    <ul className="list-disc list-inside space-y-0.5">
                      {srv.missingReasons.map((reason, idx) => (
                        <li key={idx}>{reason}</li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {srv.isActive ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => toggleService(srv.id)}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded text-xs font-bold transition shadow-sm bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white"
                >
                  Wstrzymaj świadczenie usługi (zwolnij {srv.workersRequired} os.)
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  onClick={() => toggleService(srv.id)}
                  disabled={!canActivate}
                  className={`w-full py-2.5 rounded text-xs font-bold transition shadow-sm ${
                    !srv.meetsRequirements
                      ? 'bg-stone-200 text-stone-500 cursor-not-allowed'
                      : !hasEnoughSlots
                        ? 'bg-stone-200 text-stone-500 cursor-not-allowed'
                        : 'bg-purple-700 hover:bg-purple-800 text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  {!srv.meetsRequirements
                    ? 'Nie spełniasz wymagań'
                    : !hasEnoughSlots
                      ? `Brak wolnych rąk do pracy (wym. ${srv.workersRequired})`
                      : 'Uruchom usługę na stałe'}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { DIFFICULTY_DESCRIPTIONS, COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG } from '../../engine/constants';
import {
  computeStartingActivityStats,
  getRequiredWorkers,
  getCompanyPrefix,
  formatCompanyName,
  canSelfSellProducedGoods,
} from '../../engine/activityHelper';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';
import type { Difficulty, CompanySize, ActivityCategory } from '../../engine/types';

const SUFFIXES = ['', '-EX', '-POL'];
const SIZES: CompanySize[] = ['micro', 'small', 'medium'];
const CATEGORIES = (branchesData as ActivityCategory[]).map(cat => ({
  ...cat,
  activities: cat.activities.filter(act => !act.locked),
}));

export function StartScreen() {
  const { newGame, setScreen, pendingSlotId } = useGameStore();

  const [difficulty, setDifficulty] = useState<Difficulty>('medium');
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState('Moja Firma');
  const [suffix, setSuffix] = useState('');
  const [usePrefix, setUsePrefix] = useState(true);
  const [size, setSize] = useState<CompanySize>('micro');
  const [inPZPR, setInPZPR] = useState(false);

  const [checkedCategories, setCheckedCategories] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    handel: true,
    produkcja: false,
    uslugi: false,
  });

  const [subByCategory, setSubByCategory] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
    const init: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const cat of CATEGORIES) {
      if (cat.activities[0]) {
        init[cat.id] = cat.activities[0].id;
      }
    }
    return init;
  });

  const activeCategoryIds = CATEGORIES.map(c => c.id).filter(id => checkedCategories[id]);
  const selectedActivities = activeCategoryIds
    .map(catId => subByCategory[catId])
    .filter(Boolean);

  const toggleCategory = (catId: string) => {
    setCheckedCategories(prev => {
      const nextValue = !prev[catId];
      const nextState = { ...prev, [catId]: nextValue };

      const activeCount = Object.values(nextState).filter(Boolean).length;
      if (activeCount === 0) {
        return prev;
      }
      return nextState;
    });
  };

  const { branza, mafia, related } = computeStartingActivityStats(selectedActivities);
  const requiredWorkers = getRequiredWorkers(size, activeCategoryIds.length);
  const prefix = getCompanyPrefix(selectedActivities);
  const fullNamePreview = formatCompanyName({
    name: companyName.trim() || 'Moja Firma',
    suffix,
    usePrefix,
    activity: selectedActivities[0] ?? '',
    activities: selectedActivities,
  });

  const hasBothProdAndTrade =
    activeCategoryIds.includes('produkcja') && activeCategoryIds.includes('handel');
  const canSelfSell = hasBothProdAndTrade && canSelfSellProducedGoods(selectedActivities);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 p-6 flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="max-w-4xl w-full bg-white rounded-lg shadow-lg p-6 md:p-8">
        <button
          onClick={() => setScreen('menu')}
          className="text-sm text-stone-500 hover:text-stone-900 mb-4"
        >
          ← Powrót do menu
        </button>

        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-1">Nowa gra</h1>
        <p className="text-stone-600 mb-6">1 stycznia 1989. Reforma Wilczka wchodzi w życie.</p>

        {/* Nazwa firmy */}
        <h2 className="font-bold mb-2">Nazwa firmy</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-row gap-2 mb-2">
          {usePrefix && prefix && (
            <div className="px-3 py-2 rounded border border-stone-300 bg-stone-100 font-bold text-stone-900 flex items-center shrink-0">
              {prefix}
            </div>
          )}
          <input
            value={companyName}
            onChange={e => setCompanyName(e.target.value)}
            className="flex-1 min-w-0 border p-2 rounded"
            placeholder="np. Inter-Fruit"
          />
          <select
            value={suffix}
            onChange={e => setSuffix(e.target.value)}
            className="w-[110px] sm:w-32 shrink-0 border p-2 rounded bg-white"
          >
            {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
              <option key={s} value={s}>{s || '(bez sufiksu)'}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-6 text-xs text-stone-600">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={usePrefix}
              onChange={e => setUsePrefix(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 accent-stone-900 cursor-pointer"
            />
            <span>Dodawaj automatyczny skrót profilu przed nazwą ({prefix || 'P...'})</span>
          </label>
          <span>
            Podgląd: <strong className="text-stone-900">{fullNamePreview}</strong>
          </span>
        </div>
            
        {/* Działalność (3 niezależne filary) */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between mb-2">
          <h2 className="font-bold">Działalność (Handel, Produkcja, Usługi)</h2>
          <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">
            Możesz łączyć dowolne filary (od 1 do 3 naraz)
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3 mb-3">
          {CATEGORIES.map(cat => {
            const isChecked = !!checkedCategories[cat.id];
            const isLastActive = isChecked && activeCategoryIds.length <= 1;

            return (
              <div
                key={cat.id}
                className={`border rounded-lg p-3 transition ${
                  isChecked ? 'border-stone-900 bg-stone-50' : 'border-stone-200 bg-white opacity-70'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-3">
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold cursor-pointer min-w-[140px]">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={isChecked}
                      disabled={isLastActive}
                      onChange={() => toggleCategory(cat.id)}
                      className="w-4 h-4 accent-stone-900 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                    />
                    <span>{cat.name}</span>
                  </label>

                  {isChecked ? (
                    <select
                      value={subByCategory[cat.id] ?? ''}
                      onChange={e =>
                        setSubByCategory(prev => ({ ...prev, [cat.id]: e.target.value }))
                      }
                      className="flex-1 border p-2 rounded bg-white text-sm"
                    >
                      {cat.activities.map(act => (
                        <option key={act.id} value={act.id}>
                          {act.name} (Reputacja: {act.rep}, Mafia: {act.mafia})
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-xs text-stone-400 italic">
                      Zaznacz, aby dodać filar: {cat.name}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Podsumowanie synergii i wymogów */}
        <div
          className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-xs mb-6 space-y-1.5 ${
            related
              ? 'bg-stone-100 border-stone-300 text-stone-800'
              : 'bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-900'
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              {selectedActivities.length === 1 ? (
                <span>Wybrano <b>1 filar</b> działalności (skrót: <b>{prefix}</b>).</span>
              ) : related ? (
                <span>
                  ✓ <b>Spójny profil działalności ({prefix}):</b> wybrane branże są ze sobą powiązane (brak kary do reputacji).
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span>
                  ⚠ <b>Niespójny profil ({prefix}):</b> niepowiązane podkategorie dają <b>-20%</b> do reputacji branżowej i ustawiają startową opinię u mafii na <b>50%</b>.
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-4 font-semibold shrink-0">
              <span>Reputacja branży: {branza}%</span>
              <span>Mafia (start): {mafia}%</span>
              <span>Wymagani pracownicy: {requiredWorkers}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {hasBothProdAndTrade && (
            <div className={`pt-1 border-t ${related ? 'border-stone-200' : 'border-amber-200'}`}>
              {canSelfSell ? (
                <span className="text-emerald-800 font-semibold">
                  ✓ Profil handlowy pasuje do produkcji — własne wyroby będziesz mógł sprzedawać także samodzielnie w zakładce Sprzedawaj.
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span>
                  ℹ Wybrany profil handlowy nie pokrywa się branżowo z produkcją — własne wyroby zbędziesz hurtowo na ekranie Produkcja.
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Rozmiar firmy */}
        <h2 className="font-bold mb-2">Rozmiar firmy</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-6">
          {SIZES.map(sz => (
            <button
              key={sz}
              onClick={() => setSize(sz)}
              className={`py-2 rounded border text-center font-semibold text-sm ${
                size === sz ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-stone-50'
              }`}
            >
              {COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[sz].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Poziom trudności */}
        <h2 className="font-bold mb-2">Poziom trudności</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-6">
          {(['easy', 'medium', 'hard'] as Difficulty[]).map(d => (
            <button
              key={d}
              onClick={() => setDifficulty(d)}
              className={`py-2 rounded border text-center font-semibold text-sm ${
                difficulty === d ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-stone-50'
              }`}
            >
              {DIFFICULTY_DESCRIPTIONS[d].title}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Przynależność polityczna */}
        <h2 className="font-bold mb-2">Przynależność polityczna</h2>
        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-8">
          <button
            onClick={() => setInPZPR(false)}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded border font-semibold text-sm ${
              !inPZPR ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-stone-50'
            }`}
          >
            Bezpartyjny
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setInPZPR(true)}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded border font-semibold text-sm ${
              inPZPR ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-stone-50'
            }`}
          >
            Członek PZPR
          </button>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => {
            if (!pendingSlotId) {
              alert('Nie wybrano slotu. Wróć do menu i wybierz slot.');
              return;
            }
            newGame(
              {
                difficulty,
                inPZPR,
                activity: selectedActivities.join(','),
                activities: selectedActivities,
                companyName: companyName.trim() || 'Moja Firma',
                suffix,
                usePrefix,
                size,
              },
              pendingSlotId
            );
          }}
          className="w-full bg-stone-900 text-white py-3 rounded text-lg font-bold hover:bg-stone-800 transition"
        >
          Zacznij: 1 stycznia 1989 →
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { useSettingsStore } from '../../store/settingsStore';
import { formatMoney } from '../../engine/currency';
import { getInflationRate, getLoanQuarterlyRate, scaleByInflation } from '../../engine/economy';
import { StatBar } from '../components/StatBar';
import { WPLYWY_LABELS, WYDATKI_LABELS, REPUTATION_LABELS, REPUTATION_DESCRIPTIONS } from '../ui-labels';

export function StatisticsScreen() {
  const { state, takeLoan } = useGameStore();
  const showHidden = useSettingsStore(s => s.showHiddenStats);

  if (!state) return null;
  const f = state.company.finances;
  const r = state.company.reputation;

  const inflationRate = getInflationRate(state);
  const bankRatePct = (getLoanQuarterlyRate('bank', inflationRate) * 100).toFixed(1);
  const greyRatePct = (getLoanQuarterlyRate('szara_strefa', inflationRate) * 100).toFixed(1);
  const loanAmount = scaleByInflation(20000, state);

  const visibleRep: (keyof typeof r)[] = [
    'branza',
    'opiniaPubliczna',
    'opiniaBranza',
    'opiniaPracownicy',
  ];
  const hiddenRep: (keyof typeof r)[] = [
    'opiniaMafia',
    'opiniaMedia',
    'opiniaPolitycy',
    'opiniaSkarbowa',
    'dokladnoscPrzewidywan',
  ];

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Statystyki Firmy</h1>

      {/* FINANSE */}
      <section className="mb-8">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-1">Finanse</h2>
        <p className="text-2xl font-mono mb-4">{formatMoney(f.stanKonta, state)}</p>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-6">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold mb-3 text-green-800">Wpływy</h3>
            {Object.entries(f.wplywy).map(([k, v]) => (
              <div key={k} className="flex justify-between text-sm border-b border-stone-100 py-1.5">
                <span className="text-stone-700">{WPLYWY_LABELS[k] ?? k}</span>
                <span className="font-mono">{formatMoney(v as number, state)}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold mb-3 text-red-800">Wydatki</h3>
            {Object.entries(f.wydatki).map(([k, v]) => (
              <div key={k} className="flex justify-between text-sm border-b border-stone-100 py-1.5">
                <span className="text-stone-700">{WYDATKI_LABELS[k] ?? k}</span>
                <span className="font-mono">{formatMoney(v as number, state)}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <h3 className="font-bold mb-1">Kredyty</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mb-3">
          Oprocentowanie nowych pożyczek zależy od bieżącej inflacji ({inflationRate.toFixed(1)}% r/r).
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-4">
          <button
            onClick={() => takeLoan('bank', loanAmount)}
            className="border px-4 py-2 rounded bg-white hover:bg-stone-50"
          >
            Kredyt bankowy (+{formatMoney(loanAmount, state)}, {bankRatePct}% / kw.)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => takeLoan('szara_strefa', loanAmount)}
            className="border px-4 py-2 rounded bg-white hover:bg-stone-50"
          >
            Kredyt z szarej strefy (+{formatMoney(loanAmount, state)}, {greyRatePct}% / kw.)
          </button>
        </div>
        {state.company.loans.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4">
            <h4 className="font-semibold mb-3">Aktywne kredyty i zobowiązania</h4>
            {state.company.loans.map(loan => (
              <div key={loan.id} className="text-sm border-b border-stone-100 py-1.5 flex justify-between gap-4 last:border-b-0">
                <span>
                  {loan.source === 'bank'
                    ? 'Bank'
                    : loan.source === 'zakup_raty'
                      ? 'Zakup na raty'
                      : 'Szara strefa'}
                  {loan.interestRate > 0 ? ` (${(loan.interestRate * 100).toFixed(1)}%/kw.)` : ''}
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono">
                  Pozostało: {formatMoney(loan.remaining, state)} ({loan.quartersLeft} kw.)
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* REPUTACJA */}
      <section>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-1">Reputacja</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-4">
          Reputacja ogólna: <b>{Math.round(r.ogolna)}%</b>
        </p>

        <h3 className="font-bold mb-3 text-stone-800">Widoczne statystyki</h3>
        <div className="grid gap-4 mb-8">
          {visibleRep.map(k => (
            <div key={k} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4">
              <StatBar label={REPUTATION_LABELS[k] ?? k} value={r[k] ?? 50} />
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-2">{REPUTATION_DESCRIPTIONS[k]}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <h3 className="font-bold mb-3 text-stone-800">Statystyki ukryte</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-stone-600 mb-3">
          {showHidden
            ? 'Tryb debugowania: widzisz dokładne wartości.'
            : 'Nie widzisz dokładnych wartości — tylko przybliżony stan. Wpływają na reputację ogólną oraz dokładność szacunków.'}
        </p>
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {hiddenRep.map(k => (
            <div key={k} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4">
              <StatBar label={REPUTATION_LABELS[k] ?? k} value={r[k] ?? 50} hidden={!showHidden} />
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-2">{REPUTATION_DESCRIPTIONS[k]}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>

import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
import { makeRng } from '../../engine/rng';
import { getInflationRate, getPriceIndex, INITIAL_PRICE_INDEX } from '../../engine/economy';
import { isDenominated } from '../../engine/currency';
import branchesData from '../../data/branches.json';
import partiesData from '../../data/parties.json';
import { partyLabel } from '../ui-labels';

const IDEOLOGY_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  radical_left: 'lewica', moderate_left: 'lewica', center: 'centrum',
  moderate_right: 'prawica', radical_right: 'prawica'
};

export function StatsScreen() {
  const { state } = useGameStore();
  if (!state) return null;

  const hasTygodnik = state.company.inventory.includes('tygodnik_opinii');
  const rng = makeRng(state.seed, state.year * 13 + 7);
  const macro = state.macroStats || { unemployment: 10, development: 40 };

  const inflationRate = getInflationRate(state);
  const priceIndex = getPriceIndex(state);
  const cumulativePrices = (priceIndex / INITIAL_PRICE_INDEX).toFixed(2);
  const inflationBarWidth = Math.min(100, (inflationRate / 55) * 100);
  const inflationBarColor =
    inflationRate >= 25 ? 'bg-red-600' : inflationRate >= 10 ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-500';
  const denominated = isDenominated(state);

  const presId = state.politics.president?.partyId;
  const premId = state.politics.premierId;
  let cohesion = 0;
  let cohesionText = 'Brak rządu';

  if (presId && premId) {
    if (presId === premId) { cohesion = 100; cohesionText = 'Pełna (ta sama partia)'; }
    else {
      const presParty = (partiesData as any[]).find(p => p.id === presId);
      const premParty = (partiesData as any[]).find(p => p.id === premId);
      if (presParty && premParty) {
        const presIdeology = IDEOLOGY_MAP[presParty.ideology];
        const premIdeology = IDEOLOGY_MAP[premParty.ideology];
        if (presIdeology === premIdeology) { cohesion = 60; cohesionText = 'Umiarkowana (ta sama strona)'; }
        else { cohesion = 20; cohesionText = 'Niska (konflikt)'; }
      }
    }
  }

  const rulingParty = (partiesData as any[]).find(p => p.id === premId);
  const econView = rulingParty?.economicView ?? 0;
  const programText = econView > 0 ? `Wolnorynkowy (+${econView})` : econView < 0 ? `Socjalny (${econView})` : 'Centrowy';

  const allCategories = branchesData as any[];
  const promCat = rng.pick(allCategories);
  const unpromCat = rng.pick(allCategories.filter(c => c.id !== promCat.id));

  return (
    <div className="p-6">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">Statystyki Globalne</h1>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-6">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Sytuacja Polityczna</h2>
          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-stone-500 mb-1">Prezydent: <b className="text-stone-900">{state.politics.president?.name || 'Brak'}</b></div>
            <div className="text-sm text-stone-500 mb-3">Premier: <b className="text-stone-900">{partyLabel(premId)}</b></div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
              <span>Zgodność rządu</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{cohesionText}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded">
              <div className={`h-full rounded ${cohesion > 80 ? 'bg-green-500' : cohesion > 40 ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-red-500'}`} style={{ width: `${cohesion}%` }} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="text-sm mb-1">Program rządu: <b className="text-stone-900">{programText}</b></div>
            <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded relative">
              <div className="absolute top-0 bottom-0 w-2 bg-stone-900 rounded-full" style={{ left: `${((econView + 2) / 4) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-stone-400 mt-1">
              <span>Socjalizm</span>
              <span>Wolny Rynek</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4">Gospodarka</h2>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Rozwój kraju</span>
                <span>{macro.development}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded"><div className="h-full bg-blue-500 rounded" style={{ width: `${macro.development}%` }} /></div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Bezrobocie</span>
                <span>{macro.unemployment}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded"><div className="h-full bg-red-500 rounded" style={{ width: `${macro.unemployment}%` }} /></div>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1">
                {macro.unemployment > 15 ? 'Wysokie bezrobocie: łatwiej o tanich pracowników.' : 'Niskie bezrobocie: pracownicy będą żądać lepszych umów.'}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Inflacja (r/r)</span>
                <span className="font-mono font-semibold">{inflationRate.toFixed(1)}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2 bg-stone-200 rounded">
                <div className={`h-full rounded ${inflationBarColor}`} style={{ width: `${inflationBarWidth}%` }} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1">
                <span>Poziom cen od 1989 r.: <b>x{cumulativePrices}</b></span>
                <span>Waluta: <b>{denominated ? 'PLN (po denominacji)' : 'stare zł (PLZ)'}</b></span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-1">
                {inflationRate >= 25
                  ? 'Wysoka inflacja: drogie nowe kredyty, a gotówka na koncie szybko traci na wartości.'
                  : inflationRate >= 10
                    ? 'Umiarkowana inflacja: płace, czynsz i ceny u dostawców systematycznie rosną.'
                    : 'Stabilne ceny: niższe koszty nowych kredytów i przewidywalny rynek.'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Prognozy rynkowe</h2>
      {!hasTygodnik ? (
        <div className="bg-stone-100 border border-stone-300 p-4 rounded-lg text-center text-stone-500 text-sm">
          Kup "Tygodnik Opinii" w zakładce Biuro, aby odblokować prognozy i dodatkowe statystyki.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 p-4 rounded-lg">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-green-800 mb-1">Perspektywiczna branża</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-green-700">W tym roku rynek łaskawie patrzy na: <b>{promCat.name}</b>. Inwestycja tutaj da dodatkowe premie i oferty.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 p-4 rounded-lg">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-red-800 mb-1">Nierokująca branża</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-red-700">Unikaj w tym roku: <b>{unpromCat.name}</b>. Panuje tam zastój i trudno o zysk.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../store/gameStore';

import { PresidentialElectionScreen } from './screens/PresidentialElectionScreen';
import { NewsScreen, isCompanyInboxItem } from './screens/NewsScreen';
import { GlobalNewsScreen } from './screens/GlobalNewsScreen';
import { ChangeActivityScreen } from './screens/ChangeActivityScreen';
import { BuyScreen } from './screens/BuyScreen';
import { SellScreen } from './screens/SellScreen';
import { CombatScreen } from './screens/CombatScreen';
import { EmployeesScreen } from './screens/EmployeesScreen';
import { OfficeScreen } from './screens/OfficeScreen';
import { RankingsScreen } from './screens/RankingsScreen';
import { AchievementsScreen } from './screens/AchievementsScreen';

  getActivityFullInfo,

import type { TurnActionTarget } from '../engine/types';

type MainTab = 'main' | 'news' | 'management' | 'global';
type ManagementTab =
  | 'changeActivity'
  | 'buy'
  | 'sell'
  | 'production'
  | 'services'
  | 'party'
  | 'employees'
  | 'office'
  | 'activity'
  | 'statistics'
  | 'mafiaActivity';
type GlobalTab = 'rankings' | 'globalNews' | 'achievements' | 'stats';

const TARGET_LABELS: Record<TurnActionTarget, string> = {
  main: 'Główny',
  news: 'Wiadomości',
  changeActivity: 'Działalność',
  buy: 'Kupuj',
  sell: 'Sprzedawaj',
  production: 'Produkcja',
  services: 'Usługi',
  party: 'Polityka',
  competition: 'Rankingi',
  employees: 'Pracownicy',
  office: 'Biuro',
  activity: 'Aktywności',
  statistics: 'Statystyki',
  mafiaActivity: 'Mafia',
  rankings: 'Rankingi',
  achievements: 'Osiągnięcia',
  stats: 'Makro',
};

export function Dashboard() {
  const {
    state,
    doTurn,
    undoLastAction,
    takeEmergencyLoan,
    dismissElection,
    dismissPresidentialResult,
    debugSendTestMessage,
    debugStartTaxCombat,
    debugStartChase,
  } = useGameStore();

  const debugMode = useSettingsStore(s => s.debugMode);

  const [mainTab, setMainTab] = useState<MainTab>('main');
  const [mgmtTab, setMgmtTab] = useState<ManagementTab>('changeActivity');
  const [globalTab, setGlobalTab] = useState<GlobalTab>('rankings');
  const [showOptions, setShowOptions] = useState(false);
  const [showDebugMinigames, setShowDebugMinigames] = useState(false);
  const [turnBarExpanded, setTurnBarExpanded] = useState(false);

  if (!state) return <div className="p-8">Ładowanie...</div>;

  if (state.showIntro) return <IntroScreen />;
  if (state.lastPresidentialResult) return <PresidentialElectionScreen onClose={dismissPresidentialResult} />;
  if (state.lastElectionResult) return <ElectionScreen onClose={dismissElection} />;
  if (state.lastChoiceResult) return <ConsequenceScreen />;
  if (state.pendingEvents.length > 0) return <EventScreen />;
  if (state.activeCombat) return <CombatScreen />;
  if (state.activeChase) return <ChaseScreen />;

  if (state.gameOver) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-stone-100">
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-w-md text-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2">Koniec gry</h1>
          <p className="mb-4">{state.gameOver.reason}</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">Przetrwałeś do {state.year} Q{state.quarter}.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (state.pendingWarResolution) return <WarVictoryModal />;

  const party = partyLabel(state.ceo.currentPartyId);
  const chosenActivityLabel = state.chosenActivityThisTurn ?? 'brak';

  const inboxItems = state.news.filter(isCompanyInboxItem);
  const unreadInboxCount = inboxItems.filter(
    n => !n.read || (n.choices && !n.resolved)
  ).length;
  const unreadGlobalNewsCount = state.news.filter(
    n => !isCompanyInboxItem(n) && !n.read
  ).length;

  const companyActivities = getCompanyActivities(state.company);
  const activeCategories = getCategoryIdsForActivities(companyActivities);
  const hasTrade = activeCategories.includes('handel');
  const hasProduction = activeCategories.includes('produkcja');
  const hasServices = activeCategories.includes('uslugi');

  const displayCompanyName = formatCompanyName(state.company);
  const activityInfo = getActivityFullInfo(companyActivities);
  const sizeLabel = COMPANY_SIZE_CONFIG[state.company.size]?.label || state.company.size;

  const isInsideActivitySubscreen =
    mgmtTab === 'buy' || mgmtTab === 'sell' || mgmtTab === 'production';

  const emergencyLoanAmount = scaleByInflation(BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.amount, state);
  const emergencyLoanRatePct = (
    Math.max(
      BANKRUPT_EMERGENCY_LOAN.interestRate,
      getLoanQuarterlyRate('szara_strefa', getInflationRate(state)) + 0.03
    ) * 100
  ).toFixed(1);

  const turnActions = state.turnActions ?? [];
  const latestAction = turnActions.length > 0 ? turnActions[turnActions.length - 1] : null;
  const undoCount = state.undoStack?.length ?? 0;

  const navigateToTarget = (target: TurnActionTarget) => {
    setTurnBarExpanded(false);

    if (target === 'main') {
      setMainTab('main');
      return;
    }
    if (target === 'news') {
      setMainTab('news');
      return;
    }
    if (
      target === 'rankings' ||
      target === 'achievements' ||
      target === 'stats' ||
      target === 'competition'
    ) {
      setMainTab('global');
      setGlobalTab(target === 'competition' ? 'rankings' : target);
      return;
    }

    setMainTab('management');
    if ((target === 'buy' || target === 'sell') && !hasTrade) {
      setMgmtTab('changeActivity');
      return;
    }
    if (target === 'production' && !hasProduction) {
      setMgmtTab('changeActivity');
      return;
    }
    if (target === 'services' && !hasServices) {
      setMgmtTab('changeActivity');
      return;
    }
    if (target === 'mafiaActivity' && !state.ceo.inMafia) {
      setMainTab('main');
      return;
    }

    setMgmtTab(target as ManagementTab);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-stone-100 p-4 md:p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between md:items-end gap-3 mb-4">
        <div>
          <h1
            className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold truncate max-w-[300px] sm:max-w-md md:max-w-xl"
            title={displayCompanyName}
          >
            {displayCompanyName}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600">
            {state.year} Q{state.quarter} · {state.phase} {state.ceo.inMafia && ' · <mafia>'}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4">
          <div className="text-left md:text-right">
            <p className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-mono">
              {formatMoney(state.company.finances.stanKonta, state)}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-stone-600">
              Reputacja: {Math.round(state.company.reputation.ogolna)}%
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowOptions(true)}
            className="bg-white border px-3 py-2 rounded text-sm hover:bg-stone-50 shrink-0"
          >
            ⚙
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Pasek trybu debugowania */}
      {debugMode && (
        <div className="w-full bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 text-amber-950 rounded-lg p-2.5 mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 bg-amber-200 rounded">
            🛠 DEBUG
          </span>

          <button
            onClick={() => debugSendTestMessage()}
            className="text-xs bg-white border border-amber-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded hover:bg-amber-100 font-medium"
          >
            ✉ Wyślij testową wiadomość
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setShowDebugMinigames(v => !v)}
            className={`text-xs border border-amber-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded font-medium ${
              showDebugMinigames ? 'bg-amber-200' : 'bg-white hover:bg-amber-100'
            }`}
          >
            🎮 Minigry {showDebugMinigames ? '▲' : '▼'}
          </button>

          {showDebugMinigames && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 pl-1 border-l border-amber-300">

                  setShowDebugMinigames(false);
                  debugStartTaxCombat();

                  setShowDebugMinigames(false);
                  debugStartChase();
                }}
                className="text-xs bg-white border border-amber-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded hover:bg-amber-100"
              >

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTurnBarExpanded(v => !v)}

            <span className="text-base shrink-0">📋</span>

                  : 'bg-white border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800'

                    key={entry.id}
                    className="py-2 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs"

                    <div className="flex items-start gap-2 min-w-0">
                      <span className="text-stone-400 font-mono shrink-0">
                        #{turnActions.length - idx}

                      <span className="text-stone-800 break-words">{entry.text}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start sm:self-auto shrink-0">
                      {idx === 0 && undoCount > 0 && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => undoLastAction()}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 border border-amber-400 text-amber-950 rounded font-bold transition"
                        >
                          ↩ Cofnij
                        </button>
                      )}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigateToTarget(entry.target)}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-900 rounded font-semibold transition"
                      >
                        {TARGET_LABELS[entry.target]} →
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}

            {/* Szybkie skróty do głównych ekranów rundy */}
            <div className="mt-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-emerald-100 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[11px] text-stone-500 font-semibold mr-1">Szybki skok:</span>
              {!state.chosenActivityThisTurn && (
                <QuickJumpButton onClick={() => navigateToTarget('activity')}>
                  ⚡ Aktywności (0/1)
                </QuickJumpButton>
